    12→---
    13→
    14→# Research: Architecture Rethink — Bugs, Branch Model, TUI, Tmux Integration, Plugin System
    15→
    16→**Date**: 2026-02-15
    17→**Researcher**: Claude
    18→**Git Commit**: 3bb1635
    19→**Branch**: main
    20→**Repository**: dual
    21→
    22→## Research Question
    23→
    24→Document the current state of Dual's architecture to understand fundamental bugs (branch model, context-aware CLI, config defaults), the tmux integration model, and how the system needs to evolve toward a TUI + plugin architecture. User's key requirements: TUI as primary interface, tmux/zellij as a plugin (not hardcoded), context-aware commands, internal branch management.
    25→
    26→## Summary
    27→
    28→Dual v2.0.0 is a Rust CLI (11 source files, ~3,600 LOC) that manages isolated dev workspaces via Docker containers + tmux sessions + shell command routing. Three fundamental bugs exist in the branch model, context detection, and config defaults. The tmux integration is tightly coupled (hardcoded in `src/tmux.rs` and `src/main.rs`). The current architecture has no plugin system, no TUI, and no context-awareness for commands. Below is a complete documentation of what exists and how each component works.
    29→
    30→---
    31→
    32→## Detailed Findings
    33→
    34→### 1. Bug: Branch Model — `dual create` Registers Branches That Cannot Be Cloned
    35→
    36→**What exists:**
    37→
    38→`cmd_create()` at `src/main.rs:159-206` takes a `repo` name and `branch` name, looks up the repo's URL from existing workspaces, and registers a new `WorkspaceEntry` with `path: None`:
    39→
    40→```rust
    41→// src/main.rs:184-190
    42→let entry = state::WorkspaceEntry {
    43→    repo: repo.to_string(),
    44→    url,                    // copied from existing workspace
    45→    branch: branch.to_string(),  // user-provided branch name
    46→    path: None,             // signals "clone on launch"
    47→};
    48→```
    49→
    50→When `cmd_launch()` runs for this workspace, it hits `src/main.rs:252`:
    51→
    52→```rust
    53→clone::clone_workspace(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.url, &entry.branch)
    54→```
    55→
    56→Which calls `src/clone.rs:46-58`:
    57→
    58→```rust
    59→let mut cmd = Command::new("git");
    60→cmd.arg("clone");
    61→if is_local_path(url) {
    62→    cmd.arg("--local");
    63→}
    64→cmd.arg("-b").arg(branch);  // <-- THIS: git clone -b <branch>
    65→cmd.arg(url).arg(&target_dir);
    66→```
    67→
    68→**The problem:** `git clone -b <branch>` requires the branch to already exist in the remote (or local source). If the user creates an internal-only branch name (e.g., `feat/new-thing` that doesn't exist in origin), the clone fails with:
    69→
    70→```
    71→fatal: Remote branch feat/new-thing not found in upstream origin
    72→```
    73→
    74→Dual's `create` command registers the workspace in state successfully (line 192-198), but the actual clone at launch time fails because `git clone -b` can only check out branches that exist in the source repository.
    75→
    76→**How it currently works — the full chain:**
    77→1. `dual create myrepo feat/new-thing` → writes `WorkspaceEntry{repo:"myrepo", url:"git@github.com:...", branch:"feat/new-thing", path:None}` to `~/.dual/workspaces.toml`
    78→2. `dual launch myrepo-feat__new-thing` → resolves entry, sees `path: None`, calls `clone_workspace()`
    79→3. `clone_workspace()` runs `git clone -b feat/new-thing <url> <dir>` → **fails** if branch doesn't exist in origin
    80→
    81→**What the user expects:** `dual create` should create a *new* branch from the current state (like `git checkout -b`), not require it to pre-exist in origin. Dual branches are internal workspace concepts.
    82→
    83→### 2. Bug: Context-Unaware CLI — Commands Don't Detect Current Repository
    84→
    85→**What exists:**
    86→
    87→The CLI at `src/cli.rs:24-30` requires explicit repo/branch args for `create`:
    88→
    89→```rust
    90→Create {
    91→    /// Repo name (must already be added)
    92→    repo: String,
    93→    /// Branch name
    94→    branch: String,
    95→},
    96→```
    97→
    98→`cmd_create()` at `src/main.rs:159` receives these as required positional args:
    99→
   100→```rust
   101→fn cmd_create(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> i32 {
   102→```
   103→
   104→There is a `detect_workspace()` function at `src/main.rs:669-688` that checks if the current directory belongs to a known workspace:
   105→
   106→```rust
   107→fn detect_workspace(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<state::WorkspaceEntry> {
   108→    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;
   109→    let root = std::process::Command::new("git")
   110→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   111→        .output()
   112→        ...
   113→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   114→        let ws_dir = st.workspace_dir(ws);
   115→        if ws_dir == root {
   116→            return Some(ws.clone());
   117→        }
   118→    }
   119→    None
   120→}
   121→```
   122→
   123→And there's `detect_git_repo()` at `src/main.rs:723-757` that gets the repo root, remote URL, and current branch from git.
   124→
   125→**How these are used today:**
   126→- `detect_workspace()` is only used by `cmd_sync()` at `src/main.rs:562` — to auto-detect which workspace you're in when no arg is given
   127→- `detect_git_repo()` is only used by `cmd_add()` at `src/main.rs:72` — to register the current directory
   128→- `cmd_create()` does **not** use either function — it requires explicit `<repo> <branch>` args
   129→
   130→**The gap:** If a user is inside `~/code/myproject` (which is already added as `myproject/main`), running `dual create feat/auth` should automatically know the repo is `myproject`. Currently it requires `dual create myproject feat/auth`.
   131→
   132→Similarly, other commands (`launch`, `destroy`, `open`, `urls`) require the full workspace identifier (`myrepo-feat__auth`). None of them auto-detect the current repo context.
   133→
   134→### 3. Bug: Default `.dual.toml` Generated by `dual add` Is Confusing
   135→
   136→**What exists:**
   137→
   138→When `dual add` is run and no `.dual.toml` exists, it creates one at `src/main.rs:101-111`:
   139→
   140→```rust
   141→let hints_path = repo_root.join(".dual.toml");
   142→if !hints_path.exists() {
   143→    let hints = config::RepoHints::default();
   144→    if let Err(e) = config::write_hints(&repo_root, &hints) {
   145→        warn!("failed to write .dual.toml: {e}");
   146→    } else {
   147→        info!("Created .dual.toml with defaults (image: node:20)");
   148→        info!("Edit it to customize ports, image, setup command, and env vars.");
   149→    }
   150→}
   151→```
   152→
   153→`RepoHints::default()` at `src/config.rs:44-54`:
   154→
   155→```rust
   156→impl Default for RepoHints {
   157→    fn default() -> Self {
   158→        Self {
   159→            image: DEFAULT_IMAGE.to_string(),  // "node:20"
   160→            ports: Vec::new(),
   161→            setup: None,
   162→            env: HashMap::new(),
   163→            shared: None,
   164→        }
   165→    }
   166→}
   167→```
   168→
   169→When serialized by `write_hints()` (using `toml::to_string_pretty`), this produces the current `.dual.toml`:
   170→
   171→```toml
   172→image = "node:20"
   173→ports = []
   174→
   175→[env]
   176→```
   177→
   178→**What's confusing:**
   179→- `ports = []` — empty array with no context about what this is for
   180→- `[env]` — empty section header with nothing in it
   181→- No comments explaining any field
   182→- `setup` is `None` so it's omitted entirely (not visible to the user)
   183→- `[shared]` is omitted (has `skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none"` at `src/config.rs:36`)
   184→- No indication of what values are valid or what the fields do
   185→
   186→The `toml` crate's `to_string_pretty()` function does not support emitting comments. The serialization produces bare TOML without any documentation.
   187→
   188→### 4. Complete Architecture — Module-by-Module Documentation
   189→
   190→#### 4.1 CLI (`src/cli.rs` — 74 lines)
   191→
   192→Uses `clap` derive macros. All commands are in the `Command` enum:
   193→
   194→| Command | CLI Signature | Required Args | Optional Args |
   195→|---------|--------------|---------------|---------------|
   196→| `Add` | `dual add` | none | `--name NAME` |
   197→| `Create` | `dual create <repo> <branch>` | repo, branch | none |
   198→| `Launch` | `dual launch <workspace>` | workspace | none |
   199→| `List` | `dual list` | none | none |
   200→| `Destroy` | `dual destroy <workspace>` | workspace | none |
   201→| `Open` | `dual open [workspace]` | none | workspace |
   202→| `Urls` | `dual urls [workspace]` | none | workspace |
   203→| `Sync` | `dual sync [workspace]` | none | workspace |
   204→| `Proxy` | `dual proxy` | none | none |
   205→| `ShellRc` | `dual shell-rc <container>` | container | none (hidden) |
   206→
   207→No subcommand = `cmd_default()` which shows workspace list.
   208→
   209→#### 4.2 Config (`src/config.rs` — 310 lines)
   210→
   211→Two responsibilities:
   212→1. **Per-repo hints** (`.dual.toml`): `RepoHints` struct with `image`, `ports`, `setup`, `env`, `shared`
   213→2. **Naming conventions**: `workspace_id()`, `container_name()`, `encode_branch()`, `decode_branch()`, `workspace_dir()`
   214→
   215→Key naming scheme:
   216→- Workspace ID: `{repo}-{encoded_branch}` (e.g., `lightfast-feat__auth`)
   217→- Container name: `dual-{repo}-{encoded_branch}` (e.g., `dual-lightfast-feat__auth`)
   218→- Branch encoding: `/` → `__` (e.g., `feat/auth` → `feat__auth`)
   219→
   220→Config fields and their implementation status:
   221→
   222→| Field | Type | Implemented | Used By |
   223→|-------|------|-------------|---------|
   224→| `image` | `String` | Yes | `container::create()` |
   225→| `ports` | `Vec<u16>` | Yes | `proxy::ProxyState`, `proxy::workspace_urls()` |
   226→| `setup` | `Option<String>` | Schema only | Nothing — never executed |
   227→| `env` | `HashMap<String,String>` | Schema only | Nothing — loaded then dropped |
   228→| `shared` | `Option<SharedConfig>` | Yes | `shared::init_from_main()`, `shared::copy_to_branch()` |
   229→
   230→#### 4.3 State (`src/state.rs` — 670 lines)
   231→
   232→Global workspace registry at `~/.dual/workspaces.toml`.
   233→
   234→`WorkspaceState` struct:
   235→- `workspace_root: Option<String>` — defaults to `~/.dual/workspaces`
   236→- `workspaces: Vec<WorkspaceEntry>` — all registered workspaces
   237→
   238→`WorkspaceEntry` struct:
   239→- `repo: String` — short name (e.g., "lightfast")
   240→- `url: String` — git remote URL or local path
   241→- `branch: String` — branch name
   242→- `path: Option<String>` — `Some` = user's existing dir (from `dual add`), `None` = managed clone
   243→
   244→State persistence uses atomic writes:
   245→1. Serialize to TOML
   246→2. Acquire advisory file lock (`fs2::FileExt`)
   247→3. Write to `.tmp` file
   248→4. Backup existing to `.toml.bak`
   249→5. Atomic rename `.tmp` → `.toml`
   250→6. Release lock
   251→
   252→#### 4.4 Clone (`src/clone.rs` — 181 lines)
   253→
   254→Handles git cloning for managed workspaces (those with `path: None`).
   255→
   256→`clone_workspace()` runs: `git clone [-b branch] [--local] <url> <target_dir>`
   257→
   258→Key behaviors:
   259→- Skip if `.git` already exists in target
   260→- `--local` flag for filesystem URLs (uses hardlinks)
   261→- `-b <branch>` for branch checkout — **this is where Bug #1 manifests**
   262→- Target dir: `{workspace_root}/{repo}/{encoded_branch}/`
   263→
   264→#### 4.5 Container (`src/container.rs` — 278 lines)
   265→
   266→Docker lifecycle management. Each workspace gets one container named `dual-{repo}-{encoded_branch}`.
   267→
   268→`create()` builds these Docker args:
   269→```
   270→docker create --name <name>
   271→  -v <workspace_dir>:/workspace
   272→  -v /workspace/node_modules    # anonymous volume for isolation
   273→  -w /workspace
   274→  <image>
   275→  sleep infinity               # keep container alive for exec
   276→```
   277→
   278→Notable gaps:
   279→- No `-e KEY=VALUE` for env vars (despite `hints.env` existing)
   280→- No `--env-file` support
   281→- No additional volume mounts (e.g., for shared config)
   282→- No port publishing (`-p`) — containers use bridge network, proxy handles routing
   283→- No `setup` command execution after creation
   284→
   285→Status checking uses `docker inspect --format {{.State.Running}}`.
   286→
   287→Container IP retrieval uses `docker inspect --format {{range .NetworkSettings.Networks}}{{.IPAddress}}{{end}}`.
   288→
   289→#### 4.6 Shell (`src/shell.rs` — 202 lines)
   290→
   291→Generates shell RC files that intercept runtime commands.
   292→
   293→Hardcoded command list at line 2-4:
   294→```rust
   295→const CONTAINER_COMMANDS: &[&str] = &[
   296→    "npm", "npx", "pnpm", "node", "python", "python3", "pip", "pip3", "curl", "make",
   297→];
   298→```
   299→
   300→For each command, generates a shell function:
   301→```bash
   302→npm() {
   303→    if [ -t 1 ]; then
   304→        command docker exec -t -w /workspace dual-lightfast-main npm "$@"
   305→    else
   306→        command docker exec -w /workspace dual-lightfast-main npm "$@"
   307→    fi
   308→}
   309→```
   310→
   311→RC files written to `~/.config/dual/rc/{container_name}.sh`.
   312→
   313→The shell RC is sourced in the tmux session via `send_keys()` after session creation.
   314→
   315→#### 4.7 Tmux (`src/tmux.rs` — 226 lines)
   316→
   317→**This is the module most relevant to the architectural rethink.**
   318→
   319→Current tmux integration:
   320→- Session naming: `dual-{repo}-{encoded_branch}` (matches container names)
   321→- `create_session()` — runs `tmux new-session -d -s <name> -c <cwd>`
   322→- `attach()` — runs `tmux attach-session -t <name>`
   323→- `detach()` — runs `tmux detach-client -s <name>`
   324→- `destroy()` — runs `tmux kill-session -t <name>`
   325→- `is_alive()` — runs `tmux has-session -t <name>`
   326→- `list_sessions()` — runs `tmux list-sessions -F #{session_name}`, filters by `dual-` prefix
   327→- `send_keys()` — runs `tmux send-keys -t <name> <keys> Enter`
   328→
   329→**How tmux is used in the launch flow** (`src/main.rs:322-337`):
   330→```rust
   331→// Step 5: Create tmux session if not alive
   332→if !tmux::is_alive(&session_name) {
   333→    let source_cmd = shell::source_file_command(&rc_path);
   334→    if let Err(e) = tmux::create_session(&session_name, &workspace_dir, Some(&source_cmd)) {
   335→        error!("tmux session creation failed: {e}");
   336→        return 1;
   337→    }
   338→}
   339→
   340→// Step 6: Attach
   341→tmux::attach(&session_name)
   342→```
   343→
   344→**Tight coupling observations:**
   345→1. `tmux` is imported and called directly in `main.rs` — no abstraction layer
   346→2. Session names are computed at call sites using `tmux::session_name()`
   347→3. `print_workspace_status()` calls `tmux::is_alive()` for each workspace
   348→4. `cmd_destroy()` calls `tmux::destroy()` directly
   349→5. No trait or interface exists that could be swapped for zellij or another multiplexer
   350→6. The session only gets one window/pane — no customization of what runs inside
   351→
   352→**What the session contains:** Just a single shell pane with the RC sourced. There is no:
   353→- Second pane for editor (nvim)
   354→- Third pane for Claude Code
   355→- Window layout configuration
   356→- Hook for post-attach commands
   357→
   358→#### 4.8 Shared Config (`src/shared.rs` — 353 lines)
   359→
   360→Manages propagation of gitignored files across workspaces.
   361→
   362→Two modes:
   363→1. **Main workspace** (`init_from_main`): moves files to `~/.dual/shared/{repo}/`, creates symlinks back
   364→2. **Branch workspace** (`copy_to_branch`): copies files from shared dir into workspace
   365→
   366→The `[shared]` section in `.dual.toml`:
   367→```toml
   368→[shared]
   369→files = [".vercel", ".env.local"]
   370→```
   371→
   372→Triggered during `cmd_launch()` at `src/main.rs:261-288` and during `cmd_sync()` at `src/main.rs:543-666`.
   373→
   374→#### 4.9 Proxy (`src/proxy.rs` — 358 lines)
   375→
   376→HTTP reverse proxy using hyper + tokio. Routes `{workspace_id}.localhost:{port}` to container IP.
   377→
   378→Builds routing table from workspace state:
   379→- For each workspace with a running container
   380→- Load `.dual.toml` to get ports
   381→- Get container IP via `docker inspect`
   382→- Map `{workspace_id}:{port}` → `{container_ip}:{port}`
   383→
   384→Listens on all configured ports on `127.0.0.1`.
   385→
   386→#### 4.10 Main (`src/main.rs` — 925 lines)
   387→
   388→Command dispatch and implementation of all CLI commands. Contains the core launch pipeline:
   389→
   390→```
   391→cmd_launch():
   392→  1. Resolve workspace from state
   393→  2. Compute container/session names
   394→  3. Resolve workspace directory (use existing path or clone)
   395→  4. Handle shared files (init or copy)
   396→  5. Ensure container exists and is running
   397→  6. Write shell RC file
   398→  7. Create tmux session (if not alive)
   399→  8. Attach to tmux session
   400→```
   401→
   402→### 5. Tmux Integration — Current User Workflow Implications
   403→
   404→**The user's current workflow (outside Dual):**
   405→1. Open terminal
   406→2. Run `tmux`
   407→3. `Meta + s` to select a tmux session (which has `nvim .` + Claude Code running)
   408→
   409→**The user's desired workflow with Dual:**
   410→1. Run `dual` (enters TUI)
   411→2. See list of repos/branches
   412→3. Select a branch → Dual's hook/plugin opens a tmux session with the workspace dir + configured panes
   413→4. Can detach from tmux directly, or `Meta + s` to switch sessions within tmux
   414→5. Can return to Dual TUI to see full workspace overview
   415→
   416→**Current architecture gaps for this workflow:**
   417→- Dual creates a bare tmux session (single pane, no editor, no Claude) — `src/tmux.rs:20-46`
   418→- No way to configure what panes/windows the tmux session should have
   419→- No way to run commands in panes (only `send_keys` to pane 0 for RC sourcing)
   420→- No hook to execute after session creation
   421→- No mechanism to integrate with the user's existing tmux (if they're already in a tmux session, `tmux attach` inside tmux is a nested session)
   422→- No zellij support at all — the word "zellij" appears nowhere in the codebase
   423→
   424→### 6. Plugin System — What Exists (Nothing)
   425→
   426→The codebase has zero extensibility infrastructure:
   427→
   428→- **No traits for behavior injection** — all functions are concrete
   429→- **No lifecycle hooks** — no callbacks at any stage
   430→- **No plugin discovery or loading** — no dynamic dispatch
   431→- **No middleware chain** — fixed pipelines
   432→- **No observer pattern** — state mutations don't emit events
   433→- **No command extension** — container commands are a hardcoded constant array
   434→- **No configuration-driven behavior** — `.dual.toml` is data-only, no conditional logic
   435→
   436→The only "extension point" is the `.dual.toml` file, which provides data but no behavior.
   437→
   438→### 7. State of Unimplemented Features
   439→
   440→| Feature | Schema/Spec | Implementation |
   441→|---------|-------------|----------------|
   442→| `setup` command | `config.rs:29` | Never executed |
   443→| `env` variables | `config.rs:33` | Loaded then dropped at `main.rs:262` |
   444→| TUI | SPEC Phase 4 | Not started |
   445→| Fuzzy picker | SPEC Phase 4 | Not started |
   446→| Meta-key switching | SPEC Phase 4 | Not started |
   447→| Auto-detection (.nvmrc, etc.) | SPEC line 163-169 | Not implemented |
   448→| Worktree support | Old SPEC | Removed (full clones chosen) |
   449→
   450→### 8. How The User's Tmux Use-Cases Map to Current Architecture
   451→
   452→| Use Case | Current Support | Gap |
   453→|----------|----------------|-----|
   454→| Open branch → get tmux session | Yes (bare session) | No pane customization |
   455→| Session has nvim + Claude Code | No | No pane/window config |
   456→| Switch between branches via tmux | Partial (sessions exist) | No switcher integration |
   457→| Run `dual` from inside tmux | Works but creates nested attach | No detection of existing tmux |
   458→| Detach and return to Dual | No | TUI doesn't exist |
   459→| Use existing tmux setup | No | Dual creates its own sessions |
   460→| Use zellij instead of tmux | No | Hardcoded tmux dependency |
   461→| Configure per-repo session layout | No | No layout config |
   462→

   491→
   492→## Architecture Documentation
   493→
   494→### Current Module Dependency Graph
   495→
   496→```
   497→main.rs
   498→├── cli.rs        (command parsing)
   499→├── config.rs     (naming, hints)
   500→├── state.rs      (workspace registry)
   501→├── clone.rs      (git clone)        ← depends on config (naming)
   502→├── container.rs  (docker lifecycle) ← depends on config (naming)
   503→├── shell.rs      (RC generation)    ← depends on config (naming, via container name)
   504→├── tmux.rs       (session lifecycle)← depends on config (naming)
   505→├── proxy.rs      (reverse proxy)    ← depends on config, container, state
   506→└── shared.rs     (config propagation)← depends on config
   507→```
   508→
   509→All modules are leaf dependencies of `main.rs`. No module depends on another module except through `config` for naming conventions.
   510→
   511→### Two Types of Workspaces
   512→
   513→| Type | Created By | `path` Field | Clone Behavior | Destroy Behavior |
   514→|------|-----------|-------------|----------------|------------------|
   515→| Main | `dual add` | `Some("/absolute/path")` | No clone needed | Does not remove directory |
   516→| Branch | `dual create` | `None` | `git clone -b` into managed dir | Removes clone directory |
   517→
   518→### Data Flow: `dual launch`
   519→
   520→```
   521→workspaces.toml → resolve_workspace(identifier)
   522→                      ↓
   523→              WorkspaceEntry{repo, url, branch, path}
   524→                      ↓
   525→              ┌───────┴────────┐
   526→              │ path: Some     │ path: None
   527→              │ use existing   │ clone_workspace()
   528→              │ directory      │    git clone -b branch url dir
   529→              └───────┬────────┘
   530→                      ↓
   531→              .dual.toml → load_hints()
   532→                      ↓
   533→              RepoHints{image, ports, setup, env, shared}
   534→                      ↓
   535→              shared files handling
   536→                      ↓
   537→              container::create(name, dir, image)
   538→                      ↓ (only image used, env/setup dropped)
   539→              container::start(name)
   540→                      ↓
   541→              shell::write_rc_file(container_name)
   542→                      ↓
   543→              tmux::create_session(name, dir, source_cmd)
   544→                      ↓
   545→              tmux::attach(name)
   546→```
   547→
   548→## Historical Context (from thoughts/)
   549→
   550→- `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-dual-tui-design.md` — TUI design research covering ratatui+crossterm stack, three TUI models (full takeover, overlay, hybrid), tree view interactions, and TUI↔tmux transition options (A: inside tmux, B: manage terminal directly, C: replace tmux)
   551→- `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-config-propagation-across-workspaces.md` — Shared config solution design. Implemented as Solution D (shared dir + symlinks)
   552→- `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-env-vars-plugin-infrastructure.md` — Documents that `env` and `setup` fields exist in schema but are never wired. No plugin infrastructure exists
   553→- `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-github-repo-setup-v2-release.md` — v2.0.0 release planning, now completed
   554→- `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-02-15-github-v2-release.md` — v2.0.0 release plan, executed
   555→
   556→## Related Research
   557→
   558→- `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-dual-tui-design.md` — TUI framework analysis and design options
   559→- `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-env-vars-plugin-infrastructure.md` — Extensibility analysis
   560→
   561→## Open Questions
   562→
   563→1. **Branch creation strategy**: Should `dual create` clone from origin then `git checkout -b`, or clone from the local main workspace (faster, uses `--local` hardlinks)?
   564→2. **Worktrees vs full clones**: The current architecture uses full clones. Git worktrees would solve the "branch doesn't exist in origin" problem (they create from local HEAD), but were rejected in SPEC for lock contention reasons. Should this be revisited?
   565→3. **TUI as default**: Should `dual` (no args) launch the TUI, with `dual list` as the non-interactive fallback?
   566→4. **Multiplexer trait**: What is the minimal trait interface for tmux/zellij abstraction? (`create_session`, `attach`, `detach`, `destroy`, `is_alive`, `send_keys`?)
   567→5. **Session layout config**: Where should per-repo tmux layouts be configured? In `.dual.toml`? In a separate file? Via plugin?
   568→6. **Plugin format**: Lua scripts? TOML declarations? Shell hooks? Rust dynamic libraries?
   569→7. **Existing tmux detection**: If the user is already in a tmux session, should Dual create a new window in that session instead of a nested attach?
   570→

 154→- `src/main.rs:101-111` — creation trigger in `cmd_add()`\n   155→\n   156→---\n   157→\n   158→## Part 2: Current Architecture — Complete Module Map\n   159→\n   160→### 2.1 Source Files Overview\n   161→\n   162→| File | Lines | Purpose |\n   163→|------|-------|---------|\n   164→| `src/lib.rs` | 9 | Public module re-exports |\n   165→| `src/cli.rs` | 74 | Clap-based CLI definitions |\n   166→| `src/main.rs` | 924 | Entry point, command dispatch, all handlers |\n   167→| `src/config.rs` | 310 | Per-repo hints (`.dual.toml`), naming conventions |\n   168→| `src/state.rs` | 669 | Global workspace state with atomic writes + file locking |\n   169→| `src/clone.rs` | 180 | Git clone management |\n   170→| `src/container.rs` | 277 | Docker container lifecycle |\n   171→| `src/shell.rs` | 201 | Shell RC generation for command routing |\n   172→| `src/tmux.rs` | 225 | Tmux session management |\n   173→| `src/proxy.rs` | 357 | HTTP reverse proxy with subdomain routing |\n   174→| `src/shared.rs` | 352 | Config propagation across workspaces |\n   175→| **Total** | **~3,578** | |\n   176→\n   177→### 2.2 Module Dependency Graph\n   178→\n   179→```\n   180→main.rs (orchestration layer)\n   181→├── cli.rs        (command parsing — leaf)\n   182→├── config.rs     (naming + hints — used by almost everything)\n   183→├── state.rs      (workspace registry — depends on config)\n   184→├── clone.rs      (git operations — depends on config)\n   185→├── container.rs  (docker lifecycle — leaf)\n   186→├── shell.rs      (RC generation — leaf)\n   187→├── tmux.rs       (session lifecycle — depends on config)\n   188→├── proxy.rs      (reverse proxy — depends on config, container, state)\n   189→└── shared.rs     (file propagation — depends on config)\n   190→```\n   191→\n   192→`config.rs` is the central hub — all modules depend on it for naming conventions.\n   193→\n   194→### 2.3 Data Flow: `dual launch`\n   195→\n   196→```\n   197→workspaces.toml → resolve_workspace(identifier)\n   198→                      ↓\n   199→              WorkspaceEntry{repo, url, branch, path}\n   200→                      ↓\n   201→              ┌───────┴────────┐\n   202→              │ path: Some     │ path: None\n   203→              │ use existing   │ clone_workspace()\n   204→              │ directory      │    git clone -b branch url dir\n   205→              └───────┬────────┘\n   206→                      ↓\n   207→              .dual.toml → load_hints()\n   208→                      ↓\n   209→              RepoHints{image, ports, setup, env, shared}\n   210→                      ↓\n   211→              shared files handling\n   212→                      ↓\n   213→              container::create(name, dir, image)\n   214→                      ↓ (only image used, env/setup dropped)\n   215→              container::start(name)\n   216→                      ↓\n   217→              shell::write_rc_file(container_name)\n   218→                      ↓\n   219→              tmux::create_session(name, dir, source_cmd)\n   220→                      ↓\n   221→              tmux::attach(name)\n   222→```\n   223→\n   224→### 2.4 Two Workspace Types\n   225→\n   226→| Type | Created By | `path` Field | Clone Behavior | Destroy Behavior |\n   227→|------|-----------|-------------|----------------|------------------|\n   228→| Main | `dual add` | `Some(\"/absolute/path\")` | No clone needed | Does NOT remove directory |\n   229→| Branch | `dual create` | `None` | `git clone -b` into managed dir | Removes clone directory |\n   230→\n   231→### 2.5 Naming Conventions\n   232→\n   233→All naming flows through `src/config.rs`:\n   234→\n   235→| Thing | Pattern | Example |\n   236→|-------|---------|---------|\n   237→| Workspace ID | `{repo}-{encoded_branch}` | `lightfast-feat__auth` |\n   238→| Container name | `dual-{repo}-{encoded_branch}` | `dual-lightfast-feat__auth` |\n   239→| Tmux session | `dual-{repo}-{encoded_branch}` | `dual-lightfast-feat__auth` |\n   240→| Directory | `{workspace_root}/{repo}/{encoded_branch}/` | `~/.dual/workspaces/lightfast/feat__auth/` |\n   241→| Branch encoding | `/` → `__` | `feat/auth` → `feat__auth` |\n   242→\n   243→Container names and tmux session names are **identical** (verified by test at `src/tmux.rs:200-212`).\n   244→\n   245→### 2.6 Unimplemented Schema Fields\n   246→\n   247→| Field | Location | Status |\n   248→|-------|----------|--------|\n   249→| `setup` | `config.rs:29` | Schema exists, never executed |\n   250→| `env` | `config.rs:33` | Loaded then dropped at `main.rs:262` — never passed to container |\n   251→| Auto-detection | SPEC lines 163-169 | `.nvmrc`, `.node-version`, etc. — not implemented |\n   252→\n   253→---\n   254→\n   255→## Part 3: Tmux Integration — Current State\n   256→\n   257→### 3.1 All Tmux Functions\n   258→\n   259→| Function | Location | Command | Purpose |\n   260→|----------|----------|---------|---------|\n   261→| `is_available()` | `tmux.rs:8-13` | `tmux -V` | Check if tmux is installed |\n   262→| `create_session()` | `tmux.rs:20-46` | `tmux new-session -d -s {name} -c {cwd}` | Create detached session |\n   263→| `attach()` | `tmux.rs:49-64` | `tmux attach-session -t {name}` | Attach terminal to session |\n   264→| `detach()` | `tmux.rs:67-69` | `tmux detach-client -s {name}` | Detach from session |\n   265→| `destroy()` | `tmux.rs:72-74` | `tmux kill-session -t {name}` | Kill session |\n   266→| `is_alive()` | `tmux.rs:77-82` | `tmux has-session -t {name}` | Check existence |\n   267→| `list_sessions()` | `tmux.rs:86-102` | `tmux list-sessions -F #{session_name}` | List dual-managed sessions |\n   268→| `send_keys()` | `tmux.rs:105-107` | `tmux send-keys -t {name} {keys} Enter` | Send command to session |\n   269→| `session_name()` | `tmux.rs:122-125` | N/A | Generate `dual-{repo}-{encoded_branch}` |\n   270→\n   271→### 3.2 Session Creation — What Gets Created\n   272→\n   273→`build_new_session_args()` at `tmux.rs:110-119` produces only:\n   274→\n   275→```\n   276→tmux new-session -d -s dual-lightfast-main -c /path/to/workspace\n   277→```\n   278→\n   279→That's it. One window, one pane, default shell, no layout. After creation, `send_keys()` sources the shell RC file. The session contains:\n   280→\n   281→- Single shell pane\n   282→- Shell RC sourced (command routing active)\n   283→- Working directory set to workspace\n   284→- **No** nvim pane\n   285→- **No** Claude Code pane\n   286→- **No** custom layout\n   287→- **No** additional windows\n   288→\n   289→### 3.3 Integration Points in main.rs\n   290→\n   291→| Location | Function | Tmux Call |\n   292→|----------|----------|-----------|\n   293→| `main.rs:233` | `cmd_launch()` | `tmux::session_name()` |\n   294→| `main.rs:323` | `cmd_launch()` | `tmux::is_alive()` |\n   295→| `main.rs:325` | `cmd_launch()` | `tmux::create_session()` |\n   296→| `main.rs:333` | `cmd_launch()` | `tmux::attach()` |\n   297→| `main.rs:381` | `cmd_destroy()` | `tmux::session_name()` |\n   298→| `main.rs:384` | `cmd_destroy()` | `tmux::is_alive()` |\n   299→| `main.rs:386` | `cmd_destroy()` | `tmux::destroy()` |\n   300→| `main.rs:696` | `print_workspace_status()` | `tmux::session_name()` |\n   301→| `main.rs:708` | `print_workspace_status()` | `tmux::is_alive()` |\n   302→\n   303→### 3.4 Tight Coupling\n   304→\n   305→1. `tmux` is imported and called directly — no trait, no abstraction\n   306→2. Session names computed at call sites using `tmux::session_name()`\n   307→3. Status display hardcodes `tmux::is_alive()` checks\n   308→4. `cmd_destroy()` calls `tmux::destroy()` directly\n   309→5. No backend swap mechanism for zellij or anything else\n   310→6. The word \"zellij\" appears nowhere in the codebase\n   311→\n   312→### 3.5 Edge Cases Not Handled\n   313→\n   314→| Scenario | What Happens | Expected |\n   315→|----------|-------------|----------|\n   316→| User already in tmux | `tmux attach` creates nested tmux | Should detect `$TMUX` env var |\n   317→| Detach from session | Must use native `Ctrl+b d` | No Dual-level detach command |\n   318→| Crash recovery | Tmux sessions survive, Dual can re-attach | Works accidentally via `is_alive()` check |\n   319→| Session layout customization | Not possible | Users want nvim + Claude + shell panes |\n   320→| Zellij user | No support | Should work via plugin/backend swap |\n   321→\n   322→---\n   323→\n   324→## Part 4: User Workflow Mapping — Tmux Setup Patterns\n   325→\n   326→### 4.1 The User's Current Workflow (Without Dual)\n   327→\n   328→```\n   329→1. Open terminal emulator (Ghostty/iTerm/etc.)\n   330→2. Run `tmux`\n   331→3. Press Meta+s to select a tmux session\n   332→4. Session has: nvim . + Claude Code already running\n   333→5. Work in that session\n   334→6. Meta+s to switch to another session\n   335→```\n   336→\n   337→**Key observations:**\n   338→- User creates tmux sessions manually (or via scripts)\n   339→- Each session is pre-configured with specific panes/windows\n   340→- Switching is via tmux's native session picker (`choose-tree` or custom binding)\n   341→- The session is the \"workspace\" — it has the tools the user needs\n   342→\n   343→### 4.2 The User's Desired Workflow (With Dual)\n   344→\n   345→```\n   346→1. Run `dual` (enters TUI)\n   347→2. See tree view: repos → branches\n   348→3. Select a branch\n   349→4. Hook/plugin opens tmux session with:\n   350→   - Correct working directory\n   351→   - Configured panes (nvim, Claude, shell, etc.)\n   352→5. Work in tmux session\n   353→6. To switch: either\n   354→   a. Detach tmux, return to Dual TUI, pick another\n   355→   b. Meta+s within tmux to switch sessions directly\n   356→7. If user runs `dual` from inside tmux: should work (show TUI, allow navigation)\n   357→```\n   358→\n   359→### 4.3 Common Tmux Setup Patterns to Support\n   360→\n   361→**Pattern A: Simple Shell**\n   362→```\n   363→┌─────────────────────────────────┐\n   364→│ $ (shell with command routing)  │\n   365→│                                 │\n   366→│                                 │\n   367→│                                 │\n   368→└─────────────────────────────────┘\n   369→```\n   370→Current Dual behavior. Single pane, shell RC sourced.\n   371→\n   372→**Pattern B: Editor + Shell**\n   373→```\n   374→┌──────────────────┬──────────────┐\n   375→│                  │              │\n   376→│  nvim .          │  $ (shell)   │\n   377→│                  │              │\n   378→│                  │              │\n   379→└──────────────────┴──────────────┘\n   380→```\n   381→Most common developer setup. Editor takes 60-70% width, shell on right.\n   382→\n   383→**Pattern C: Editor + Claude + Shell**\n   384→```\n   385→┌──────────────────┬──────────────┐\n   386→│                  │  claude      │\n   387→│  nvim .          ├──────────────┤\n   388→│                  │  $ (shell)   │\n   389→│                  │              │\n   390→└──────────────────┴──────────────┘\n   391→```\n   392→The user's described setup. Editor left, Claude top-right, shell bottom-right.\n   393→\n   394→**Pattern D: Editor + Multiple Shells**\n   395→```\n   396→┌──────────────────┬──────────────┐\n   397→│                  │  $ (shell 1) │\n   398→│  nvim .          ├──────────────┤\n   399→│                  │  $ (shell 2) │\n   400→│                  ├──────────────┤\n   401→│                  │  $ (logs)    │\n   402→└──────────────────┴──────────────┘\n   403→```\n   404→Power user setup. Multiple shells for different tasks.\n   405→\n   406→**Pattern E: Multi-Window**\n   407→```\n   408→Window 1: Editor (nvim .)\n   409→Window 2: Shell (git, CLI operations)\n   410→Window 3: Claude Code\n   411→Window 4: Logs / monitoring\n   412→```\n   413→Some users prefer windows over panes.\n   414→\n   415→**Pattern F: User's Existing Tmux Config**\n   416→```\n   417→User already has a tmux.conf with:\n   418→- Custom prefix key\n   419→- Custom pane layouts\n   420→- Plugins (tpm, tmux-resurrect, etc.)\n   421→- Status bar customization\n   422→- Key bindings for window/pane management\n   423→```\n   424→Dual must not conflict with or override the user's tmux configuration.\n   425→\n   426→### 4.4 Tmux Launch Scenarios\n   427→\n   428→**Scenario 1: User is NOT in tmux, runs `dual`**\n   429→```\n   430→Terminal → dual TUI → user selects branch → Dual creates/attaches tmux session\n   431→```\n   432→This is the clean case. Dual creates the session and attaches.\n   433→\n   434→**Scenario 2: User IS in tmux session A, runs `dual`**\n   435→```\n   436→tmux session A → dual TUI → user selects branch\n   437→  Option a: Create new tmux session B, switch to it (tmux switch-client)\n   438→  Option b: Create new tmux window in session A\n   439→  Option c: Detach from A, attach to new session B (nested tmux issue)\n   440→```\n   441→Currently Dual does option (c) which creates nested tmux — bad UX.\n   442→\n   443→**Scenario 3: User has existing tmux sessions, opens terminal, runs `dual`**\n   444→```\n   445→Terminal → dual TUI → sees branches with [running] status\n   446→  Some branches have active tmux sessions from previous work\n   447→  User selects one → Dual attaches to existing session\n   448→```\n   449→Currently works because `is_alive()` check skips creation.\n   450→\n   451→**Scenario 4: User switches between branches rapidly**\n   452→```\n   453→dual TUI → branch A → work → Meta+key → dual TUI → branch B → work → Meta+key → dual TUI\n   454→```\n   455→This requires the TUI to persist and resume quickly. Currently not possible (no TUI exists).\n   456→\n   457→**Scenario 5: User runs `dual` from a workspace directory (already in a branch)**\n   458→```\n   459→~/code/myproject (main branch) → dual → should show TUI with myproject highlighted\n   460→~/code/.dual/workspaces/myproject/feat__auth → dual → should highlight feat/auth\n   461→```\n   462→Context-aware TUI entry point.\n   463→\n   464→**Scenario 6: Multiple terminals open, each in different branches**\n   465→```\n   466→Terminal 1: tmux session → lightfast/main\n   467→Terminal 2: tmux session → lightfast/feat-auth\n   468→Terminal 3: dual TUI (overview)\n   469→```\n   470→All should coexist. TUI should show accurate real-time status.\n   471→\n   472→### 4.5 Zellij Equivalents\n   473→\n   474→For users who use zellij instead of tmux:\n   475→\n   476→| Tmux Concept | Zellij Equivalent |\n   477→|-------------|-------------------|\n   478→| Session | Session |\n   479→| Window | Tab |\n   480→| Pane | Pane |\n   481→| `tmux attach-session` | `zellij attach` |\n   482→| `tmux new-session` | `zellij --session` |\n   483→| `tmux kill-session` | `zellij kill-session` |\n   484→| `tmux has-session` | Check session existence |\n   485→| `tmux send-keys` | `zellij action write` |\n   486→| `tmux list-sessions` | `zellij list-sessions` |\n   487→\n   488→The backend trait should abstract these into a common interface.\n   489→\n   490→---\n   491→\n   492→## Part 5: TUI Design — What's Needed\n   493→\n   494→### 5.1 Current Interface\n   495→\n   496→CLI-only. No TUI library in dependencies. Output via `tracing::info!()` with Unicode status symbols:\n   497→- `●` (green) = attached\n   498→- `●` (blue) = running\n   499→- `○` (yellow) = stopped\n   500→- `◌` (dim) = lazy\n   501→\n   502→The SPEC (Phase 4, line 397) defers TUI to \"Polish\" phase.\n   503→\n   504→### 5.2 TUI Requirements (from user's description)\n   505→\n   506→1. Run `dual` → shows TUI\n   507→2. Tree view: repos at root, branches nested underneath\n   508→3. Selecting a branch opens/attaches to session\n   509→4. Must work from inside tmux (no nested issues)\n   510→5. Must coexist with user's existing tmux (`Meta+s` switching)\n   511→6. Quick return path from session back to TUI\n   512→\n   513→### 5.3 Three TUI Models (from existing research)\n   514→\n   515→**Model 1: Full Takeover**\n   516→- `dual` takes over terminal with alternate screen\n   517→- Tree view + preview pane\n   518→- Enter → exits TUI, attaches tmux session\n   519→- Meta-key → detaches tmux, returns to TUI\n   520→\n   521→**Model 2: Overlay/Popup**\n   522→- Quick picker overlay (like tmux choose-tree)\n   523→- Appears on meta-key, dismisses on selection\n   524→- No persistent dashboard\n   525→\n   526→**Model 3: Hybrid (recommended in existing research)**\n   527→- `dual` → full TUI home screen\n   528→- Select branch → transitions to tmux session\n   529→- Within tmux, meta-key → lightweight overlay picker\n   530→- Esc → returns to TUI home\n   531→\n   532→### 5.4 TUI ↔ Tmux Transition Challenge\n   533→\n   534→The critical design problem. Three options from existing research:\n   535→\n   536→**Option A: Dual Runs Inside Tmux**\n   537→```\n   538→Outer tmux session (dual-control)\n   539→├── Window 0: Dual TUI\n   540→├── Window 1: lightfast-main session\n   541→├── Window 2: lightfast-feat-auth session\n   542→└── Window 3: agent-os-main session\n   543→```\n   544→Pros: Instant switching via tmux window switch. Cons: Nested tmux if user already uses tmux.\n   545→\n   546→**Option B: Dual Manages Terminal Directly**\n   547→```\n   548→Terminal\n   549→├── State A: Dual TUI (alternate screen)\n   550→└── State B: tmux attach (tmux controls terminal)\n   551→    └── Meta-key → tmux detach → back to State A\n   552→```\n   553→Pros: Clean separation, no nested tmux. Cons: Terminal state transitions, potential flicker.\n   554→\n   555→**Option C: Dual Replaces Tmux**\n   556→```\n   557→Terminal\n   558→└── Dual TUI (full control)\n   559→    ├── Tree view\n   560→    └── Embedded PTY terminal pane\n   561→```\n   562→Pros: Complete control. Cons: Massive effort, reimplementing terminal emulation.\n   563→\n   564→### 5.5 Ratatui Stack (from existing research)\n   565→\n   566→```toml\n   567→ratatui = \"0.29\"          # TUI framework\n   568→crossterm = \"0.28\"        # Terminal backend\n   569→tui-tree-widget = \"0.22\"  # Tree view for repos/branches\n   570→tui-input = \"0.11\"        # Text input for search/filter\n   571→```\n   572→\n   573→---\n   574→\n   575→## Part 6: Plugin System — Current State (None) and Requirements\n   576→\n   577→### 6.1 Current Extensibility\n   578→\n   579→The codebase has **zero extensibility infrastructure**:\n   580→\n   581→- No traits for behavior injection\n   582→- No lifecycle hooks\n   583→- No plugin discovery or loading\n   584→- No middleware chain\n   585→- No observer pattern\n   586→- No command extension mechanism\n   587→- No configuration-driven behavior\n   588→\n   589→The only \"extension point\" is `.dual.toml` data fields.\n   590→\n   591→### 6.2 What Needs to Be Pluggable\n   592→\n   593→| Component | Why | Current State |\n   594→|-----------|-----|---------------|\n   595→| Terminal multiplexer | tmux vs zellij vs none | Hardcoded tmux in `src/tmux.rs` |\n   596→| Session layout | Per-project pane configuration | Single pane, no config |\n   597→| Container runtime | Docker vs Podman vs none | Hardcoded docker in `src/container.rs` |\n   598→| Command routing | Which commands go to container | Hardcoded list in `src/shell.rs:2-4` |\n   599→| Post-launch hooks | Run commands after workspace setup | `setup` field exists but never executes |\n   600→| Env var sources | `.env`, Vercel, 1Password | `env` field exists but never wires |\n   601→\n   602→### 6.3 The SPEC's Backend Contract\n   603→\n   604→SPEC.md lines 269-294 describe a backend interface:\n   605→\n   606→| Method | Signature |\n   607→|--------|-----------|\n   608→| `create_session` | `(workspace_id, processes[])` |\n   609→| `attach` | `(session_handle)` |\n   610→| `detach` | `(session_handle)` |\n   611→| `destroy` | `(session_handle)` |\n   612→| `is_alive` | `(session_handle) → bool` |\n   613→| `list_sessions` | `() → session_handle[]` |\n   614→\n   615→Three planned backends:\n   616→- `TmuxBackend` (default)\n   617→- `ZellijBackend` (future)\n   618→- `BasicBackend` (fallback — no multiplexer)\n   619→\n   620→None of these are implemented as traits. The tmux module is a flat collection of functions.\n   621→\n   622→### 6.4 Shell Command List\n   623→\n   624→The container command list at `src/shell.rs:2-4` is hardcoded:\n   625→\n   626→```rust\n   627→const CONTAINER_COMMANDS: &[&str] = &[\n   628→    \"npm\", \"npx\", \"pnpm\", \"node\", \"python\", \"python3\", \"pip\", \"pip3\", \"curl\", \"make\",\n   629→];\n   630→```\n   631→\n   632→No mechanism to add project-specific commands. A Go project would need `go`, a Rust project would need `cargo`, etc.\n   633→\n   634→---\n   635→\n   636→## Part 7: State and Config Systems\n   637→\n   638→### 7.1 Global State (`~/.dual/workspaces.toml`)\n   639→\n   640→```rust\n   641→// src/state.rs:14-39\n   642→pub struct WorkspaceState {\n   643→    pub workspace_root: Option<String>,  // Default: ~/.dual/workspaces\n   644→    pub workspaces: Vec<WorkspaceEntry>,\n   645→}\n   646→\n   647→pub struct WorkspaceEntry {\n   648→    pub repo: String,           // \"lightfast\"\n   649→    pub url: String,            // git URL or local path\n   650→    pub branch: String,         // \"feat/auth\"\n   651→    pub path: Option<String>,   // Some = user dir, None = managed clone\n   652→}\n   653→```\n   654→\n   655→Atomic writes with advisory file locking (`fs2::FileExt`).\n   656→\n   657→No concept of:\n   658→- Branch parent (which branch was this created from?)\n   659→- Branch type (local vs remote)\n   660→- Workspace status (only computed dynamically from docker/tmux)\n   661→- Creation timestamp\n   662→- Last accessed timestamp\n   663→\n   664→### 7.2 Per-Repo Hints (`.dual.toml`)\n   665→\n   666→```rust\n   667→// src/config.rs:18-38\n   668→pub struct RepoHints {\n   669→    pub image: String,                    // Docker image\n   670→    pub ports: Vec<u16>,                  // Exposed ports\n   671→    pub setup: Option<String>,            // Setup command (UNIMPLEMENTED)\n   672→    pub env: HashMap<String, String>,     // Env vars (UNIMPLEMENTED)\n   673→    pub shared: Option<SharedConfig>,     // Shared file config\n   674→}\n   675→```\n   676→\n   677→Written to workspace root as `.dual.toml`. Committed to git.\n   678→\n   679→### 7.3 Shared Config (`~/.dual/shared/{repo}/`)\n   680→\n   681→Managed by `src/shared.rs`. ## My Understanding

You're at **v2.0.0** but the Linear project is still called "Dual v0" with outdated milestones. You want to:

1. **Clean up Linear** to reflect reality (v2.0.0 is shipped, many "In Progress" issues are actually partially done)
2. **Fix v2.x bugs** before any architectural rewrite
3. **Incrementally add features** in v2.1, v2.2, etc.
4. **Clearly separate** what's a v2.x fix vs what requires v3 architecture
5. **Remove dead weight** from the current codebase and project

### What I Found in Linear

**Project "Dual v0"** is stale:
- Still named "v0", status "Planned"
- Phase 0 milestone shows 100% but Phase 1 shows ~15%
- Several "In Progress" issues are actually partially shipped in v2.0.0 (DUAL-19 command routing, DUAL-20 proxy, DUAL-18 container lifecycle — all have working code)
- 5 issues in "Duplicate" status from old spike restructuring

**Issues that need status updates:**
| Issue | Current Status | Reality |
|-------|---------------|---------|
| DUAL-19 (command routing) | In Progress | Shell RC routing is shipped in v2.0.0 |
| DUAL-20 (multi-port proxy) | In Progress | Proxy is shipped in v2.0.0 |
| DUAL-18 (container lifecycle) | In Progress | Container module is shipped in v2.0.0 |
| DUAL-23 (lifecycle orchestration) | In Progress | `cmd_launch()` pipeline works in v2.0.0 |
| DUAL-27 (e2e test) | In Progress | E2E tests exist in `tests/e2e.rs` |
| DUAL-22 (port config) | Backlog | Basic port config shipped in v2.0.0 |

### What's Actually Broken (v2.x fixes)

1. **Bug 1 — Branch model**: `git clone -b` fails for non-origin branches
2. **Bug 2 — Context detection**: Commands require explicit repo/branch when context is available
3. **Bug 3 — Config defaults**: `.dual.toml` is confusing with empty `ports = []` and `[env]`
4. **Unimplemented fields**: `env` vars loaded then dropped, `setup` command never executed
5. **Hardcoded commands**: `CONTAINER_COMMANDS` list can't be extended per-project
6. **Nested tmux**: No `$TMUX` detection, creates nested sessions
7. **Node-specific**: Anonymous `node_modules` volume hardcoded in container creation

### What Requires v3 Architecture

1. Multiplexer trait (tmux/zellij/none)
2. TUI (ratatui — requires new dependencies, new module, completely different interaction model)
3. Plugin system (lifecycle hooks, behavior injection)
4. Session layout config (panes/windows per workspace)
5. Container runtime abstraction (Docker/Podman trait)

### What Should Be Removed

1. The hardcoded `node_modules` anonymous volume in `container.rs` (too Node-specific)
2. Stale Linear issues (duplicates, old spike tests)
3. The "Dual v0" project name

---

## Proposed Plan Structure

Before I write the full plan, does this phasing make sense?

**Phase 0: Linear Cleanup** — Update project, close shipped issues, create new milestones

**v2.1.0 — Bug Fixes & Completions** (fix what exists)
1. Fix branch model (clone then `git checkout -b`)
2. Fix `.dual.toml` defaults (hand-write commented template)
3. Wire `env` vars to container creation
4. Wire `setup` command execution
5. Make container commands configurable via `.dual.toml`
6. Remove hardcoded `node_modules` volume

**v2.2.0 — Context Awareness & UX** (improve CLI ergonomics)
1. Context-aware `dual create <branch>` (auto-detect repo)
2. Context-aware `launch`, `destroy`, `open`, `urls`
3. Detect `$TMUX` and use `switch-client` instead of nested attach
4. Improve `dual list` output

**v3.0.0 — Architecture Rewrite** (new systems)
1. Multiplexer trait + TmuxBackend
2. TUI with ratatui
3. Session layout configuration
4. ZellijBackend / BasicBackend
5. Plugin system

Find local main workspace path for this repo\n//   2. git clone --local <main_workspace_path> <target_dir>\n//   3. cd <target_dir> && git checkout -b <branch>\n```\n\nIn `src/main.rs`, update `cmd_create()`:\n- Look up the main workspace (the one with `path: Some(...)`) for the given repo\n- Pass its path to a new `clone_from_local()` function\n- If no main workspace exists, fall back to `git clone <url>` then `git checkout -b`\n\nNew function signature in `clone.rs`:\n```rust\npub fn clone_from_local(\n    main_workspace_path: &Path,\n    target_dir: &Path,\n    new_branch: &str,\n) -> Result<(), CloneError>\n```\n\n**Tests to add**:\n- `test_clone_from_local_args` — verify git clone --local + git checkout -b args\n- `test_clone_fallback_to_remote` — verify fallback when no local main exists\n- Update existing `test_clone_workspace_args` — adjust for new behavior\n\n#### 2. Fix Default `.dual.toml` (Bug 3)\n**File**: `src/config.rs`\n\n**Current behavior**: `toml::to_string_pretty()` generates bare TOML without comments.\n\n**New behavior**: Hand-write a commented template string instead of serializing.\n\n**Implementation**:\n\nReplace `write_hints()` to write a hand-crafted template:\n```rust\npub fn write_default_hints(repo_root: &Path) -> Result<(), ConfigError> {\n    let template = r#\"# Dual workspace configuration\n# See: https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual\n\n# Docker image for the container runtime\nimage = \"node:20\"\n\n# Ports your dev server uses (for reverse proxy routing)\n# Example: ports = [3000, 3001]\n# ports = []\n\n# Shell command to run after container creation (e.g., dependency install)\n# Example: setup = \"pnpm install\"\n# setup = \"\"\n\n# Environment variables passed to the container\n# Example:\n# [env]\n# NODE_ENV = \"development\"\n\n# Commands to route to the container (in addition to defaults)\n# Default: npm, npx, pnpm, node, python, python3, pip, pip3, curl, make\n# Example: extra_commands = [\"cargo\", \"go\", \"ruby\"]\n# extra_commands = []\n\n# Directories to isolate with anonymous Docker volumes\n# These directories get their own volume so they don't sync between host/container\n# Example: anonymous_volumes = [\"node_modules\", \".next\", \"target\"]\n# anonymous_volumes = [\"node_modules\"]\n\n# Files to share across all workspaces of this repo\n# These are gitignored files that should be available in every branch workspace\n# [shared]\n# files = [\".env.local\", \".vercel\"]\n\"#;\n    let hints_path = repo_root.join(\".dual.toml\");\n    std::fs::write(&hints_path, template)?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n```\n\nUpdate `cmd_add()` in `src/main.rs:101-111` to call `write_default_hints()` instead of `write_hints(&RepoHints::default())`.\n\nKeep `write_hints()` for programmatic updates — it serializes actual `RepoHints` structs.\n\n#### 3. Wire `env` Vars to Container Creation\n**Files**: `src/container.rs`, `src/main.rs`\n\n**Current behavior**: `env` HashMap loaded at `main.rs:262` then dropped. `container::create()` takes only `(name, dir, image)`.\n\n**Implementation**:\n\nUpdate `container::create()` signature:\n```rust\npub fn create(\n    name: &str,\n    workspace_dir: &Path,\n    image: &str,\n    env: &HashMap<String, String>,\n    anonymous_volumes: &[String],\n) -> Result<(), ContainerError>\n```\n\nIn `build_create_args()`, add env vars:\n```rust\nfor (key, value) in env {\n    args.push(\"-e\".to_string());\n    args.push(format!(\"{key}={value}\"));\n}\n```\n\nReplace hardcoded `node_modules` volume with configurable `anonymous_volumes`:\n```rust\nfor vol in anonymous_volumes {\n    args.push(\"-v\".to_string());\n    args.push(format!(\"/workspace/{vol}\"));\n}\n```\n\nUpdate `cmd_launch()` in `main.rs` to pass `hints.env` and `hints.anonymous_volumes` to `container::create()`.\n\n#### 4. Wire `setup` Command Execution\n**Files**: `src/container.rs`, `src/main.rs`\n\n**Current behavior**: `setup` field exists in `RepoHints` but is never executed.\n\n**Implementation**:\n\nAdd to `container.rs`:\n```rust\npub fn exec_setup(name: &str, setup_cmd: &str) -> Result<(), ContainerError> {\n    // docker exec <name> sh -c \"<setup_cmd>\"\n}\n```\n\nIn `cmd_launch()`, after `container::start()` and before tmux session creation:\n```rust\nif let Some(setup) = &hints.setup {\n    info!(\"Running setup: {setup}\");\n    container::exec_setup(&container_name, setup)?;\n}\n```\n\n#### 5. Configurable Container Commands\n**Files**: `src/config.rs`, `src/shell.rs`, `src/main.rs`\n\n**Current behavior**: `CONTAINER_COMMANDS` at `shell.rs:2-4` is a hardcoded `&[&str]`.\n\n**Implementation**:\n\nAdd to `RepoHints` in `config.rs`:\n```rust\n#[serde(default)]\npub extra_commands: Vec<String>,\n\n#[serde(default)]\npub anonymous_volumes: Vec<String>,\n```\n\nUpdate `Default` impl:\n```rust\nextra_commands: Vec::new(),\nanonymous_volumes: vec![\"node_modules\".to_string()],\n```\n\nUpdate `shell.rs` to accept additional commands:\n```rust\npub fn write_rc_file(\n    container_name: &str,\n    extra_commands: &[String],\n) -> Result<PathBuf, ShellError>\n```\n\nMerge `CONTAINER_COMMANDS` with `extra_commands` when generating RC.\n\n#### 6. Update `cmd_launch()` Pipeline\n**File**: `src/main.rs`\n\nWire all the new parameters through the launch pipeline:\n```\nload hints → pass hints.env + hints.anonymous_volumes to container::create()\n           → pass hints.setup to container::exec_setup()\n           → pass hints.extra_commands to shell::write_rc_file()\n```\n\n### Success Criteria:\n\n#### Automated Verification:\n- [ ] `cargo test` — all existing tests pass\n- [ ] `cargo clippy` — no warnings\n- [ ] `cargo fmt --check` — properly formatted\n- [ ] New tests for `clone_from_local()` pass\n- [ ] New tests for `write_default_hints()` pass\n- [ ] New tests for env var args in container create pass\n- [ ] New tests for extra_commands in shell RC generation pass\n- [ ] New tests for anonymous_volumes in container create pass\n\n#### Manual Verification:\n- [ ] `dual create myrepo feat/new-thing` creates a workspace that clones from local main and creates a new branch\n- [ ] `dual add` in a new repo creates a well-commented `.dual.toml`\n- [ ] Setting `env` vars in `.dual.toml` → they appear in `docker exec env` inside the container\n- [ ] Setting `setup = \"pnpm install\"` → runs on first launch\n- [ ] Setting `extra_commands = [\"cargo\"]` → `cargo build` routes to container\n- [ ] Setting `anonymous_volumes = [\"node_modules\", \".next\"]` → both get anonymous volumes\n\n**Implementation Note**: After completing this phase and all automated verification passes, pause here for manual confirmation from the human that the manual testing was successful before proceeding to the next phase.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2: v2.2.0 — Context Awareness & UX\n\n### Overview\nMake the CLI context-aware so commands auto-detect the current repo/workspace from the working directory. Fix tmux nested session handling.\n\n### Changes Required:\n\n#### 1. Context-Aware `dual create`\n**Files**: `src/cli.rs`, `src/main.rs`\n\nMake `repo` optional in `Create` command:\n```rust\nCreate {\n    /// Branch name\n    branch: String,\n    /// Repo name (auto-detected if omitted)\n    #[arg(long)]\n    repo: Option<String>,\n},\n```\n\nIn `cmd_create()`, if `repo` is `None`:\n1. Call `detect_git_repo()` to get the current repo info\n2. Look up the repo name from state by matching the git remote URL\n3. If found, use it. If not, error with a helpful message.\n\nThis means: `dual create feat/auth` works when inside a repo directory.\n\n#### 2. Context-Aware `launch`, `destroy`, `open`, `urls`\n**Files**: `src/cli.rs`, `src/main.rs`\n\nMake the workspace arg optional for these commands:\n```rust\nLaunch {\n    /// Workspace identifier (auto-detected if omitted)\n    workspace: Option<String>,\n},\nDestroy {\n    workspace: Option<String>,\n},\n```\n\nWhen workspace is `None`, call `detect_workspace()` to find the current workspace.\n\n#### 3. Tmux Nested Session Detection\n**File**: `src/tmux.rs`\n\nAdd detection in `attach()`:\n```rust\npub fn attach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {\n    if std::env::var(\"TMUX\").is_ok() {\n        // Already in tmux — use switch-client instead of attach\n        // tmux switch-client -t <session_name>\n    } else {\n        // Not in tmux — use attach-session\n        // tmux attach-session -t <session_name>\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### 4. Improve `dual list` Output\n**File**: `src/main.rs`\n\nGroup workspaces by repo in the list output:\n```\nlightfast\n  main          ● running    (container: up, tmux: attached)\n  feat/auth     ○ stopped    (container: stopped, tmux: none)\n  feat/billing  ◌ lazy       (not cloned yet)\n\nagent-os\n  main          ● running    (container: up, tmux: background)\n```\n\n### Success Criteria:\n\n#### Automated Verification:\n- [ ] `cargo test` — all tests pass\n- [ ] `cargo clippy` — no warnings\n- [ ] New tests for context detection in create pass\n- [ ] New tests for optional workspace args pass\n- [ ] New tests for `$TMUX` detection pass\n- [ ] New tests for grouped list output pass\n\n#### Manual Verification:\n- [ ] `cd ~/code/myproject && dual create feat/auth` auto-detects repo\n- [ ] `cd ~/code/myproject && dual launch` auto-detects workspace\n- [ ] Running `dual launch` from inside tmux uses `switch-client` (no nesting)\n- [ ] `dual list` shows grouped output by repo\n\n**Implementation Note**: After completing this phase and all automated verification passes, pause here for manual confirmation from the human that the manual testing was successful before proceeding to the next phase.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: v3.0.0 — Architecture Rewrite (Scoping Only)\n\n### Overview\nThis phase is scoped but NOT detailed for implementation yet. It defines what v3 contains so the v2.x work doesn't accidentally paint us into a corner.\n\n### 3.1 Multiplexer Trait\n\nExtract `src/tmux.rs` functions into a trait:\n```rust\npub trait MultiplexerBackend {\n    fn create_session(&self, name: &str, cwd: &Path, init_cmd: Option<&str>) -> Result<()>;\n    fn attach(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;\n    fn detach(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;\n    fn destroy(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;\n    fn is_alive(&self, name: &str) -> bool;\n    fn list_sessions(&self) -> Vec<String>;\n    fn send_keys(&self, name: &str, keys: &str) -> Result<()>;\n    fn session_name(&self, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String;\n}\n```\n\nImplement `TmuxBackend`, stub `ZellijBackend`, add `BasicBackend` (no multiplexer).\n\n### 3.2 TUI\n\nAdd ratatui + crossterm dependencies. New `src/tui/` module:\n- `mod.rs` — TUI app state and event loop\n- `tree.rs` — repo/branch tree view\n- `status.rs` — workspace status panel\n\n`dual` (no args) launches TUI. `dual list` becomes the non-interactive fallback.\n\n### 3.3 Session Layout Configuration\n\nAdd to `.dual.toml`:\n```toml\n[layout]\ntype = \"editor-claude-shell\"  # or custom\n\n[[layout.panes]]\ncommand = \"nvim .\"\nsize = \"60%\"\n\n[[layout.panes]]\ncommand = \"claude\"\nsize = \"20%\"\n\n[[layout.panes]]\ncommand = \"\"  # shell\nsize = \"20%\"\n```\n\n### 3.4 Plugin System (Future)\n\nDesign TBD. Likely TOML-declared hooks + shell commands:\n```toml\n[hooks]\npost_launch = \"pnpm install && pnpm dev\"\npre_destroy = \"pnpm build\"\n```\n\n### What v3 Requires That v2.x Must NOT Break:\n- `config.rs` naming conventions must remain stable\n- `WorkspaceEntry` struct can add fields but not change existing ones\n- `container.rs` function signatures can evolve (already changing in v2.1)\n- `.dual.toml` format must remain backward-compatible (new fields with defaults)\n\n---\n\n## Linear Issue Plan\n\n### Issues to Create for v2.1.0 Milestone:\n\n| Title | Priority | Labels | Description |\n|-------|----------|--------|-------------|\n| Fix branch model: clone from local main + git checkout -b | Urgent | Core | Bug 1 fix. Change `clone.rs` to clone from local main workspace using `--local`, then `git checkout -b` for new branches |\n| Fix default .dual.toml: hand-write commented template | High | Core, DX | Bug 3 fix. Replace `toml::to_string_pretty()` with hand-crafted commented template in `write_default_hints()` |\n| Wire env vars to container creation | High | Docker, Core | Pass `hints.env` HashMap as `-e KEY=VALUE` args to `docker create` |\n| Wire setup command execution | High | Docker, Core | Execute `hints.setup` via `docker exec` after container start |\n| Configurable container commands via .dual.toml | High | Core, DX | Add `extra_commands` field to RepoHints, merge with default CONTAINER_COMMANDS |\n| Configurable anonymous volumes via .dual.toml | High | Docker, Core | Replace hardcoded `node_modules` volume with `anonymous_volumes` field |\n\n### Issues to Create for v2.2.0 Milestone:\n\n| Title | Priority | Labels | Description |\n|-------|----------|--------|-------------|\n| Context-aware `dual create`: auto-detect repo from cwd | High | Core, DX | Bug 2 fix. Make repo arg optional, use `detect_git_repo()` when omitted |\n| Context-aware launch/destroy/open/urls | High | Core, DX | Make workspace arg optional, use `detect_workspace()` when omitted |\n| Detect $TMUX and use switch-client | High | Tmux, DX | Check `$TMUX` env var in `attach()`, use `switch-client` instead of nested attach |\n| Improve `dual list` with grouped repo output | Medium | DX | Group workspaces by repo, show container + tmux status per workspace |\n\n### Issues to Create for v3.0.0 Milestone:\n\n| Title | Priority | Labels | Description |\n|-------|----------|--------|-------------|\n| Multiplexer trait: extract tmux behind backend interface | High | Core, Tmux | Create `MultiplexerBackend` trait, implement `TmuxBackend` |\n| TUI: ratatui-based workspace browser | High | Core, DX | Add ratatui/crossterm deps, implement tree view, `dual` → TUI |\n| Session layout configuration | Medium | Core, Tmux | Add `[layout]` section to `.dual.toml` for pane/window config |\n| ZellijBackend stub | Medium | Core | Implement `MultiplexerBackend` for zellij |\n| BasicBackend: no-multiplexer fallback | Low | Core | Implement `MultiplexerBackend` that just runs in current terminal |\n\n---\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n### Unit Tests:\n- Clone module: test local clone args, fallback behavior, checkout -b construction\n- Config module: test template generation, extra_commands/anonymous_volumes parsing\n- Container module: test env var args, anonymous volume args, setup exec args\n- Shell module: test merged command lists (defaults + extras)\n- Tmux module: test `$TMUX` detection, switch-client vs attach args\n- CLI module: test optional args parsing\n\n### Integration Tests:\n- Full launch pipeline with env vars → verify env inside container\n- Full launch pipeline with setup command → verify setup ran\n- Clone from local → verify new branch exists\n- Context detection → verify auto-detect from cwd\n\n### Manual Testing Steps:\n1. Create a workspace with `dual create myrepo feat/new-thing` where `feat/new-thing` doesn't exist at origin\n2. Verify `.dual.toml` has helpful comments when created by `dual add`\n3. Add env vars to `.dual.toml`, launch, verify inside container\n4. Set `setup = \"echo hello > /tmp/setup-ran\"`, launch, verify file exists\n5. Run `dual create feat/test` from inside a repo directory (no repo arg)\n6. Run `dual launch` from inside tmux — verify no nesting\n\n## Performance Considerations\n\n- Clone from local with `--local` uses hardlinks — significantly faster than remote clone\n- `detect_workspace()` and `detect_git_repo()` run git commands — acceptable for interactive CLI, but TUI (v3) should cache results\n- Anonymous volumes list is typically small (1-3 items) — no performance concern\n\n## Migration Notes\n\n### v2.0.0 → v2.1.0\n- **`.dual.toml` backward compatible**: New fields (`extra_commands`, `anonymous_volumes`) have defaults via `#[serde(default)]`\n- **Existing workspaces**: Already-cloned workspaces are unaffected. Only new `dual create` uses the new clone strategy\n- **Existing containers**: Must be recreated to pick up env vars/anonymous volumes. `dual destroy` + `dual launch` handles this\n\n### v2.1.0 → v2.2.0\n- **CLI breaking change**: `dual create` positional args change from `<repo> <branch>` to `<branch> [--repo REPO]`. This is a minor breaking change — document in changelog\n- **Optional workspace args**: Previously required args become optional. Fully backward compatible (explicit args still work)\n\n### v2.2.0 → v3.0.0\n- **TUI as default**: `dual` (no args) changes from showing list to launching TUI. `dual list` remains as non-interactive fallback\n- **Multiplexer trait**: Internal refactor, no user-facing change unless using zellij\n\n## References\n\n- Research: `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-v3-architecture-rethink.md`\n- Earlier research: `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-architecture-rethink-bugs-tui-plugins.md`\n- TUI design: `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-dual-tui-design.md`\n- SPEC.md: Backend trait contract (lines 269-294), TUI (lines 298-320, 397)\n- Linear project: https://linear.app/jps0000-dual/project/dual-v0-7f8871a38577\n"
}

> AGENT

Per the roadmap, v2.1.0 and v2.2.0 are complete. Next up is **v3.0.0 — Architecture Rewrite**.

The plan only scoped v3 at a high level. The five items are:

1. **Multiplexer trait** — Extract `src/tmux.rs` behind a `MultiplexerBackend` trait, implement `TmuxBackend`
2. **TUI** — ratatui-based workspace browser (`dual` → TUI)
3. **Session layout configuration** — `[layout]` section in `.dual.toml` for pane/window config
4. **ZellijBackend / BasicBackend** — Additional multiplexer implementations
5. **Plugin system** — Lifecycle hooks

These have dependencies — the multiplexer trait is the foundation for everything else (TUI needs it to launch sessions, layouts need it for pane management, zellij needs the trait to exist).

     1→---
     2→date_created: 2026-02-05
     3→author: Human
     4→status: validated
