     1→use std::path::PathBuf;
     2→
     3→use clap::Parser;
     4→use dual::cli::{Cli, Command};
     5→use dual::clone;
     6→use dual::config;
     7→use dual::container;
     8→use dual::proxy;
     9→use dual::shared;
    10→use dual::shell;
    11→use dual::state;
    12→use dual::tmux;
    13→use tracing::{debug, error, info, warn};
    14→
    15→fn main() {
    16→    // Initialize tracing with DUAL_LOG env var (default: info)
    17→    tracing_subscriber::fmt()
    18→        .with_env_filter(
    19→            tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::try_from_env("DUAL_LOG")
    20→                .unwrap_or_else(|_| tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::new("info")),
    21→        )
    22→        .without_time()
    23→        .with_target(false)
    24→        .init();
    25→
    26→    let cli = Cli::parse();
    27→
    28→    let exit_code = match cli.command {
    29→        None => cmd_default(),
    30→        Some(Command::Add { name }) => cmd_add(name.as_deref()),
    31→        Some(Command::Create { branch, repo }) => cmd_create(repo.as_deref(), &branch),
    32→        Some(Command::Launch { workspace }) => cmd_launch(workspace.as_deref()),
    33→        Some(Command::List) => cmd_list(),
    34→        Some(Command::Destroy { workspace }) => cmd_destroy(workspace.as_deref()),
    35→        Some(Command::Open { workspace }) => cmd_open(workspace),
    36→        Some(Command::Urls { workspace }) => cmd_urls(workspace),
    37→        Some(Command::Sync { workspace }) => cmd_sync(workspace),
    38→        Some(Command::Proxy) => cmd_proxy(),
    39→        Some(Command::ShellRc { container }) => cmd_shell_rc(&container),
    40→    };
    41→
    42→    std::process::exit(exit_code);
    43→}
    44→
    45→/// Default (no subcommand): show workspace list with launch hint.
    46→fn cmd_default() -> i32 {
    47→    let st = match state::load() {
    48→        Ok(s) => s,
    49→        Err(e) => {
    50→            error!("{e}");
    51→            info!("Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.");
    52→            return 1;
    53→        }
    54→    };
    55→
    56→    let workspaces = st.all_workspaces();
    57→    if workspaces.is_empty() {
    58→        info!("No workspaces. Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.");
    59→        return 0;
    60→    }
    61→
    62→    println!("Workspaces:\n");
    63→    print_workspace_status(&st);
    64→    println!("Use `dual launch <workspace>` to start a workspace.");
    65→    println!("Use `dual add` to register a new repo.");
    66→    0
    67→}
    68→
    69→/// Register the current repo as a dual workspace.
    70→fn cmd_add(name: Option<&str>) -> i32 {
    71→    // Detect git repo info from current directory
    72→    let (repo_root, url, branch) = match detect_git_repo() {
    73→        Ok(info) => info,
    74→        Err(e) => {
    75→            error!("{e}");
    76→            return 1;
    77→        }
    78→    };
    79→
    80→    // Derive repo name
    81→    let repo_name = match name {
    82→        Some(n) => n.to_string(),
    83→        None => derive_repo_name(&repo_root),
    84→    };
    85→
    86→    // Load or create state
    87→    let mut st = match state::load() {
    88→        Ok(s) => s,
    89→        Err(e) => {
    90→            error!("{e}");
    91→            return 1;
    92→        }
    93→    };
    94→
    95→    // Check for duplicates
    96→    if st.has_workspace(&repo_name, &branch) {
    97→        error!("workspace {}/{} already exists", repo_name, branch);
    98→        return 1;
    99→    }
   100→
   101→    // Check for .dual.toml — if missing, create a default one with helpful comments
   102→    let hints_path = repo_root.join(".dual.toml");
   103→    if !hints_path.exists() {
   104→        if let Err(e) = config::write_default_hints(&repo_root) {
   105→            warn!("failed to write .dual.toml: {e}");
   106→        } else {
   107→            info!("Created .dual.toml with defaults (image: node:20)");
   108→            info!("Edit it to customize ports, image, setup command, and env vars.");
   109→        }
   110→    }
   111→
   112→    // Initialize shared directory if [shared] is configured
   113→    let hints = config::load_hints(&repo_root).unwrap_or_default();
   114→    if let Some(ref shared_config) = hints.shared
   115→        && !shared_config.files.is_empty()
   116→    {
   117→        match shared::ensure_shared_dir(&repo_name) {
   118→            Ok(shared_dir) => {
   119→                match shared::init_from_main(&repo_root, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   120→                    Ok(moved) => {
   121→                        for f in &moved {
   122→                            info!("  shared: {f} → ~/.dual/shared/{repo_name}/");
   123→                        }
   124→                    }
   125→                    Err(e) => warn!("shared init failed: {e}"),
   126→                }
   127→            }
   128→            Err(e) => warn!("could not create shared directory: {e}"),
   129→        }
   130→    }
   131→
   132→    // Add workspace entry
   133→    let entry = state::WorkspaceEntry {
   134→        repo: repo_name.clone(),
   135→        url,
   136→        branch: branch.clone(),
   137→        path: Some(repo_root.to_string_lossy().to_string()),
   138→    };
   139→
   140→    if let Err(e) = st.add_workspace(entry) {
   141→        error!("{e}");
   142→        return 1;
   143→    }
   144→
   145→    // Save state
   146→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   147→        error!("failed to save state: {e}");
   148→        return 1;
   149→    }
   150→
   151→    let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&repo_name, &branch);
   152→    info!("Added workspace: {ws_id}");
   153→    info!("Use `dual launch {ws_id}` to start.");
   154→    0
   155→}
   156→
   157→/// Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo.
   158→fn cmd_create(repo_arg: Option<&str>, branch: &str) -> i32 {
   159→    let mut st = match state::load() {
   160→        Ok(s) => s,
   161→        Err(e) => {
   162→            error!("{e}");
   163→            return 1;
   164→        }
   165→    };
   166→
   167→    // Resolve repo name: use --repo if provided, otherwise auto-detect from cwd
   168→    let repo = match repo_arg {
   169→        Some(r) => r.to_string(),
   170→        None => match detect_repo_from_cwd(&st) {
   171→            Some(r) => {
   172→                info!("Auto-detected repo: {r}");
   173→                r
   174→            }
   175→            None => {
   176→                error!("could not detect repo from current directory");
   177→                info!("Usage: dual create <branch> --repo <name>");
   178→                info!("Or run from inside a repo that was added with `dual add`.");
   179→                return 1;
   180→            }
   181→        },
   182→    };
   183→
   184→    // Find an existing workspace for this repo
   185→    let existing = st.workspaces_for_repo(&repo);
   186→    if existing.is_empty() {
   187→        error!("repo '{repo}' not found. Run `dual add` inside the repo first.");
   188→        return 1;
   189→    }
   190→
   191→    // Check if this branch already exists
   192→    if st.has_workspace(&repo, branch) {
   193→        error!("workspace {repo}/{branch} already exists");
   194→        return 1;
   195→    }
   196→
   197→    // Get URL from existing entry
   198→    let url = existing[0].url.clone();
   199→
   200→    // Add new entry (no explicit path — will be cloned on launch)
   201→    let entry = state::WorkspaceEntry {
   202→        repo: repo.clone(),
   203→        url,
   204→        branch: branch.to_string(),
   205→        path: None,
   206→    };
   207→
   208→    if let Err(e) = st.add_workspace(entry) {
   209→        error!("{e}");
   210→        return 1;
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   214→        error!("failed to save state: {e}");
   215→        return 1;
   216→    }
   217→
   218→    let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&repo, branch);
   219→    info!("Created workspace: {ws_id}");
   220→    info!("Use `dual launch {ws_id}` to start.");
   221→    0
   222→}
   223→
   224→/// Launch a specific workspace: clone → container → shell RC → tmux → attach.
   225→fn cmd_launch(workspace_arg: Option<&str>) -> i32 {
   226→    let st = match state::load() {
   227→        Ok(s) => s,
   228→        Err(e) => {
   229→            error!("{e}");
   230→            return 1;
   231→        }
   232→    };
   233→
   234→    // Resolve workspace: use arg if provided, otherwise auto-detect from cwd
   235→    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {
   236→        match st.resolve_workspace(ws) {
   237→            Some(e) => e,
   238→            None => {
   239→                error!("unknown workspace '{ws}'");
   240→                info!("Configured workspaces:");
   241→                for w in st.all_workspaces() {
   242→                    let id = config::workspace_id(&w.repo, &w.branch);
   243→                    info!("  {id}");
   244→                }
   245→                return 1;
   246→            }
   247→        }
   248→    } else {
   249→        match detect_workspace(&st) {
   250→            Some(e) => {
   251→                let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&e.repo, &e.branch);
   252→                info!("Auto-detected workspace: {ws_id}");
   253→                st.resolve_workspace(&ws_id).unwrap()
   254→            }
   255→            None => {
   256→                error!("could not detect workspace from current directory");
   257→                info!("Usage: dual launch [workspace]");
   258→                return 1;
   259→            }
   260→        }
   261→    };
   262→
   263→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   264→    let container_name = config::container_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   265→    let session_name = tmux::session_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   266→    debug!(
   267→        repo = %entry.repo,
   268→        branch = %entry.branch,
   269→        "resolved workspace"
   270→    );
   271→
   272→    // Step 1: Resolve workspace directory
   273→    let workspace_dir = if let Some(ref path) = entry.path {
   274→        let dir = PathBuf::from(path);
   275→        if !dir.join(".git").exists() {
   276→            error!(
   277→                "workspace path {} does not contain a git repo",
   278→                dir.display()
   279→            );
   280→            return 1;
   281→        }
   282→        dir
   283→    } else {
   284→        // Try to clone from local main workspace first (fast, hardlinks)
   285→        let target_dir = config::workspace_dir(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   286→        let main_workspace_path = st
   287→            .workspaces_for_repo(&entry.repo)
   288→            .into_iter()
   289→            .find(|ws| ws.path.is_some())
   290→            .and_then(|ws| ws.path.as_ref().map(PathBuf::from));
   291→
   292→        match main_workspace_path {
   293→            Some(main_path) if main_path.join(".git").exists() => {
   294→                info!("Cloning from local main workspace...");
   295→                match clone::clone_from_local(&main_path, &target_dir, &entry.branch) {
   296→                    Ok(dir) => dir,
   297→                    Err(e) => {
   298→                        error!("local clone failed: {e}");
   299→                        return 1;
   300→                    }
   301→                }
   302→            }
   303→            _ => {
   304→                // Fallback: clone from remote URL
   305→                match clone::clone_workspace(
   306→                    &workspace_root,
   307→                    &entry.repo,
   308→                    &entry.url,
   309→                    &entry.branch,
   310→                ) {
   311→                    Ok(dir) => dir,
   312→                    Err(e) => {
   313→                        error!("clone failed: {e}");
   314→                        return 1;
   315→                    }
   316→                }
   317→            }
   318→        }
   319→    };
   320→
   321→    // Step 2: Handle shared files
   322→    let hints = config::load_hints(&workspace_dir).unwrap_or_default();
   323→    if let Some(ref shared_config) = hints.shared
   324→        && !shared_config.files.is_empty()
   325→        && let Ok(shared_dir) = shared::ensure_shared_dir(&entry.repo)
   326→    {
   327→        if entry.path.is_some() {
   328→            // Main workspace: ensure shared files are initialized
   329→            match shared::init_from_main(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   330→                Ok(moved) => {
   331→                    for f in &moved {
   332→                        info!("  shared: {f} → ~/.dual/shared/{}/", entry.repo);
   333→                    }
   334→                }
   335→                Err(e) => warn!("shared init failed: {e}"),
   336→            }
   337→        } else {
   338→            // Branch workspace: copy shared files
   339→            match shared::copy_to_branch(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   340→                Ok(copied) => {
   341→                    for f in &copied {
   342→                        info!("  shared: copied {f}");
   343→                    }
   344→                }
   345→                Err(e) => warn!("shared copy failed: {e}"),
   346→            }
   347→        }
   348→    }
   349→
   350→    // Step 3: Ensure container exists and is running
   351→    let is_new_container = matches!(
   352→        container::status(&container_name),
   353→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing
   354→    );
   355→    match container::status(&container_name) {
   356→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing => {
   357→            info!("Creating container {container_name}...");
   358→            if let Err(e) = container::create(
   359→                &container_name,
   360→                &workspace_dir,
   361→                &hints.image,
   362→                &hints.env,
   363→                &hints.anonymous_volumes,
   364→            ) {
   365→                error!("container create failed: {e}");
   366→                return 1;
   367→            }
   368→            if let Err(e) = container::start(&container_name) {
   369→                error!("container start failed: {e}");
   370→                return 1;
   371→            }
   372→        }
   373→        container::ContainerStatus::Stopped => {
   374→            info!("Starting container {container_name}...");
   375→            if let Err(e) = container::start(&container_name) {
   376→                error!("container start failed: {e}");
   377→                return 1;
   378→            }
   379→        }
   380→        container::ContainerStatus::Running => {}
   381→    }
   382→
   383→    // Step 3.5: Run setup command on new containers
   384→    if is_new_container && let Some(ref setup) = hints.setup {
   385→        info!("Running setup: {setup}");
   386→        if let Err(e) = container::exec_setup(&container_name, setup) {
   387→            error!("setup failed: {e}");
   388→            return 1;
   389→        }
   390→    }
   391→
   392→    // Step 4: Write shell RC file
   393→    let rc_path = match shell::write_rc_file(&container_name, &hints.extra_commands) {
   394→        Ok(p) => p,
   395→        Err(e) => {
   396→            error!("failed to write shell RC: {e}");
   397→            return 1;
   398→        }
   399→    };
   400→
   401→    // Step 5: Create tmux session if not alive
   402→    if !tmux::is_alive(&session_name) {
   403→        let source_cmd = shell::source_file_command(&rc_path);
   404→        if let Err(e) = tmux::create_session(&session_name, &workspace_dir, Some(&source_cmd)) {
   405→            error!("tmux session creation failed: {e}");
   406→            return 1;
   407→        }
   408→    }
   409→
   410→    // Step 6: Attach
   411→    info!("Attaching to {session_name}...");
   412→    if let Err(e) = tmux::attach(&session_name) {
   413→        error!("tmux attach failed: {e}");
   414→        return 1;
   415→    }
   416→
   417→    0
   418→}
   419→
   420→/// List all configured workspaces with their live status.
   421→fn cmd_list() -> i32 {
   422→    let st = match state::load() {
   423→        Ok(s) => s,
   424→        Err(e) => {
   425→            error!("{e}");
   426→            return 1;
   427→        }
   428→    };
   429→
   430→    let workspaces = st.all_workspaces();
   431→    if workspaces.is_empty() {
   432→        info!("No workspaces configured.");
   433→        return 0;
   434→    }
   435→
   436→    print_workspace_status(&st);
   437→    0
   438→}
   439→
   440→/// Destroy a workspace: tmux → container → clone.
   441→fn cmd_destroy(workspace_arg: Option<&str>) -> i32 {
   442→    let mut st = match state::load() {
   443→        Ok(s) => s,
   444→        Err(e) => {
   445→            error!("{e}");
   446→            return 1;
   447→        }
   448→    };
   449→
   450→    // Resolve workspace: use arg if provided, otherwise auto-detect from cwd
   451→    let workspace;
   452→    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {
   453→        workspace = ws.to_string();
   454→        match st.resolve_workspace(ws) {
   455→            Some(e) => e.clone(),
   456→            None => {
   457→                error!("unknown workspace '{ws}'");
   458→                return 1;
   459→            }
   460→        }
   461→    } else {
   462→        match detect_workspace(&st) {
   463→            Some(e) => {
   464→                workspace = config::workspace_id(&e.repo, &e.branch);
   465→                info!("Auto-detected workspace: {workspace}");
   466→                e
   467→            }
   468→            None => {
   469→                error!("could not detect workspace from current directory");
   470→                info!("Usage: dual destroy [workspace]");
   471→                return 1;
   472→            }
   473→        }
   474→    };
   475→
   476→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   477→    let container_name = config::container_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   478→    let session_name = tmux::session_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   479→
   480→    // Destroy tmux session
   481→    if tmux::is_alive(&session_name) {
   482→        info!("Destroying tmux session {session_name}...");
   483→        if let Err(e) = tmux::destroy(&session_name) {
   484→            warn!("tmux destroy failed: {e}");
   485→        }
   486→    }
   487→
   488→    // Stop and remove container
   489→    match container::status(&container_name) {
   490→        container::ContainerStatus::Running => {
   491→            info!("Stopping container {container_name}...");
   492→            if let Err(e) = container::stop(&container_name) {
   493→                warn!("container stop failed: {e}");
   494→            }
   495→            info!("Removing container {container_name}...");
   496→            if let Err(e) = container::destroy(&container_name) {
   497→                warn!("container remove failed: {e}");
   498→            }
   499→        }
   500→        container::ContainerStatus::Stopped => {
   501→            info!("Removing container {container_name}...");
   502→            if let Err(e) = container::destroy(&container_name) {
   503→                warn!("container remove failed: {e}");
   504→            }
   505→        }
   506→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing => {}
   507→    }
   508→
   509→    // Remove clone (only for non-explicit-path workspaces)
   510→    if entry.path.is_none() && clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch)
   511→    {
   512→        info!("Removing clone...");
   513→        if let Err(e) = clone::remove_workspace(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch) {
   514→            error!("failed to remove clone: {e}");
   515→            return 1;
   516→        }
   517→    }
   518→
   519→    // Remove from state
   520→    st.remove_workspace(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   521→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   522→        warn!("failed to save state: {e}");
   523→    }
   524→
   525→    info!("Workspace '{workspace}' destroyed.");
   526→    0
   527→}
   528→
   529→/// Open workspace services in the default browser.
   530→fn cmd_open(workspace: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   531→    let st = match state::load() {
   532→        Ok(s) => s,
   533→        Err(e) => {
   534→            error!("{e}");
   535→            return 1;
   536→        }
   537→    };
   538→
   539→    let url_groups = proxy::workspace_urls(&st);
   540→    if url_groups.is_empty() {
   541→        info!("No URLs configured. Add 'ports' to .dual.toml in your repo.");
   542→        return 0;
   543→    }
   544→
   545→    // Filter by workspace if specified
   546→    let filtered: Vec<_> = match &workspace {
   547→        Some(ws) => url_groups.into_iter().filter(|(id, _)| id == ws).collect(),
   548→        None => url_groups,
   549→    };
   550→
   551→    if filtered.is_empty() {
   552→        if let Some(ws) = &workspace {
   553→            error!("no URLs for workspace '{ws}'");
   554→        }
   555→        return 1;
   556→    }
   557→
   558→    for (_, urls) in &filtered {
   559→        for url_line in urls {
   560→            // Extract the URL part (after the status icon)
   561→            let url = url_line
   562→                .trim()
   563→                .trim_start_matches('\u{25cf}')
   564→                .trim_start_matches('\u{25cb}')
   565→                .trim();
   566→            let http_url = format!("http://{url}");
   567→            #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
   568→            let _ = std::process::Command::new("open").arg(&http_url).spawn();
   569→            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
   570→            let _ = std::process::Command::new("xdg-open")
   571→                .arg(&http_url)
   572→                .spawn();
   573→            info!("Opening {http_url}");
   574→        }
   575→    }
   576→
   577→    0
   578→}
   579→
   580→/// Show workspace URLs.
   581→fn cmd_urls(workspace: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   582→    let st = match state::load() {
   583→        Ok(s) => s,
   584→        Err(e) => {
   585→            error!("{e}");
   586→            return 1;
   587→        }
   588→    };
   589→
   590→    let url_groups = proxy::workspace_urls(&st);
   591→    if url_groups.is_empty() {
   592→        info!("No URLs configured. Add 'ports' to .dual.toml in your repo.");
   593→        return 0;
   594→    }
   595→
   596→    // Filter by workspace if specified
   597→    let filtered: Vec<_> = match &workspace {
   598→        Some(ws) => url_groups.into_iter().filter(|(id, _)| id == ws).collect(),
   599→        None => url_groups,
   600→    };
   601→
   602→    for (workspace_id, urls) in &filtered {
   603→        info!("{workspace_id}");
   604→        for url in urls {
   605→            info!("{url}");
   606→        }
   607→        info!("");
   608→    }
   609→
   610→    0
   611→}
   612→
   613→/// Start the reverse proxy.
   614→fn cmd_proxy() -> i32 {
   615→    let st = match state::load() {
   616→        Ok(s) => s,
   617→        Err(e) => {
   618→            error!("{e}");
   619→            return 1;
   620→        }
   621→    };
   622→
   623→    let rt = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().expect("failed to create tokio runtime");
   624→    match rt.block_on(proxy::start(&st)) {
   625→        Ok(()) => 0,
   626→        Err(e) => {
   627→            error!("proxy failed: {e}");
   628→            1
   629→        }
   630→    }
   631→}
   632→
   633→/// Output shell RC for a container (used by `eval "$(dual shell-rc <name>)"`).
   634→fn cmd_shell_rc(container_name: &str) -> i32 {
   635→    print!("{}", shell::generate_rc(container_name, &[]));
   636→    0
   637→}
   638→
   639→/// Sync shared config files for a workspace.
   640→fn cmd_sync(workspace_arg: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   641→    let st = match state::load() {
   642→        Ok(s) => s,
   643→        Err(e) => {
   644→            error!("{e}");
   645→            return 1;
   646→        }
   647→    };
   648→
   649→    // Resolve which workspace we're syncing
   650→    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {
   651→        match st.resolve_workspace(&ws) {
   652→            Some(e) => e.clone(),
   653→            None => {
   654→                error!("unknown workspace '{ws}'");
   655→                return 1;
   656→            }
   657→        }
   658→    } else {
   659→        match detect_workspace(&st) {
   660→            Some(e) => e,
   661→            None => {
   662→                error!("not inside a dual workspace");
   663→                info!("Usage: dual sync [workspace]");
   664→                return 1;
   665→            }
   666→        }
   667→    };
   668→
   669→    // Load hints
   670→    let workspace_dir = st.workspace_dir(&entry);
   671→    let hints = config::load_hints(&workspace_dir).unwrap_or_default();
   672→    let shared_config = match &hints.shared {
   673→        Some(s) if !s.files.is_empty() => s,
   674→        _ => {
   675→            error!("no [shared] section in .dual.toml (or files list is empty)");
   676→            return 1;
   677→        }
   678→    };
   679→
   680→    let shared_dir = match shared::ensure_shared_dir(&entry.repo) {
   681→        Ok(d) => d,
   682→        Err(e) => {
   683→            error!("{e}");
   684→            return 1;
   685→        }
   686→    };
   687→
   688→    let is_main = entry.path.is_some();
   689→
   690→    if is_main {
   691→        // Main workspace: init shared dir, then prompt to sync all branches
   692→        match shared::init_from_main(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   693→            Ok(moved) => {
   694→                for f in &moved {
   695→                    info!("  moved {f} → shared/");
   696→                }
   697→            }
   698→            Err(e) => {
   699→                error!("{e}");
   700→                return 1;
   701→            }
   702→        }
   703→
   704→        // Prompt to sync all branches
   705→        let branches: Vec<_> = st
   706→            .workspaces_for_repo(&entry.repo)
   707→            .into_iter()
   708→            .filter(|ws| ws.path.is_none())
   709→            .collect();
   710→
   711→        if branches.is_empty() {
   712→            info!("No branch workspaces to sync.");
   713→            return 0;
   714→        }
   715→
   716→        // Interactive prompt — use println! directly since this is user interaction
   717→        println!(
   718→            "\nSync shared files to ALL {} branch workspace(s)? [y/N]",
   719→            branches.len()
   720→        );
   721→        let mut input = String::new();
   722→        std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut input).unwrap_or(0);
   723→        if !input.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("y") {
   724→            info!("Cancelled.");
   725→            return 0;
   726→        }
   727→
   728→        for branch_entry in &branches {
   729→            let branch_dir = st.workspace_dir(branch_entry);
   730→            if !branch_dir.exists() {
   731→                continue; // Not yet cloned
   732→            }
   733→            let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&branch_entry.repo, &branch_entry.branch);
   734→            match shared::copy_to_branch(&branch_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   735→                Ok(copied) => {
   736→                    info!("{ws_id}: synced {} file(s)", copied.len());
   737→                }
   738→                Err(e) => error!("{ws_id}: {e}"),
   739→            }
   740→        }
   741→    } else {
   742→        // Branch workspace: copy from shared dir
   743→        match shared::copy_to_branch(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   744→            Ok(copied) => {
   745→                if copied.is_empty() {
   746→                    info!(
   747→                        "No shared files available yet. Run `dual sync` in the main workspace first."
   748→                    );
   749→                } else {
   750→                    for f in &copied {
   751→                        info!("  synced {f}");
   752→                    }
   753→                }
   754→            }
   755→            Err(e) => {
   756→                error!("{e}");
   757→                return 1;
   758→            }
   759→        }
   760→    }
   761→
   762→    0
   763→}
   764→
   765→/// Detect the repo name from the current working directory.
   766→///
   767→/// Matches the git remote URL of the cwd against known workspace URLs in state.
   768→fn detect_repo_from_cwd(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<String> {
   769→    let (_, url, _) = detect_git_repo().ok()?;
   770→
   771→    // Match against known workspace URLs
   772→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   773→        if ws.url == url {
   774→            return Some(ws.repo.clone());
   775→        }
   776→    }
   777→
   778→    // Also try matching by git root path against workspace paths
   779→    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;
   780→    let root = std::process::Command::new("git")
   781→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   782→        .output()
   783→        .ok()
   784→        .filter(|o| o.status.success())
   785→        .map(|o| PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string()))?;
   786→
   787→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   788→        let ws_dir = st.workspace_dir(ws);
   789→        if ws_dir == root || ws_dir == cwd {
   790→            return Some(ws.repo.clone());
   791→        }
   792→    }
   793→
   794→    None
   795→}
   796→
   797→/// Detect which workspace the current directory belongs to.
   798→fn detect_workspace(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<state::WorkspaceEntry> {
   799→    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;
   800→
   801→    // Try git root first (handles being in subdirectories)
   802→    let root = std::process::Command::new("git")
   803→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   804→        .output()
   805→        .ok()
   806→        .filter(|o| o.status.success())
   807→        .map(|o| PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string()))
   808→        .unwrap_or(cwd);
   809→
   810→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   811→        let ws_dir = st.workspace_dir(ws);
   812→        if ws_dir == root {
   813→            return Some(ws.clone());
   814→        }
   815→    }
   816→    None
   817→}
   818→
   819→/// Print workspace status grouped by repo.
   820→fn print_workspace_status(st: &state::WorkspaceState) {
   821→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   822→
   823→    // Collect unique repo names in order of first appearance
   824→    let mut repos: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   825→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   826→        if !repos.contains(&ws.repo) {
   827→            repos.push(ws.repo.clone());
   828→        }
   829→    }
   830→
   831→    for repo in &repos {
   832→        println!("{repo}");
   833→        for ws in st.workspaces_for_repo(repo) {
   834→            let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   835→            let session_name = tmux::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   836→
   837→            let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {
   838→                ws.path
   839→                    .as_ref()
   840→                    .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join(".git").exists())
   841→                    .unwrap_or(false)
   842→            } else {
   843→                clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)
   844→            };
   845→            let container_st = container::status(&container_name);
   846→            let tmux_alive = tmux::is_alive(&session_name);
   847→
   848→            let (icon, status_text) = match (&container_st, tmux_alive) {
   849→                (container::ContainerStatus::Running, true) => {
   850→                    ("\u{25cf}", "running  (container: up, tmux: attached)")
   851→                }
   852→                (container::ContainerStatus::Running, false) => {
   853→                    ("\u{25cf}", "running  (container: up, tmux: none)")
   854→                }
   855→                (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, true) => (
   856→                    "\u{25cb}",
   857→                    "stopped  (container: stopped, tmux: background)",
   858→                ),
   859→                (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, false) => {
   860→                    ("\u{25cb}", "stopped  (container: stopped, tmux: none)")
   861→                }
   862→                (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) if clone_exists => {
   863→                    ("\u{25cb}", "stopped  (not launched)")
   864→                }
   865→                (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) => {
   866→                    ("\u{25cc}", "lazy     (not cloned yet)")
   867→                }
   868→            };
   869→
   870→            let branch_display = config::decode_branch(&config::encode_branch(&ws.branch));
   871→            println!("  {branch_display:<24} {icon} {status_text}");
   872→        }
   873→        println!();
   874→    }
   875→}
   876→
   877→/// Detect git repo info from the current directory.
   878→fn detect_git_repo() -> Result<(PathBuf, String, String), String> {
   879→    // Get repo root
   880→    let root_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   881→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   882→        .output()
   883→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   884→    if !root_output.status.success() {
   885→        return Err("not inside a git repository".to_string());
   886→    }
   887→    let root = PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&root_output.stdout).trim());
   888→
   889→    // Get remote URL
   890→    let url_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   891→        .args(["remote", "get-url", "origin"])
   892→        .output()
   893→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   894→    let url = if url_output.status.success() {
   895→        String::from_utf8_lossy(&url_output.stdout)
   896→            .trim()
   897→            .to_string()
   898→    } else {
   899→        root.to_string_lossy().to_string() // local-only repo, use path as URL
   900→    };
   901→
   902→    // Get current branch
   903→    let branch_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   904→        .args(["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"])
   905→        .output()
   906→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   907→    let branch = String::from_utf8_lossy(&branch_output.stdout)
   908→        .trim()
   909→        .to_string();
   910→
   911→    Ok((root, url, branch))
   912→}
   913→
   914→/// Derive a short repo name from a directory path.
   915→/// "/Users/jeevan/code/lightfast" → "lightfast"
   916→fn derive_repo_name(path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
   917→    path.file_name()
   918→        .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
   919→        .unwrap_or("repo")
   920→        .to_string()
   921→}

     5→const HINTS_FILENAME: &str = ".dual.toml";
     6→const DEFAULT_IMAGE: &str = "node:20";
     7→
     8→/// Shared configuration file propagation settings.
     9→#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
    10→pub struct SharedConfig {
    11→    /// Files and directories to share across workspaces.
    12→    /// e.g. [".vercel", ".env.local", ".env"]
    13→    #[serde(default)]

    16→
    17→/// Per-repo runtime hints, read from .dual.toml in a workspace directory.
    18→#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
    19→pub struct RepoHints {
    20→    /// Docker image to use for containers (default: "node:20")
    21→    #[serde(default = "default_image")]
    22→    pub image: String,
    23→
    24→    /// Ports that services bind to inside the container
    25→    #[serde(default)]
    26→    pub ports: Vec<u16>,
    27→
    28→    /// Setup command to run after container creation (e.g. "pnpm install")
    29→    pub setup: Option<String>,
    30→
    31→    /// Environment variables for the container
    32→    #[serde(default)]
    33→    pub env: HashMap<String, String>,
    34→
    35→    /// Additional commands to route to the container (merged with defaults)
    36→    #[serde(default)]
    37→    pub extra_commands: Vec<String>,
    38→
    39→    /// Directories to isolate with anonymous Docker volumes
    40→    #[serde(default = "default_anonymous_volumes")]
    41→    pub anonymous_volumes: Vec<String>,
    42→
    43→    /// Shared files to propagate across workspaces
    44→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    45→    pub shared: Option<SharedConfig>,
    46→}
    47→
    48→fn default_image() -> String {
    49→    DEFAULT_IMAGE.to_string()
    50→}
    51→
    52→fn default_anonymous_volumes() -> Vec<String> {
    53→    vec!["node_modules".to_string()]
    54→}
    55→
    56→impl Default for RepoHints {
    57→    fn default() -> Self {
    58→        Self {
    59→            image: DEFAULT_IMAGE.to_string(),
    60→            ports: Vec::new(),
    61→            setup: None,
    62→            env: HashMap::new(),
    63→            extra_commands: Vec::new(),
    64→            anonymous_volumes: default_anonymous_volumes(),
    65→            shared: None,

    69→
    70→/// Get the shared config directory for a repo: ~/.dual/shared/{repo}/
    71→pub fn shared_dir(repo: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    72→    dirs::home_dir().map(|home| home.join(".dual").join("shared").join(repo))
    73→}
    74→
    75→/// Load RepoHints from a workspace directory's .dual.toml.
    76→/// Returns default hints if the file doesn't exist.
    77→pub fn load_hints(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<RepoHints, HintsError> {
    78→    let path = workspace_dir.join(HINTS_FILENAME);
    79→
    80→    if !path.exists() {
    81→        return Ok(RepoHints::default());
    82→    }
    83→
    84→    let contents =
    85→        std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|e| HintsError::ReadError(path.clone(), e))?;
    86→    let hints: RepoHints =
    87→        toml::from_str(&contents).map_err(|e| HintsError::ParseError(path, e))?;
    88→    Ok(hints)
    89→}
    90→
    91→/// Write RepoHints to a workspace directory's .dual.toml.
    92→pub fn write_hints(workspace_dir: &Path, hints: &RepoHints) -> Result<(), HintsError> {
    93→    let path = workspace_dir.join(HINTS_FILENAME);
    94→    let contents = toml::to_string_pretty(hints).map_err(HintsError::SerializeError)?;
    95→    std::fs::write(&path, contents).map_err(|e| HintsError::WriteError(path, e))?;
    96→    Ok(())
    97→}
    98→
    99→/// Write a default .dual.toml with helpful comments and examples.
   100→/// Used by `dual add` when creating a new repo config.
   101→pub fn write_default_hints(repo_root: &Path) -> Result<(), HintsError> {
   102→    let template = r#"# Dual workspace configuration
   103→# See: https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual
   104→
   105→# Docker image for the container runtime
   106→image = "node:20"
   107→
   108→# Ports your dev server uses (for reverse proxy routing)
   109→# Example: ports = [3000, 3001]
   110→# ports = []
   111→
   112→# Shell command to run after container creation (e.g., dependency install)
   113→# Example: setup = "pnpm install"
   114→# setup = ""
   115→
   116→# Environment variables passed to the container
   117→# Example:
   118→# [env]
   119→# NODE_ENV = "development"
   120→
   121→# Commands to route to the container (in addition to defaults)
   122→# Default: npm, npx, pnpm, node, python, python3, pip, pip3, curl, make
   123→# Example: extra_commands = ["cargo", "go", "ruby"]
   124→# extra_commands = []
   125→
   126→# Directories to isolate with anonymous Docker volumes
   127→# These directories get their own volume so they don't sync between host/container
   128→# Example: anonymous_volumes = ["node_modules", ".next", "target"]
   129→# anonymous_volumes = ["node_modules"]
   130→
   131→# Files to share across all workspaces of this repo
   132→# These are gitignored files that should be available in every branch workspace
   133→# [shared]
   134→# files = [".env.local", ".vercel"]
   135→"#;
   136→    let hints_path = repo_root.join(HINTS_FILENAME);
   137→    std::fs::write(&hints_path, template).map_err(|e| HintsError::WriteError(hints_path, e))?;
   138→    Ok(())
   139→}
   140→
   141→/// Parse hints from TOML string (for testing).
   142→pub fn parse_hints(toml_str: &str) -> Result<RepoHints, HintsError> {
   143→    let hints: RepoHints = toml::from_str(toml_str)
   144→        .map_err(|e| HintsError::ParseError(PathBuf::from("<string>"), e))?;
   145→    Ok(hints)
   146→}
   147→
   148→/// Compute the workspace identifier from repo + branch.
   149→/// e.g. ("lightfast", "feat/auth") → "lightfast-feat__auth"
   150→pub fn workspace_id(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String {
   151→    format!("{}-{}", repo, encode_branch(branch))
   152→}
   153→
   154→/// Get the workspace directory for a repo + branch combination.
   155→/// Layout: {workspace_root}/{repo}/{encoded_branch}/
   156→pub fn workspace_dir(workspace_root: &Path, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> PathBuf {
   157→    workspace_root.join(repo).join(encode_branch(branch))
   158→}
   159→
   160→/// Compute the container name for a repo + branch combination.
   161→/// Pattern: dual-{repo}-{encoded_branch}
   162→pub fn container_name(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String {
   163→    format!("dual-{}-{}", repo, encode_branch(branch))
   164→}
   165→
   166→/// Encode a branch name for filesystem use.
   167→/// Replaces `/` with `__` (double underscore).
   168→/// e.g. "feat/auth" → "feat__auth"
   169→pub fn encode_branch(branch: &str) -> String {
   170→    branch.replace('/', "__")
   171→}
   172→
   173→/// Decode an encoded branch name back to the original.
   174→/// Replaces `__` with `/`.
   175→/// e.g. "feat__auth" → "feat/auth"
   176→pub fn decode_branch(encoded: &str) -> String {
   177→    encoded.replace("__", "/")
   178→}
   179→
   180→#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   181→pub enum HintsError {
   182→    #[error("Failed to read {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   183→    ReadError(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   184→
   185→    #[error("Failed to write {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   186→    WriteError(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   187→
   188→    #[error("Failed to parse {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   189→    ParseError(PathBuf, toml::de::Error),
   190→
   191→    #[error("Failed to serialize hints: {0}")]
   192→    SerializeError(toml::ser::Error),
   193→}
   194→
   195→#[cfg(test)]
   196→mod tests {
   197→    use super::*;
   198→
   199→    #[test]
   200→    fn encode_branch_with_slash() {
   201→        assert_eq!(encode_branch("feat/auth"), "feat__auth");
   202→        assert_eq!(encode_branch("fix/memory-leak"), "fix__memory-leak");
   203→        assert_eq!(encode_branch("main"), "main");
   204→    }
   205→
   206→    #[test]
   207→    fn decode_branch_roundtrip() {
   208→        let original = "feat/auth";
   209→        assert_eq!(decode_branch(&encode_branch(original)), original);
   210→    }
   211→
   212→    #[test]

   217→            "dual-lightfast-feat__auth"

   219→    }
   220→

   225→    }
   226→
   227→    #[test]

   244→        assert_eq!(hints.anonymous_volumes, vec!["node_modules".to_string()]);

   253→

   257→image = "python:3.12"
   258→ports = [3000, 3001]
   259→setup = "pnpm install"
   260→
   261→[env]

   263→"#;
   264→        let hints = parse_hints(toml).unwrap();

   285→
   286→    #[test]
   287→    fn write_and_load_hints() {
   288→        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join("dual-test-hints-roundtrip");
   289→        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);

   292→        let hints = RepoHints {

   297→            extra_commands: vec!["cargo".to_string()],

   299→            shared: None,

   316→
   317→        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(".dual.toml")).unwrap();
   318→        assert!(content.contains("# Dual workspace configuration"));

     1→/// E2E integration tests for Dual.
     2→///
     3→/// These tests require Docker and tmux to be available on the system.
     4→/// Run with: `cargo test --test e2e`
     5→///
     6→/// Tests are marked `#[ignore]` so they don't run during `cargo test` (which runs
     7→/// unit tests only). Run ignored tests with: `cargo test --test e2e -- --ignored`

    28→            .expect("clone should succeed");
    29→
    30→    assert!(clone_dir.exists());

    74→
    75→#[test]
    76→#[ignore] // Requires Docker
    77→fn container_lifecycle() {
    78→    let mut f = harness::TestFixture::new();
    79→    let temp = f.temp_dir();
    80→    let repo_dir = fixtures::create_fixture_repo(&temp);
    81→    let workspace_root = temp.join("workspaces");
    82→
    83→    let state = fixtures::fixture_state(&workspace_root, &repo_dir, "test-app", "main");
    84→    let ws_root = state.workspace_root();
    85→    let url = repo_dir.to_string_lossy().to_string();
    86→
    87→    // Clone workspace first (container needs bind mount target)
    88→    dual::clone::clone_workspace(&ws_root, "test-app", &url, "main").expect("clone should succeed");
    89→
    90→    // Use test-prefixed container name
    91→    let container_name = f.container_name();
    92→    f.register_container(container_name.clone());
    93→
    94→    // Create container with explicit name
    95→    let workspace_dir = dual::config::workspace_dir(&ws_root, "test-app", "main");
    96→    let args = dual::container::build_create_args(
    97→        &container_name,
    98→        &workspace_dir,
    99→        "node:20",
   100→        &std::collections::HashMap::new(),
   101→        &["node_modules".to_string()],
   102→    );
   103→    let output = Command::new("docker").args(&args).output().unwrap();
   104→    assert!(
   105→        output.status.success(),
   106→        "docker create failed: {}",
   107→        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
   108→    );
   109→
   110→    // Status should be Stopped (created but not started)
   111→    assert_eq!(
   112→        dual::container::status(&container_name),
   113→        dual::container::ContainerStatus::Stopped
   114→    );
   115→
   116→    // Start
   117→    dual::container::start(&container_name).expect("start should succeed");
   118→    assert_eq!(
   119→        dual::container::status(&container_name),
   120→        dual::container::ContainerStatus::Running
   121→    );
   122→
   123→    // Stop
   124→    dual::container::stop(&container_name).expect("stop should succeed");
   125→    assert_eq!(
   126→        dual::container::status(&container_name),
   127→        dual::container::ContainerStatus::Stopped
   128→    );
   129→
   130→    // Destroy (remove)
   131→    dual::container::destroy(&container_name).expect("destroy should succeed");
   132→    assert_eq!(
   133→        dual::container::status(&container_name),
   134→        dual::container::ContainerStatus::Missing
   135→    );
   136→}

   228→fn network_isolation_same_port() {
   229→    let mut f1 = harness::TestFixture::new();
   230→    let mut f2 = harness::TestFixture::new();
   231→
   232→    // Set up fixture repos and workspaces for both
   233→    let temp1 = f1.temp_dir();
   234→    let repo1 = fixtures::create_fixture_repo(&temp1);
   235→    let ws_root1 = temp1.join("workspaces");
   236→    let state1 = fixtures::fixture_state(&ws_root1, &repo1, "test-app-1", "main");
   237→    let root1 = state1.workspace_root();
   238→    let clone1 =
   239→        dual::clone::clone_workspace(&root1, "test-app-1", &repo1.to_string_lossy(), "main")
   240→            .unwrap();
   241→
   242→    let temp2 = f2.temp_dir();
   243→    let repo2 = fixtures::create_fixture_repo(&temp2);
   244→    let ws_root2 = temp2.join("workspaces");
   245→    let state2 = fixtures::fixture_state(&ws_root2, &repo2, "test-app-2", "main");
   246→    let root2 = state2.workspace_root();
   247→    let clone2 =
   248→        dual::clone::clone_workspace(&root2, "test-app-2", &repo2.to_string_lossy(), "main")
   249→            .unwrap();
   250→
   251→    // Create and start both containers
   252→    let name1 = f1.container_name();
   253→    let name2 = f2.container_name();
   254→    f1.register_container(name1.clone());
   255→    f2.register_container(name2.clone());
   256→
   257→    let empty_env = std::collections::HashMap::new();
   258→    let default_vols = ["node_modules".to_string()];
   259→    let args1 =
   260→        dual::container::build_create_args(&name1, &clone1, "node:20", &empty_env, &default_vols);
   261→    let args2 =
   262→        dual::container::build_create_args(&name2, &clone2, "node:20", &empty_env, &default_vols);
   263→
   264→    let out1 = Command::new("docker").args(&args1).output().unwrap();
   265→    let out2 = Command::new("docker").args(&args2).output().unwrap();
   266→    assert!(out1.status.success());
   267→    assert!(out2.status.success());
   268→
   269→    dual::container::start(&name1).unwrap();
   270→    dual::container::start(&name2).unwrap();
   271→
   272→    // Both containers should be running
   273→    assert_eq!(
   274→        dual::container::status(&name1),
   275→        dual::container::ContainerStatus::Running
   276→    );
   277→    assert_eq!(
   278→        dual::container::status(&name2),
   279→        dual::container::ContainerStatus::Running
   280→    );
   281→
   282→    // Start node server on :3000 in both containers (bind same port, different namespaces)
   283→    let start1 = Command::new("docker")
   284→        .args([
   285→            "exec",
   286→            "-d",
   287→            &name1,
   288→            "node",
   289→            "-e",
   290→            "require('http').createServer((q,s)=>{s.end('c1')}).listen(3000)",
   291→        ])
   292→        .output()
   293→        .unwrap();
   294→    let start2 = Command::new("docker")
   295→        .args([
   296→            "exec",
   297→            "-d",
   298→            &name2,
   299→            "node",
   300→            "-e",
   301→            "require('http').createServer((q,s)=>{s.end('c2')}).listen(3000)",
   302→        ])
   303→        .output()
   304→        .unwrap();
   305→    assert!(start1.status.success());
   306→    assert!(start2.status.success());
   307→
   308→    // Give servers a moment to start
   309→    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1));
   310→
   311→    // Curl from inside each container to verify both are running on :3000
   312→    let curl1 = Command::new("docker")
   313→        .args(["exec", &name1, "curl", "-s", "http://localhost:3000"])
   314→        .output()
   315→        .unwrap();
   316→    let curl2 = Command::new("docker")
   317→        .args(["exec", &name2, "curl", "-s", "http://localhost:3000"])
   318→        .output()
   319→        .unwrap();
   320→
   321→    let body1 = String::from_utf8_lossy(&curl1.stdout);
   322→    let body2 = String::from_utf8_lossy(&curl2.stdout);
   323→
   324→    assert_eq!(body1.trim(), "c1", "container 1 should serve 'c1'");
   325→    assert_eq!(body2.trim(), "c2", "container 2 should serve 'c2'");
   326→}

   343→    // Create detached session

   345→
   346→    // Session should be alive

   348→
   349→    // Destroy session
   350→    dual::tmux::destroy(&session_name).expect("destroy should succeed");
   351→

   370→
   371→    // Send a command that creates a file
   372→    let marker = format!("dual-tmux-test-{}", f.short_id);
   373→    dual::tmux::send_keys(&session_name, &format!("echo '{}' > marker.txt", marker))
   374→        .expect("send_keys should succeed");
   375→
   376→    // Poll for marker file with timeout (tmux send-keys is async)
   377→    let marker_path = temp.join("marker.txt");
   378→    let start = std::time::Instant::now();
   379→    let timeout = std::time::Duration::from_secs(10);
   380→    while !marker_path.exists() {
   381→        assert!(
   382→            start.elapsed() < timeout,
   383→            "marker file should exist (timed out after {timeout:?})"
   384→        );
   385→        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100));
   386→    }
   387→
   388→    let contents = std::fs::read_to_string(&marker_path).unwrap();
   389→    assert_eq!(contents.trim(), marker);
   390→}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/src/backend.rs",
  "content": "use std::path::Path;\n\n/// Abstraction over terminal multiplexers (tmux, zellij, etc.)\n///\n/// Each implementation wraps a specific multiplexer binary and provides\n/// session lifecycle management. The trait is object-safe so command handlers\n/// can accept `&dyn MultiplexerBackend`.\npub trait MultiplexerBackend {\n    /// Check if the multiplexer binary is installed and available.\n    fn is_available(&self) -> bool;\n\n    /// Create a new detached session with the given name and working directory.\n    /// Optionally send an initial command after creation.\n    fn create_session(\n        &self,\n        session_name: &str,\n        cwd: &Path,\n        init_cmd: Option<&str>,\n    ) -> Result<(), BackendError>;\n\n    /// Attach the current terminal to an existing session.\n    /// If already inside the multiplexer, use switch-client instead.\n    fn attach(&self, session_name: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError>;\n\n    /// Detach the current client from a session.\n    fn detach(&self, session_name: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError>;\n\n    /// Destroy a session and all its windows/panes.\n    fn destroy(&self, session_name: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError>;\n\n    /// Check if a session exists and has running processes.\n    fn is_alive(&self, session_name: &str) -> bool;\n\n    /// List all Dual-managed sessions (filtered by `dual-` prefix).\n    fn list_sessions(&self) -> Vec<String>;\n\n    /// Send keystrokes to a session's active pane.\n    fn send_keys(&self, session_name: &str, keys: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError>;\n\n    /// Check if we're currently inside this multiplexer.\n    fn is_inside(&self) -> bool;\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]\npub enum BackendError {\n    #[error(\"{multiplexer} not found: {detail}\")]\n    NotFound {\n        multiplexer: String,\n        detail: String,\n    },\n\n    #[error(\"{multiplexer} {operation} failed for session '{session}': {stderr}\")]\n    Failed {\n        multiplexer: String,\n        operation: String,\n        session: String,\n        stderr: String,\n    },\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use std::path::Path;\nuse std::process::Command;\n\nuse crate::backend::{BackendError, MultiplexerBackend};\n\n/// Session name prefix for dual-managed sessions.\nconst SESSION_PREFIX: &str = \"dual-\";\n\n/// Terminal multiplexer backend using tmux.\npub struct TmuxBackend;\n\nimpl TmuxBackend {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Default for TmuxBackend {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self::new()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl MultiplexerBackend for TmuxBackend {\n    fn is_available(&self) -> bool {\n        Command::new(\"tmux\")\n            .arg(\"-V\")\n            .output()\n            .is_ok_and(|o| o.status.success())\n    }\n\n    fn create_session(\n        &self,\n        session_name: &str,\n        cwd: &Path,\n        init_cmd: Option<&str>,\n    ) -> Result<(), BackendError> {\n        let output = Command::new(\"tmux\")\n            .args(build_new_session_args(session_name, cwd))\n            .output()\n            .map_err(|e| BackendError::NotFound {\n                multiplexer: \"tmux\".to_string(),\n                detail: e.to_string(),\n            })?;\n\n        if !output.status.success() {\n            let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();\n            return Err(BackendError::Failed {\n                multiplexer: \"tmux\".to_string(),\n                operation: \"new-session\".to_string(),\n                session: session_name.to_string(),\n                stderr,\n            });\n        }\n\n        // Send initial command if provided\n        if let Some(cmd) = init_cmd {\n            self.send_keys(session_name, cmd)?;\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    fn attach(&self, session_name: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {\n        let (cmd, op) = if self.is_inside() {\n            ([\"switch-client\", \"-t\", session_name], \"switch-client\")\n        } else {\n            ([\"attach-session\", \"-t\", session_name], \"attach-session\")\n        };\n\n        let status = Command::new(\"tmux\")\n            .args(cmd)\n            .status()\n            .map_err(|e| BackendError::NotFound {\n                multiplexer: \"tmux\".to_string(),\n                detail: e.to_string(),\n            })?;\n\n        if !status.success() {\n            return Err(BackendError::Failed {\n                multiplexer: \"tmux\".to_string(),\n                operation: op.to_string(),\n                session: session_name.to_string(),\n                stderr: format!(\"exit code: {}\", status.code().unwrap_or(-1)),\n            });\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    fn detach(&self, session_name: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {\n        tmux_simple(&[\"detach-client\", \"-s\", session_name])\n    }\n\n    fn destroy(&self, session_name: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {\n        tmux_simple(&[\"kill-session\", \"-t\", session_name])\n    }\n\n    fn is_alive(&self, session_name: &str) -> bool {\n        Command::new(\"tmux\")\n            .args([\"has-session\", \"-t\", session_name])\n            .output()\n            .is_ok_and(|o| o.status.success())\n    }\n\n    fn list_sessions(&self) -> Vec<String> {\n        let output = Command::new(\"tmux\")\n            .args([\"list-sessions\", \"-F\", \"#{session_name}\"])\n            .output();\n\n        match output {\n            Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {\n                let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);\n                stdout\n                    .lines()\n                    .filter(|line| line.starts_with(SESSION_PREFIX))\n                    .map(|s| s.to_string())\n                    .collect()\n            }\n            _ => Vec::new(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn send_keys(&self, session_name: &str, keys: &str) -> Result<(), BackendError> {\n        tmux_simple(&[\"send-keys\", \"-t\", session_name, keys, \"Enter\"])\n    }\n\n    fn is_inside(&self) -> bool {\n        std::env::var(\"TMUX\").is_ok_and(|v| !v.is_empty())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Build the arguments for `tmux new-session` (public for testing).\npub fn build_new_session_args(session_name: &str, cwd: &Path) -> Vec<String> {\n    vec![\n        \"new-session\".to_string(),\n        \"-d\".to_string(),\n        \"-s\".to_string(),\n        session_name.to_string(),\n        \"-c\".to_string(),\n        cwd.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n    ]\n}\n\nfn tmux_simple(args: &[&str]) -> Result<(), BackendError> {\n    let output = Command::new(\"tmux\")\n        .args(args)\n        .output()\n        .map_err(|e| BackendError::NotFound {\n            multiplexer: \"tmux\".to_string(),\n            detail: e.to_string(),\n        })?;\n\n    if !output.status.success() {\n        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();\n        return Err(BackendError::Failed {\n            multiplexer: \"tmux\".to_string(),\n            operation: args.first().unwrap_or(&\"unknown\").to_string(),\n            session: args\n                .iter()\n                .position(|&a| a == \"-t\" || a == \"-s\")\n                .and_then(|i| args.get(i + 1))\n                .unwrap_or(&\"unknown\")\n                .to_string(),\n            stderr,\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::path::Path;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn new_session_args_correct() {\n        let args = build_new_session_args(\n            \"dual-lightfast-main\",\n            Path::new(\"/home/user/dual-workspaces/lightfast/main\"),\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            args,\n            vec![\n                \"new-session\",\n                \"-d\",\n                \"-s\",\n                \"dual-lightfast-main\",\n                \"-c\",\n                \"/home/user/dual-workspaces/lightfast/main\",\n            ]\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn session_prefix_is_dual() {\n        assert_eq!(SESSION_PREFIX, \"dual-\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn tmux_availability_check_runs() {\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        // This test just verifies the function doesn't panic\n        let _available = backend.is_available();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn is_inside_detects_env() {\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n\n        // Save and restore TMUX env var\n        let original = std::env::var(\"TMUX\").ok();\n\n        // SAFETY: This test runs single-threaded and restores the original value.\n        unsafe {\n            // When TMUX is set to a non-empty value, we're inside tmux\n            std::env::set_var(\"TMUX\", \"/tmp/tmux-1000/default,12345,0\");\n            assert!(backend.is_inside());\n\n            // When TMUX is empty, we're not inside tmux\n            std::env::set_var(\"TMUX\", \"\");\n            assert!(!backend.is_inside());\n\n            // When TMUX is unset, we're not inside tmux\n            std::env::remove_var(\"TMUX\");\n            assert!(!backend.is_inside());\n\n            // Restore original value\n            match original {\n                Some(v) => std::env::set_var(\"TMUX\", v),\n                None => std::env::remove_var(\"TMUX\"),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn default_impl_works() {\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::default();\n        // Just verify it compiles and doesn't panic\n        let _ = backend.is_inside();\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/src/main.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Print workspace status grouped by repo.\nfn print_workspace_status(st: &state::WorkspaceState) {\n    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();\n\n    // Collect unique repo names in order of first appearance\n    let mut repos: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {\n        if !repos.contains(&ws.repo) {\n            repos.push(ws.repo.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    for repo in &repos {\n        println!(\"{repo}\");\n        for ws in st.workspaces_for_repo(repo) {\n            let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n            let session_name = tmux::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n\n            let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {\n                ws.path\n                    .as_ref()\n                    .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join(\".git\").exists())\n                    .unwrap_or(false)\n            } else {\n                clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)\n            };\n            let container_st = container::status(&container_name);\n            let tmux_alive = tmux::is_alive(&session_name);",
  "new_string": "/// Print workspace status grouped by repo.\nfn print_workspace_status(st: &state::WorkspaceState, backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) {\n    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();\n\n    // Collect unique repo names in order of first appearance\n    let mut repos: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {\n        if !repos.contains(&ws.repo) {\n            repos.push(ws.repo.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    for repo in &repos {\n        println!(\"{repo}\");\n        for ws in st.workspaces_for_repo(repo) {\n            let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n            let session_name = config::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n\n            let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {\n                ws.path\n                    .as_ref()\n                    .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join(\".git\").exists())\n                    .unwrap_or(false)\n            } else {\n                clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)\n            };\n            let container_st = container::status(&container_name);\n            let tmux_alive = backend.is_alive(&session_name);"
}

  "old_string": "// ─── Tmux Tests (require tmux) ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[test]\n#[ignore] // Requires tmux\nfn tmux_session_lifecycle() {\n    if !dual::tmux::is_available() {\n        eprintln!(\"tmux not available, skipping\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    let mut f = harness::TestFixture::new();\n    let temp = f.temp_dir();\n    let session_name = f.session_name();\n    f.register_tmux_session(session_name.clone());\n\n    // Create detached session\n    dual::tmux::create_session(&session_name, &temp, None).expect(\"create should succeed\");\n\n    // Session should be alive\n    assert!(dual::tmux::is_alive(&session_name));\n\n    // Destroy session\n    dual::tmux::destroy(&session_name).expect(\"destroy should succeed\");\n\n    // Session should no longer be alive\n    assert!(!dual::tmux::is_alive(&session_name));\n}\n\n#[test]\n#[ignore] // Requires tmux\nfn tmux_send_keys() {\n    if !dual::tmux::is_available() {\n        eprintln!(\"tmux not available, skipping\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    let mut f = harness::TestFixture::new();\n    let temp = f.temp_dir();\n    let session_name = f.session_name();\n    f.register_tmux_session(session_name.clone());\n\n    dual::tmux::create_session(&session_name, &temp, None).expect(\"create should succeed\");\n\n    // Send a command that creates a file\n    let marker = format!(\"dual-tmux-test-{}\", f.short_id);\n    dual::tmux::send_keys(&session_name, &format!(\"echo '{}' > marker.txt\", marker))\n        .expect(\"send_keys should succeed\");\n\n    // Poll for marker file with timeout (tmux send-keys is async)\n    let marker_path = temp.join(\"marker.txt\");\n    let start = std::time::Instant::now();\n    let timeout = std::time::Duration::from_secs(10);\n    while !marker_path.exists() {\n        assert!(\n            start.elapsed() < timeout,\n            \"marker file should exist (timed out after {timeout:?})\"\n        );\n        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100));\n    }\n\n    let contents = std::fs::read_to_string(&marker_path).unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(contents.trim(), marker);\n}",
  "new_string": "// ─── Tmux Tests (require tmux) ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nuse dual::backend::MultiplexerBackend;\nuse dual::tmux_backend::TmuxBackend;\n\n#[test]\n#[ignore] // Requires tmux\nfn tmux_session_lifecycle() {\n    let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n    if !backend.is_available() {\n        eprintln!(\"tmux not available, skipping\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    let mut f = harness::TestFixture::new();\n    let temp = f.temp_dir();\n    let session_name = f.session_name();\n    f.register_tmux_session(session_name.clone());\n\n    // Create detached session\n    backend\n        .create_session(&session_name, &temp, None)\n        .expect(\"create should succeed\");\n\n    // Session should be alive\n    assert!(backend.is_alive(&session_name));\n\n    // Destroy session\n    backend\n        .destroy(&session_name)\n        .expect(\"destroy should succeed\");\n\n    // Session should no longer be alive\n    assert!(!backend.is_alive(&session_name));\n}\n\n#[test]\n#[ignore] // Requires tmux\nfn tmux_send_keys() {\n    let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n    if !backend.is_available() {\n        eprintln!(\"tmux not available, skipping\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    let mut f = harness::TestFixture::new();\n    let temp = f.temp_dir();\n    let session_name = f.session_name();\n    f.register_tmux_session(session_name.clone());\n\n    backend\n        .create_session(&session_name, &temp, None)\n        .expect(\"create should succeed\");\n\n    // Send a command that creates a file\n    let marker = format!(\"dual-tmux-test-{}\", f.short_id);\n    backend\n        .send_keys(&session_name, &format!(\"echo '{}' > marker.txt\", marker))\n        .expect(\"send_keys should succeed\");\n\n    // Poll for marker file with timeout (tmux send-keys is async)\n    let marker_path = temp.join(\"marker.txt\");\n    let start = std::time::Instant::now();\n    let timeout = std::time::Duration::from_secs(10);\n    while !marker_path.exists() {\n        assert!(\n            start.elapsed() < timeout,\n            \"marker file should exist (timed out after {timeout:?})\"\n        );\n        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100));\n    }\n\n    let contents = std::fs::read_to_string(&marker_path).unwrap();\n    assert_eq!(contents.trim(), marker);\n}"
}

> AGENT

Phase 1 is complete. Here's a summary of what was done:

**Phase 1: Multiplexer Trait Extraction — Complete**

| Change | File |
|--------|------|
| Created `MultiplexerBackend` trait + `BackendError` | `src/backend.rs` (new) |
| Created `TmuxBackend` implementing the trait | `src/tmux_backend.rs` (new) |
| Moved `session_name()` alongside `container_name()` | `src/config.rs` |
| Updated module exports | `src/lib.rs` |
| Replaced all 9 `tmux::*` call sites with `backend.*` / `config::session_name()` | `src/main.rs` |
| Updated e2e tests to use `TmuxBackend` | `tests/e2e.rs` |
| Deleted old module | `src/tmux.rs` (removed) |

**Verification:**
- `cargo fmt --check` — clean
- `cargo clippy` — zero warnings
- `cargo test` — all 131 tests pass (92 lib + 19 main + 3 e2e + 7 fixture + 10 harness)
- No direct `tmux::` imports remain in `main.rs`
- Zero behavior change — pure refactor

The backend is now created once in `main()` as `TmuxBackend::new()` and passed as `&dyn MultiplexerBackend` to `cmd_default`, `cmd_launch`, `cmd_list`, `cmd_destroy`, and `print_workspace_status`. This makes it straightforward to add `ZellijBackend` or `BasicBackend` later.

Ready for your manual verification (`dual launch`, `dual destroy`, `dual list`, running from inside tmux) before proceeding to Phase 2 (TUI scaffold).
