     1→use fs2::FileExt;
     2→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
     3→use std::fs::{self, File};
     4→use std::io::Write;
     5→use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     6→use tracing::debug;
     7→
     8→use crate::config;
     9→
    10→const STATE_DIR: &str = ".dual";
    11→const STATE_FILENAME: &str = "workspaces.toml";
    12→const DEFAULT_WORKSPACE_ROOT: &str = ".dual/workspaces";
    13→
    14→#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
    15→pub struct WorkspaceState {
    16→    /// Root directory for all workspace clones (default: ~/.dual/workspaces)
    17→    pub workspace_root: Option<String>,
    18→
    19→    /// Active workspace entries
    20→    #[serde(default)]
    21→    pub workspaces: Vec<WorkspaceEntry>,
    22→}
    23→
    24→#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
    25→pub struct WorkspaceEntry {
    26→    /// Short repo name (e.g. "lightfast")
    27→    pub repo: String,
    28→
    29→    /// Git URL or local path
    30→    pub url: String,
    31→
    32→    /// Branch name (e.g. "main", "feat/auth")
    33→    pub branch: String,
    34→
    35→    /// Explicit path to workspace directory (for `dual add` — user's existing clone).
    36→    /// If None, workspace lives at {workspace_root}/{repo}/{encoded_branch}/
    37→    /// and will be cloned on first launch.
    38→    pub path: Option<String>,
    39→}
    40→
    41→impl WorkspaceState {
    42→    /// Create a new empty state.
    43→    pub fn new() -> Self {
    44→        Self {
    45→            workspace_root: None,
    46→            workspaces: Vec::new(),
    47→        }
    48→    }
    49→
    50→    /// Resolve the workspace root directory as an absolute path.
    51→    /// Uses the configured value or defaults to ~/.dual/workspaces.
    52→    pub fn workspace_root(&self) -> PathBuf {
    53→        if let Some(ref root) = self.workspace_root {
    54→            let expanded = shellexpand(root);
    55→            PathBuf::from(expanded)
    56→        } else {
    57→            dirs::home_dir()
    58→                .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
    59→                .join(DEFAULT_WORKSPACE_ROOT)
    60→        }
    61→    }
    62→
    63→    /// Get the workspace directory for an entry.
    64→    /// Uses explicit path if set, otherwise computes from workspace_root.
    65→    pub fn workspace_dir(&self, entry: &WorkspaceEntry) -> PathBuf {
    66→        if let Some(ref path) = entry.path {
    67→            PathBuf::from(shellexpand(path))
    68→        } else {
    69→            config::workspace_dir(&self.workspace_root(), &entry.repo, &entry.branch)
    70→        }
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    /// Find a workspace entry by identifier (e.g. "lightfast-main").
    74→    pub fn resolve_workspace(&self, identifier: &str) -> Option<&WorkspaceEntry> {
    75→        self.workspaces.iter().find(|ws| {
    76→            let id = config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
    77→            id == identifier
    78→        })
    79→    }
    80→
    81→    /// Get all workspace entries.
    82→    pub fn all_workspaces(&self) -> &[WorkspaceEntry] {
    83→        &self.workspaces
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    /// Add a workspace entry. Returns Err if duplicate exists.
    87→    pub fn add_workspace(&mut self, entry: WorkspaceEntry) -> Result<(), StateError> {
    88→        if self.has_workspace(&entry.repo, &entry.branch) {
    89→            return Err(StateError::DuplicateWorkspace(
    90→                entry.repo.clone(),
    91→                entry.branch.clone(),
    92→            ));
    93→        }
    94→        self.workspaces.push(entry);
    95→        Ok(())
    96→    }
    97→
    98→    /// Remove a workspace entry by repo + branch. Returns true if found.
    99→    pub fn remove_workspace(&mut self, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> bool {
   100→        let before = self.workspaces.len();
   101→        self.workspaces
   102→            .retain(|ws| !(ws.repo == repo && ws.branch == branch));
   103→        self.workspaces.len() < before
   104→    }
   105→
   106→    /// Find all workspaces for a given repo name.
   107→    pub fn workspaces_for_repo(&self, repo: &str) -> Vec<&WorkspaceEntry> {
   108→        self.workspaces
   109→            .iter()
   110→            .filter(|ws| ws.repo == repo)
   111→            .collect()
   112→    }
   113→
   114→    /// Check if a workspace entry exists for repo + branch.
   115→    pub fn has_workspace(&self, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> bool {
   116→        self.workspaces
   117→            .iter()
   118→            .any(|ws| ws.repo == repo && ws.branch == branch)
   119→    }
   120→}
   121→
   122→impl Default for WorkspaceState {
   123→    fn default() -> Self {
   124→        Self::new()
   125→    }
   126→}
   127→
   128→/// Get the state file path: ~/.dual/workspaces.toml
   129→pub fn state_path() -> Option<PathBuf> {
   130→    dirs::home_dir().map(|home| home.join(STATE_DIR).join(STATE_FILENAME))
   131→}
   132→
   133→/// Load state from default location. Returns empty state if file doesn't exist.
   134→pub fn load() -> Result<WorkspaceState, StateError> {
   135→    let path = state_path().ok_or(StateError::NoHomeDir)?;
   136→
   137→    if !path.exists() {
   138→        return Ok(WorkspaceState::new());
   139→    }
   140→
   141→    let contents =
   142→        std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|e| StateError::ReadError(path.clone(), e))?;
   143→    let state: WorkspaceState =
   144→        toml::from_str(&contents).map_err(|e| StateError::ParseError(path.clone(), e))?;
   145→    validate(&state)?;
   146→    debug!(count = state.workspaces.len(), "loaded state");
   147→    Ok(state)
   148→}
   149→
   150→/// Load state from a specific path.
   151→pub fn load_from(path: &Path) -> Result<WorkspaceState, StateError> {
   152→    if !path.exists() {
   153→        return Ok(WorkspaceState::new());
   154→    }
   155→
   156→    let contents =
   157→        std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| StateError::ReadError(path.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   158→    let state: WorkspaceState =
   159→        toml::from_str(&contents).map_err(|e| StateError::ParseError(path.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   160→    validate(&state)?;
   161→    Ok(state)
   162→}
   163→
   164→/// Save state to default location. Uses atomic write with backup.
   165→pub fn save(state: &WorkspaceState) -> Result<(), StateError> {
   166→    let path = state_path().ok_or(StateError::NoHomeDir)?;
   167→
   168→    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
   169→        fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(parent.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   170→    }
   171→
   172→    atomic_save(state, &path)
   173→}
   174→
   175→/// Save state to a specific path. Uses atomic write with backup.
   176→pub fn save_to(state: &WorkspaceState, path: &Path) -> Result<(), StateError> {
   177→    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
   178→        fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(parent.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   179→    }
   180→
   181→    atomic_save(state, path)
   182→}
   183→
   184→/// Atomic save: serialize -> write to temp file -> backup existing -> rename.
   185→///
   186→/// Uses advisory file locking to prevent concurrent writes.
   187→/// The lock is held on a lockfile for the duration of the write-backup-rename sequence.
   188→fn atomic_save(state: &WorkspaceState, path: &Path) -> Result<(), StateError> {
   189→    let contents = toml::to_string_pretty(state).map_err(StateError::SerializeError)?;
   190→
   191→    let lock_path = path.with_extension("lock");
   192→
   193→    // Acquire advisory lock
   194→    let lock_file =
   195→        File::create(&lock_path).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(lock_path.clone(), e))?;
   196→    lock_file
   197→        .lock_exclusive()
   198→        .map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(lock_path.clone(), e))?;
   199→
   200→    // Write to temp file in the same directory (same filesystem for rename)
   201→    let tmp_path = path.with_extension("tmp");
   202→    let mut tmp_file =
   203→        File::create(&tmp_path).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(tmp_path.clone(), e))?;
   204→    tmp_file
   205→        .write_all(contents.as_bytes())
   206→        .map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(tmp_path.clone(), e))?;
   207→    tmp_file
   208→        .sync_all()
   209→        .map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(tmp_path.clone(), e))?;
   210→
   211→    // Backup existing file (best-effort — don't fail if original doesn't exist)
   212→    let bak_path = path.with_extension("toml.bak");
   213→    if path.exists() {
   214→        let _ = fs::copy(path, &bak_path);
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    // Atomic rename (POSIX guarantees this is atomic on same filesystem)
   218→    fs::rename(&tmp_path, path).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(path.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   219→
   220→    // Release lock (dropped with file, but explicit for clarity)
   221→    let _ = lock_file.unlock();
   222→    let _ = fs::remove_file(&lock_path);
   223→
   224→    Ok(())
   225→}
   226→
   227→/// Parse state from TOML string (for testing).
   228→pub fn parse(toml_str: &str) -> Result<WorkspaceState, StateError> {
   229→    let state: WorkspaceState = toml::from_str(toml_str)
   230→        .map_err(|e| StateError::ParseError(PathBuf::from("<string>"), e))?;
   231→    validate(&state)?;
   232→    Ok(state)
   233→}
   234→
   235→fn validate(state: &WorkspaceState) -> Result<(), StateError> {
   236→    for (i, ws) in state.workspaces.iter().enumerate() {
   237→        if ws.repo.is_empty() {
   238→            return Err(StateError::Validation(format!(
   239→                "workspaces[{i}]: 'repo' cannot be empty"
   240→            )));
   241→        }
   242→        if ws.url.is_empty() {
   243→            return Err(StateError::Validation(format!(
   244→                "workspaces[{i}] ({}): 'url' cannot be empty",
   245→                ws.repo
   246→            )));
   247→        }
   248→        if ws.branch.is_empty() {
   249→            return Err(StateError::Validation(format!(
   250→                "workspaces[{i}] ({}): 'branch' cannot be empty",
   251→                ws.repo
   252→            )));
   253→        }
   254→    }
   255→    Ok(())
   256→}
   257→
   258→/// Minimal ~ expansion for workspace_root paths.
   259→fn shellexpand(path: &str) -> String {
   260→    if let Some(rest) = path.strip_prefix("~/")
   261→        && let Some(home) = dirs::home_dir()
   262→    {
   263→        return home.join(rest).to_string_lossy().into_owned();
   264→    }
   265→    path.to_string()
   266→}
   267→
   268→#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   269→pub enum StateError {
   270→    #[error("Could not determine home directory")]
   271→    NoHomeDir,
   272→
   273→    #[error("Failed to read {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   274→    ReadError(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   275→
   276→    #[error("Failed to write {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   277→    WriteError(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   278→
   279→    #[error("Failed to parse {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   280→    ParseError(PathBuf, toml::de::Error),
   281→
   282→    #[error("Failed to serialize state: {0}")]
   283→    SerializeError(toml::ser::Error),
   284→
   285→    #[error("Invalid state: {0}")]
   286→    Validation(String),
   287→
   288→    #[error("Workspace {0}/{1} already exists")]
   289→    DuplicateWorkspace(String, String),
   290→}

   310→    #[test]
   311→    fn parse_state_with_workspaces() {
   312→        let toml = r#"
   313→workspace_root = "~/my-workspaces"
   314→
   315→[[workspaces]]
   316→repo = "lightfast"
   317→url = "git@github.com:org/lightfast.git"
   318→branch = "main"
   319→path = "/Users/dev/code/lightfast"
   320→
   321→[[workspaces]]
   322→repo = "lightfast"
   323→url = "git@github.com:org/lightfast.git"
   324→branch = "feat/auth"
   325→
   326→[[workspaces]]
   327→repo = "agent-os"
   328→url = "/local/path/to/agent-os"
   329→branch = "main"
   330→path = "/local/path/to/agent-os"
   331→"#;
   332→        let state = parse(toml).unwrap();
   333→        assert_eq!(state.workspace_root.as_deref(), Some("~/my-workspaces"));
   334→        assert_eq!(state.workspaces.len(), 3);

   336→        assert_eq!(state.workspaces[0].branch, "main");

  "content": "use crossterm::event::{self, Event, KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyEventKind};\n\nuse super::app::App;\n\npub enum Action {\n    Quit,\n    Select(String), // workspace_id\n}\n\npub fn handle_events(app: &mut App) -> Result<Option<Action>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    if event::poll(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100))? {\n        if let Event::Key(key) = event::read()? {\n            // Only handle key press events (ignore release/repeat)\n            if key.kind == KeyEventKind::Press {\n                return Ok(handle_key(app, key));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(None)\n}\n\nfn handle_key(app: &mut App, key: KeyEvent) -> Option<Action> {\n    match key.code {\n        KeyCode::Char('q') | KeyCode::Esc => Some(Action::Quit),\n        KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => {\n            app.move_up();\n            None\n        }\n        KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => {\n            app.move_down();\n            None\n        }\n        KeyCode::Enter => {\n            if let Some(workspace_id) = app.selected_workspace_id() {\n                Some(Action::Select(workspace_id))\n            } else {\n                // Selected a repo header — toggle expand/collapse\n                app.toggle_expand();\n                None\n            }\n        }\n        _ => None,\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyEventKind, KeyEventState, KeyModifiers};\n\n    fn key(code: KeyCode) -> KeyEvent {\n        KeyEvent {\n            code,\n            modifiers: KeyModifiers::NONE,\n            kind: KeyEventKind::Press,\n            state: KeyEventState::NONE,\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn make_app() -> App {\n        use crate::backend::MultiplexerBackend;\n        use crate::state::{WorkspaceEntry, WorkspaceState};\n        use crate::tmux_backend::TmuxBackend;\n\n        let mut state = WorkspaceState::new();\n        state\n            .add_workspace(WorkspaceEntry {\n                repo: \"lightfast\".into(),\n                url: \"url\".into(),\n                branch: \"main\".into(),\n                path: None,\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n        state\n            .add_workspace(WorkspaceEntry {\n                repo: \"lightfast\".into(),\n                url: \"url\".into(),\n                branch: \"feat/auth\".into(),\n                path: None,\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        App::new(&state, &backend)\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn quit_on_q() {\n        let mut app = make_app();\n        let action = handle_key(&mut app, key(KeyCode::Char('q')));\n        assert!(matches!(action, Some(Action::Quit)));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn quit_on_esc() {\n        let mut app = make_app();\n        let action = handle_key(&mut app, key(KeyCode::Esc));\n        assert!(matches!(action, Some(Action::Quit)));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn navigate_down() {\n        let mut app = make_app();\n        let initial = app.selected();\n        handle_key(&mut app, key(KeyCode::Down));\n        assert_eq!(app.selected(), initial + 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn navigate_up_at_top() {\n        let mut app = make_app();\n        handle_key(&mut app, key(KeyCode::Up));\n        assert_eq!(app.selected(), 0);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn enter_on_repo_header_toggles() {\n        let mut app = make_app();\n        // First item should be a repo header\n        let action = handle_key(&mut app, key(KeyCode::Enter));\n        assert!(action.is_none()); // toggle, not select\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn j_and_k_navigate() {\n        let mut app = make_app();\n        handle_key(&mut app, key(KeyCode::Char('j')));\n        assert_eq!(app.selected(), 1);\n        handle_key(&mut app, key(KeyCode::Char('k')));\n        assert_eq!(app.selected(), 0);\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/src/tui/app.rs",
  "content": "use crate::backend::MultiplexerBackend;\nuse crate::clone;\nuse crate::config;\nuse crate::container;\nuse crate::state::WorkspaceState;\n\nuse super::event;\nuse super::ui;\n\n/// A single item in the flattened display list.\npub struct DisplayItem {\n    /// Text to show\n    pub display: String,\n    /// Workspace status (None = repo header)\n    pub status: Option<WorkspaceStatus>,\n    /// Workspace id if this is a branch item\n    pub workspace_id: Option<String>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy)]\npub enum WorkspaceStatus {\n    /// tmux session alive\n    Running,\n    /// Container stopped, no tmux\n    Stopped,\n    /// Not cloned yet\n    Lazy,\n}\n\nstruct RepoGroup {\n    name: String,\n    workspaces: Vec<WorkspaceItem>,\n    expanded: bool,\n}\n\nstruct WorkspaceItem {\n    branch: String,\n    workspace_id: String,\n    status: WorkspaceStatus,\n}\n\npub struct App {\n    repos: Vec<RepoGroup>,\n    selected: usize,\n    item_count: usize,\n}\n\nimpl App {\n    /// Build app state from workspace state and live backend status.\n    pub fn new(state: &WorkspaceState, backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> Self {\n        let workspace_root = state.workspace_root();\n\n        // Group workspaces by repo (preserve insertion order)\n        let mut repo_names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n        for ws in state.all_workspaces() {\n            if !repo_names.contains(&ws.repo) {\n                repo_names.push(ws.repo.clone());\n            }\n        }\n\n        let repos: Vec<RepoGroup> = repo_names\n            .iter()\n            .map(|repo| {\n                let workspaces = state\n                    .workspaces_for_repo(repo)\n                    .into_iter()\n                    .map(|ws| {\n                        let session_name = config::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n                        let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n                        let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {\n                            ws.path\n                                .as_ref()\n                                .is_some_and(|p| std::path::PathBuf::from(p).join(\".git\").exists())\n                        } else {\n                            clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)\n                        };\n\n                        let status = if backend.is_alive(&session_name) {\n                            WorkspaceStatus::Running\n                        } else if matches!(\n                            container::status(&container_name),\n                            container::ContainerStatus::Stopped\n                        ) || clone_exists\n                        {\n                            WorkspaceStatus::Stopped\n                        } else {\n                            WorkspaceStatus::Lazy\n                        };\n\n                        WorkspaceItem {\n                            branch: ws.branch.clone(),\n                            workspace_id: config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch),\n                            status,\n                        }\n                    })\n                    .collect();\n\n                RepoGroup {\n                    name: repo.clone(),\n                    workspaces,\n                    expanded: true,\n                }\n            })\n            .collect();\n\n        let item_count = Self::count_items(&repos);\n        App {\n            repos,\n            selected: 0,\n            item_count,\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn count_items(repos: &[RepoGroup]) -> usize {\n        repos\n            .iter()\n            .map(|r| 1 + if r.expanded { r.workspaces.len() } else { 0 })\n            .sum()\n    }\n\n    /// Flatten repos into display items for rendering.\n    pub fn flatten_items(&self) -> Vec<DisplayItem> {\n        let mut items = Vec::new();\n        for repo in &self.repos {\n            let chevron = if repo.expanded { \"▼\" } else { \"▶\" };\n            items.push(DisplayItem {\n                display: format!(\"{chevron} {}\", repo.name),\n                status: None,\n                workspace_id: None,\n            });\n            if repo.expanded {\n                for ws in &repo.workspaces {\n                    let icon = match ws.status {\n                        WorkspaceStatus::Running => \"●\",\n                        WorkspaceStatus::Stopped => \"○\",\n                        WorkspaceStatus::Lazy => \"◌\",\n                    };\n                    let label = match ws.status {\n                        WorkspaceStatus::Running => \"running\",\n                        WorkspaceStatus::Stopped => \"stopped\",\n                        WorkspaceStatus::Lazy => \"lazy\",\n                    };\n                    let branch_display =\n                        config::decode_branch(&config::encode_branch(&ws.branch));\n                    items.push(DisplayItem {\n                        display: format!(\"  {branch_display:<24} {icon} {label}\"),\n                        status: Some(ws.status),\n                        workspace_id: Some(ws.workspace_id.clone()),\n                    });\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        items\n    }\n\n    pub fn selected(&self) -> usize {\n        self.selected\n    }\n\n    pub fn move_down(&mut self) {\n        if self.selected + 1 < self.item_count {\n            self.selected += 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn move_up(&mut self) {\n        self.selected = self.selected.saturating_sub(1);\n    }\n\n    /// Get the workspace_id of the currently selected item, or None if it's a repo header.\n    pub fn selected_workspace_id(&self) -> Option<String> {\n        let items = self.flatten_items();\n        items\n            .get(self.selected)\n            .and_then(|item| item.workspace_id.clone())\n    }\n\n    /// Toggle expand/collapse of the repo group at the current selection.\n    pub fn toggle_expand(&mut self) {\n        // Find which repo group this index belongs to\n        let mut idx = 0;\n        for repo in &mut self.repos {\n            if idx == self.selected {\n                repo.expanded = !repo.expanded;\n                self.item_count = Self::count_items_from_repos(&self.repos);\n                // Clamp selected if it now exceeds item count\n                if self.selected >= self.item_count {\n                    self.selected = self.item_count.saturating_sub(1);\n                }\n                return;\n            }\n            idx += 1;\n            if repo.expanded {\n                idx += repo.workspaces.len();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn count_items_from_repos(repos: &[RepoGroup]) -> usize {\n        Self::count_items(repos)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Main TUI entry point.\n///\n/// Returns `Ok(Some(workspace_id))` if user selected a workspace to launch.\n/// Returns `Ok(None)` if user quit.\npub fn run(\n    state: &WorkspaceState,\n    backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend,\n) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let mut terminal = ratatui::init();\n    let mut app = App::new(state, backend);\n\n    let result = loop {\n        terminal.draw(|frame| ui::render(frame, &app))?;\n\n        if let Some(action) = event::handle_events(&mut app)? {\n            match action {\n                event::Action::Quit => break Ok(None),\n                event::Action::Select(workspace_id) => {\n                    break Ok(Some(workspace_id));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    };\n\n    ratatui::restore();\n    result\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::state::{WorkspaceEntry, WorkspaceState};\n    use crate::tmux_backend::TmuxBackend;\n\n    fn test_state() -> WorkspaceState {\n        let mut state = WorkspaceState::new();\n        state\n            .add_workspace(WorkspaceEntry {\n                repo: \"lightfast\".into(),\n                url: \"url\".into(),\n                branch: \"main\".into(),\n                path: None,\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n        state\n            .add_workspace(WorkspaceEntry {\n                repo: \"lightfast\".into(),\n                url: \"url\".into(),\n                branch: \"feat/auth\".into(),\n                path: None,\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n        state\n            .add_workspace(WorkspaceEntry {\n                repo: \"agent-os\".into(),\n                url: \"url\".into(),\n                branch: \"main\".into(),\n                path: None,\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n        state\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn app_groups_by_repo() {\n        let state = test_state();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n\n        assert_eq!(app.repos.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(app.repos[0].name, \"lightfast\");\n        assert_eq!(app.repos[0].workspaces.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(app.repos[1].name, \"agent-os\");\n        assert_eq!(app.repos[1].workspaces.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn flatten_items_expanded() {\n        let state = test_state();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n        let items = app.flatten_items();\n\n        // 2 repo headers + 3 workspaces = 5\n        assert_eq!(items.len(), 5);\n        assert!(items[0].status.is_none()); // repo header\n        assert!(items[0].display.contains(\"lightfast\"));\n        assert!(items[1].status.is_some()); // workspace\n        assert!(items[1].display.contains(\"main\"));\n        assert!(items[3].status.is_none()); // repo header\n        assert!(items[3].display.contains(\"agent-os\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn navigation_bounds() {\n        let state = test_state();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let mut app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n\n        assert_eq!(app.selected(), 0);\n        app.move_up(); // should stay at 0\n        assert_eq!(app.selected(), 0);\n\n        // Move to the end\n        for _ in 0..10 {\n            app.move_down();\n        }\n        assert_eq!(app.selected(), 4); // 5 items, max index = 4\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn selected_workspace_id_returns_none_for_header() {\n        let state = test_state();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n        // First item is a repo header\n        assert!(app.selected_workspace_id().is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn selected_workspace_id_returns_id_for_branch() {\n        let state = test_state();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let mut app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n        app.move_down(); // move to first workspace\n        assert_eq!(\n            app.selected_workspace_id(),\n            Some(\"lightfast-main\".to_string())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn toggle_collapse_reduces_items() {\n        let state = test_state();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let mut app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n\n        assert_eq!(app.item_count, 5);\n        app.toggle_expand(); // collapse \"lightfast\"\n        assert_eq!(app.item_count, 3); // 2 headers + 1 workspace (agent-os/main)\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn toggle_expand_restores_items() {\n        let state = test_state();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let mut app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n\n        app.toggle_expand(); // collapse\n        assert_eq!(app.item_count, 3);\n        app.toggle_expand(); // expand again\n        assert_eq!(app.item_count, 5);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn empty_state_produces_no_items() {\n        let state = WorkspaceState::new();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n        assert_eq!(app.flatten_items().len(), 0);\n        assert_eq!(app.item_count, 0);\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Layout};\nuse ratatui::style::{Color, Modifier, Style};\nuse ratatui::text::{Line, Span};\nuse ratatui::widgets::{Block, Borders, List, ListItem, ListState, Paragraph};\nuse ratatui::Frame;\n\nuse super::app::{App, WorkspaceStatus};\n\npub fn render(frame: &mut Frame, app: &App) {\n    let area = frame.area();\n\n    let chunks = Layout::vertical([\n        Constraint::Length(1), // header\n        Constraint::Min(3),   // workspace list\n        Constraint::Length(1), // footer\n    ])\n    .split(area);\n\n    // Header\n    let header = Paragraph::new(Line::from(vec![\n        Span::styled(\" dual\", Style::default().add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)),\n        Span::raw(\"  workspace browser\"),\n    ]));\n    frame.render_widget(header, chunks[0]);\n\n    // Workspace list\n    let block = Block::default()\n        .borders(Borders::ALL)\n        .border_style(Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray));\n\n    let items = app.flatten_items();\n\n    if items.is_empty() {\n        let empty = Paragraph::new(\"  No workspaces. Run `dual add` in a repo to get started.\")\n            .block(block);\n        frame.render_widget(empty, chunks[1]);\n    } else {\n        let list_items: Vec<ListItem> = items\n            .iter()\n            .map(|item| {\n                let style = match item.status {\n                    Some(WorkspaceStatus::Running) => Style::default().fg(Color::Green),\n                    Some(WorkspaceStatus::Stopped) => Style::default().fg(Color::Yellow),\n                    Some(WorkspaceStatus::Lazy) => Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray),\n                    None => Style::default().add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD), // repo header\n                };\n                ListItem::new(Line::from(Span::styled(&item.display, style)))\n            })\n            .collect();\n\n        let list = List::new(list_items)\n            .block(block)\n            .highlight_style(\n                Style::default()\n                    .add_modifier(Modifier::REVERSED)\n                    .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),\n            )\n            .highlight_symbol(\"▸ \");\n\n        let mut list_state = ListState::default();\n        list_state.select(Some(app.selected()));\n\n        frame.render_stateful_widget(list, chunks[1], &mut list_state);\n    }\n\n    // Footer keybindings\n    let footer = Paragraph::new(Line::from(vec![\n        Span::styled(\" j/k\", Style::default().add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)),\n        Span::raw(\" navigate  \"),\n        Span::styled(\"enter\", Style::default().add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)),\n        Span::raw(\" launch  \"),\n        Span::styled(\"q\", Style::default().add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)),\n        Span::raw(\" quit\"),\n    ]))\n    .style(Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray));\n    frame.render_widget(footer, chunks[2]);\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::state::{WorkspaceEntry, WorkspaceState};\n    use crate::tmux_backend::TmuxBackend;\n    use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;\n    use ratatui::Terminal;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn render_does_not_panic_empty() {\n        let state = WorkspaceState::new();\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n\n        let test_backend = TestBackend::new(80, 24);\n        let mut terminal = Terminal::new(test_backend).unwrap();\n        terminal.draw(|frame| render(frame, &app)).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn render_does_not_panic_with_workspaces() {\n        let mut state = WorkspaceState::new();\n        state\n            .add_workspace(WorkspaceEntry {\n                repo: \"lightfast\".into(),\n                url: \"url\".into(),\n                branch: \"main\".into(),\n                path: None,\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n\n        let test_backend = TestBackend::new(80, 24);\n        let mut terminal = Terminal::new(test_backend).unwrap();\n        terminal.draw(|frame| render(frame, &app)).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn render_does_not_panic_small_terminal() {\n        let mut state = WorkspaceState::new();\n        state\n            .add_workspace(WorkspaceEntry {\n                repo: \"lightfast\".into(),\n                url: \"url\".into(),\n                branch: \"main\".into(),\n                path: None,\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n\n        let backend = TmuxBackend::new();\n        let app = App::new(&state, &backend);\n\n        let test_backend = TestBackend::new(20, 5);\n        let mut terminal = Terminal::new(test_backend).unwrap();\n        terminal.draw(|frame| render(frame, &app)).unwrap();\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "/// Default (no subcommand): show workspace list with launch hint.\nfn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {\n    let st = match state::load() {\n        Ok(s) => s,\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"{e}\");\n            info!(\"Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    };\n\n    let workspaces = st.all_workspaces();\n    if workspaces.is_empty() {\n        info!(\"No workspaces. Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.\");\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    println!(\"Workspaces:\\n\");\n    print_workspace_status(&st, backend);\n    println!(\"Use `dual launch <workspace>` to start a workspace.\");\n    println!(\"Use `dual add` to register a new repo.\");\n    0\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Default (no subcommand): launch TUI workspace browser.\nfn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {\n    let st = match state::load() {\n        Ok(s) => s,\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"{e}\");\n            info!(\"Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    };\n\n    if st.all_workspaces().is_empty() {\n        info!(\"No workspaces. Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.\");\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    match tui::run(&st, backend) {\n        Ok(Some(workspace_id)) => {\n            // User selected a workspace — launch it\n            cmd_launch(Some(&workspace_id), backend)\n        }\n        Ok(None) => 0, // User quit\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"TUI error: {e}\");\n            1\n        }\n    }\n}"
}

**Branch**: `feature/dual-56-tui-ratatui-based-workspace-browser`

1. **PR #135** — MultiplexerBackend trait extraction (DUAL-55) → `main`

**Completed phases:**
- Phase 1: MultiplexerBackend trait (tmux abstracted behind `dyn MultiplexerBackend`)
- Phase 2: TUI scaffold (ratatui workspace browser with navigation, expand/collapse, status colors)

**Unrelated local changes**: `.claude/settings.json`, `.dual.toml`, `.DS_Store` — not part of PRs.

## What's Next

**Immediate — Manual verification needed:**
1. Merge PR #135 first, then PR #136
2. Test `dual` (TUI launches), `dual launch`, `dual destroy`, `dual list`
3. Verify navigation (j/k), expand/collapse (enter on repo header), launch (enter on workspace), quit (q)

**Then — Phase 3: TUI ↔ Tmux Transition (DUAL-57)**
- Suspend TUI → launch tmux session → resume TUI on detach
- Panic hook for terminal state restoration
- Inside-tmux handling (switch-client instead of attach)

This is the final phase of the v3.0.0 plan. Want to proceed with merging the PRs, or start Phase 3 on a new branch?

     1→# v3.0.0 — Multiplexer Trait + TUI Implementation Plan
     2→
     3→## Overview
     4→
     5→Extract the tmux module behind a `MultiplexerBackend` trait, then build a ratatui-based TUI as the primary interface. `dual` (no args) launches the TUI. Selecting a workspace suspends the TUI, attaches the tmux session, and resumes the TUI when the user detaches.
     6→
     7→## Current State Analysis
     8→
     9→**Tmux integration** is 8 public functions in `src/tmux.rs` called directly from `src/main.rs` at 9 call sites. No abstraction layer exists.
    10→
    11→**CLI-only interface** — all output via `tracing::info!()` with Unicode status symbols. No TUI dependencies.
    12→
    13→### Key Discoveries:
    14→- `src/tmux.rs` — 8 functions: `is_available`, `create_session`, `attach`, `detach`, `destroy`, `is_alive`, `list_sessions`, `send_keys`, `session_name`
    15→- `src/main.rs` — 9 tmux call sites across `cmd_launch()`, `cmd_destroy()`, `print_workspace_status()`
    16→- `src/cli.rs:11` — `command: Option<Command>` already handles `dual` (no args) via `cmd_default()`
    17→- SPEC.md:269-294 — Backend trait contract with `create_session`, `attach`, `detach`, `destroy`, `is_alive`, `list_sessions`
    18→- ratatui v0.30.0 — uses `ratatui::init()` / `ratatui::restore()` for terminal state management
    19→- The TUI → tmux → TUI transition pattern: `restore()` → `tmux attach` (blocks) → tmux detach returns control → `init()` → resume TUI
    20→
    21→### The Critical Design Decision: TUI ↔ Tmux Transition
    22→
    23→**Model B: Dual manages terminal directly** (chosen)
    24→
    25→```
    26→Terminal
    27→├── State A: Dual TUI (alternate screen, raw mode)
    28→│   └── User selects workspace → ratatui::restore() → tmux attach
    29→└── State B: tmux session (tmux controls terminal)
    30→    └── User detaches (Ctrl+b d) → tmux returns → ratatui::init() → back to State A
    31→```
    32→
    33→Why Model B:
    34→- No nested tmux (Model A's problem)
    35→- No terminal emulator reimplementation (Model C's problem)
    36→- Uses ratatui's built-in `init()`/`restore()` for clean state transitions
    37→- `tmux attach-session` is a blocking call — when user detaches, it returns, and we resume
    38→
    39→## Desired End State
    40→
    41→After v3.0.0:
    42→1. `dual` → launches TUI with tree view of repos/branches
    43→2. Arrow keys/j/k to navigate, Enter to select
    44→3. Selecting a workspace: suspends TUI → launches/attaches tmux session
    45→4. Detaching from tmux (Ctrl+b d) → TUI resumes automatically
    46→5. `q` in TUI → exits Dual
    47→6. `dual list` → non-interactive fallback (existing behavior)
    48→7. All `main.rs` code uses `MultiplexerBackend` trait instead of `tmux::*` directly
    49→
    50→## What We're NOT Doing
```

   330→**File**: `src/tui/ui.rs` (new)
   331→
   332→```rust
   333→use ratatui::Frame;
   334→use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Layout};
   335→use ratatui::style::{Color, Modifier, Style};
   336→use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
   337→use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Borders, List, ListItem, ListState};
   338→
   339→use super::app::{App, WorkspaceStatus};
   340→
   341→pub fn render(frame: &mut Frame, app: &App) {
   342→    let area = frame.area();
   343→
   344→    // Title block
   345→    let block = Block::default()
   346→        .title(" dual ")
   347→        .borders(Borders::ALL);
   348→
   349→    // Build list items from repo groups
   350→    let items: Vec<ListItem> = app.flatten_items()
   351→        .into_iter()
   352→        .map(|item| {
   353→            let style = match item.status {
   354→                Some(WorkspaceStatus::Attached) => Style::default().fg(Color::Green),
   355→                Some(WorkspaceStatus::Running) => Style::default().fg(Color::Blue),
   356→                Some(WorkspaceStatus::Stopped) => Style::default().fg(Color::Yellow),
   357→                Some(WorkspaceStatus::Lazy) => Style::default().fg(Color::DarkGray),
   358→                None => Style::default().add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD), // repo header
   359→            };
   360→            ListItem::new(Line::from(item.display)).style(style)
   361→        })
   362→        .collect();
   363→
   364→    let list = List::new(items)
   365→        .block(block)
   366→        .highlight_style(Style::default().add_modifier(Modifier::REVERSED));
   367→
   368→    let mut state = ListState::default();
   369→    state.select(Some(app.selected));
   370→
   371→    frame.render_stateful_widget(list, area, &mut state);
   372→}
   373→```
   374→
   375→#### 5. Create `src/tui/event.rs` — Event Handling
   376→**File**: `src/tui/event.rs` (new)
   377→
   378→```rust
   379→use crossterm::event::{self, Event, KeyCode, KeyEvent};
   380→use super::app::App;
   381→
   382→pub enum Action {
   383→    Quit,
   384→    Select(String), // workspace_id
   385→}
   386→
   387→pub fn handle_events(app: &mut App) -> Result<Option<Action>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   388→    if event::poll(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100))? {
   389→        if let Event::Key(key) = event::read()? {
   390→            return Ok(handle_key(app, key));
   391→        }
   392→    }
   393→    Ok(None)
   394→}
   395→
   396→fn handle_key(app: &mut App, key: KeyEvent) -> Option<Action> {
   397→    match key.code {
   398→        KeyCode::Char('q') | KeyCode::Esc => Some(Action::Quit),
   399→        KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => {
   400→            app.move_up();
   401→            None
   402→        }
   403→        KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => {
   404→            app.move_down();
   405→            None
   406→        }
   407→        KeyCode::Enter => {
   408→            if let Some(workspace_id) = app.selected_workspace_id() {
   409→                Some(Action::Select(workspace_id))
   410→            } else {
   411→                // Selected a repo header — toggle expand/collapse
   412→                app.toggle_expand();
   413→                None
   414→            }
   415→        }
   416→        _ => None,
   417→    }
   418→}
   419→```
   420→
   421→#### 6. Update `src/main.rs` — Wire TUI as Default
   422→**File**: `src/main.rs`
   423→
   424→Change `cmd_default()` to launch TUI:
   425→
   426→```rust
   427→fn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {
   428→    let state = match state::load() {
   429→        Ok(s) => s,
   430→        Err(e) => {
   431→            error!("Failed to load state: {e}");
   432→            return 1;
   433→        }
   434→    };
   435→
   436→    if state.all_workspaces().is_empty() {
   437→        info!("No workspaces configured. Run `dual add` in a repo to get started.");
   438→        return 0;
   439→    }
   440→
   441→    match tui::run(&state, backend) {
   442→        Ok(Some(workspace_id)) => {
   443→            // User selected a workspace — launch it
   444→            cmd_launch_by_id(&workspace_id, backend)
   445→        }
   446→        Ok(None) => 0, // User quit
   447→        Err(e) => {
   448→            error!("TUI error: {e}");
   449→            1
   450→        }
   451→    }
   452→}
   453→```
   454→
   455→#### 7. Update `src/lib.rs` — Export TUI Module
   456→```rust
   457→pub mod tui;
   458→```
   459→
   460→### Success Criteria:
   461→
   462→#### Automated Verification:
   463→- [ ] `cargo build` — compiles with new dependencies
   464→- [ ] `cargo test` — all existing tests pass
   465→- [ ] `cargo clippy` — no warnings
   466→- [ ] TUI module unit tests pass (app state, navigation, event handling)
   467→
   468→#### Manual Verification:
   469→- [ ] `dual` shows a TUI with repos and branches listed
   470→- [ ] Arrow keys / j/k navigate the list
   471→- [ ] Enter on a repo header toggles expand/collapse
   472→- [ ] Enter on a branch shows workspace status indicators
   473→- [ ] `q` exits cleanly (terminal restored properly)
   474→- [ ] `dual list` still works as non-interactive output
   475→
   476→**Implementation Note**: After completing this phase and all verification passes, pause here for manual confirmation before proceeding to Phase 3.
   477→
   478→---
   479→
   480→## Phase 3: TUI ↔ Tmux Transition
   481→
   482→### Overview
   483→Wire the TUI selection to the full launch pipeline. When user selects a workspace: suspend TUI → launch workspace (clone/container/session if needed) → attach tmux session → when user detaches, resume TUI.
   484→
   485→### Changes Required:
   486→
   487→#### 1. Implement the Suspend → Launch → Resume Loop
   488→**File**: `src/main.rs`
   489→
   490→The key insight: `tmux attach-session` is a **blocking call**. It takes over the terminal and only returns when the user detaches (`Ctrl+b d`). This makes the suspend/resume pattern straightforward:
   491→
   492→```rust
   493→fn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {
   494→    let mut state = match state::load() { ... };
   495→
   496→    loop {
   497→        // Reload state each iteration (workspaces may have changed)
   498→        state = match state::load() {
   499→            Ok(s) => s,
   500→            Err(e) => { error!("{e}"); return 1; }
   501→        };
   502→
   503→        match tui::run(&state, backend) {
   504→            Ok(Some(workspace_id)) => {
   505→                // TUI already called ratatui::restore() before returning
   506→                // Terminal is now in normal mode — tmux can take over
   507→
   508→                // Run the full launch pipeline
   509→                let exit_code = cmd_launch_by_id(&workspace_id, backend);
   510→
   511→                if exit_code != 0 {
   512→                    // Launch failed — show error, re-enter TUI
   513→                    eprintln!("Launch failed (exit code {exit_code}). Press Enter to continue...");
   514→                    let _ = std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut String::new());
   515→                }
   516→
   517→                // When tmux detach returns (or launch failed), loop back to TUI
   518→                // ratatui::init() is called inside tui::run() at the top
   519→                continue;
   520→            }
   521→            Ok(None) => return 0, // User quit
   522→            Err(e) => {
   523→                error!("TUI error: {e}");
   524→                return 1;
   525→            }
   526→        }
   527→    }
   528→}
   529→```
```

   480→## Phase 3: TUI ↔ Tmux Transition
   481→
   482→### Overview
   483→Wire the TUI selection to the full launch pipeline. When user selects a workspace: suspend TUI → launch workspace (clone/container/session if needed) → attach tmux session → when user detaches, resume TUI.
   484→
   485→### Changes Required:
   486→
   487→#### 1. Implement the Suspend → Launch → Resume Loop
   488→**File**: `src/main.rs`
   489→
   490→The key insight: `tmux attach-session` is a **blocking call**. It takes over the terminal and only returns when the user detaches (`Ctrl+b d`). This makes the suspend/resume pattern straightforward:
   491→
   492→```rust
   493→fn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {
   494→    let mut state = match state::load() { ... };
   495→
   496→    loop {
   497→        // Reload state each iteration (workspaces may have changed)
   498→        state = match state::load() {
   499→            Ok(s) => s,
   500→            Err(e) => { error!("{e}"); return 1; }
   501→        };
   502→
   503→        match tui::run(&state, backend) {
   504→            Ok(Some(workspace_id)) => {
   505→                // TUI already called ratatui::restore() before returning
   506→                // Terminal is now in normal mode — tmux can take over
   507→
   508→                // Run the full launch pipeline
   509→                let exit_code = cmd_launch_by_id(&workspace_id, backend);
   510→
   511→                if exit_code != 0 {
   512→                    // Launch failed — show error, re-enter TUI
   513→                    eprintln!("Launch failed (exit code {exit_code}). Press Enter to continue...");
   514→                    let _ = std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut String::new());
   515→                }
   516→
   517→                // When tmux detach returns (or launch failed), loop back to TUI
   518→                // ratatui::init() is called inside tui::run() at the top
   519→                continue;
   520→            }
   521→            Ok(None) => return 0, // User quit
   522→            Err(e) => {
   523→                error!("TUI error: {e}");
   524→                return 1;
   525→            }
   526→        }
   527→    }
   528→}
   529→```
   530→
   531→#### 2. Update `tui::run()` — Handle Re-entry
   532→**File**: `src/tui/app.rs`
   533→
   534→The TUI needs to handle being called multiple times in a loop. Each call:
   535→1. `ratatui::init()` — enter alternate screen
   536→2. Build fresh app state from workspace state
   537→3. Run event loop
   538→4. On selection: `ratatui::restore()` — leave alternate screen
   539→5. Return selected workspace
   540→
   541→```rust
   542→pub fn run(
   543→    state: &WorkspaceState,
   544→    backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend,
   545→) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   546→    let mut terminal = ratatui::init();
   547→
   548→    // Build fresh state each time (may have changed since last TUI session)
   549→    let mut app = App::new(state, backend);
   550→
   551→    let result = loop {
   552→        terminal.draw(|frame| ui::render(frame, &app))?;
   553→
   554→        if let Some(action) = event::handle_events(&mut app)? {
   555→            match action {
   556→                event::Action::Quit => break Ok(None),
   557→                event::Action::Select(workspace_id) => {
   558→                    break Ok(Some(workspace_id));
   559→                }
   560→            }
   561→        }
   562→    };
   563→
   564→    ratatui::restore();
   565→    result
   566→}
   567→```
   568→
   569→#### 3. Ensure Proper Terminal Restoration on Panic
   570→**File**: `src/main.rs`
   571→
   572→Add a panic hook to restore terminal state:
   573→
   574→```rust
   575→fn main() {
   576→    // Install panic hook BEFORE entering TUI
   577→    let original_hook = std::panic::take_hook();
   578→    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |panic_info| {
   579→        // Best-effort terminal restoration
   580→        let _ = crossterm::execute!(
   581→            std::io::stderr(),
   582→            crossterm::terminal::LeaveAlternateScreen
   583→        );
   584→        let _ = crossterm::terminal::disable_raw_mode();
   585→        original_hook(panic_info);
   586→    }));
   587→
   588→    // ... rest of main
   589→}
   590→```
   591→
   592→#### 4. Handle "Already Inside Tmux" Case in TUI
   593→**File**: `src/tui/app.rs`
   594→
   595→If the user launches `dual` from inside a tmux session, the TUI still works. When they select a workspace, `backend.attach()` uses `switch-client` (from Phase 1). But after switching, the TUI process is still running in the original tmux session's pane.
   596→
   597→Two options:
   598→- **Option A**: Exit TUI after switch-client (user returns to TUI by running `dual` again)
   599→- **Option B**: Keep TUI running, user switches back to it via tmux
   600→
   601→We choose **Option A** for simplicity:
   602→
   603→```rust
   604→event::Action::Select(workspace_id) => {
   605→    if backend.is_inside() {
   606→        // Inside tmux — switch-client won't block, so exit TUI
   607→        ratatui::restore();
   608→        cmd_launch_by_id(&workspace_id, backend);
   609→        return Ok(None); // Don't loop back — tmux switch is instant
   610→    } else {
   611→        // Outside tmux — attach blocks until detach
   612→        break Ok(Some(workspace_id));
   613→    }
   614→}
   615→```
   616→
   617→### Success Criteria:
   618→
   619→#### Automated Verification:
   620→- [ ] `cargo test` — all tests pass
   621→- [ ] `cargo clippy` — no warnings
   622→- [ ] Panic hook test — TUI restores terminal on panic
   623→
   624→#### Manual Verification:
   625→- [ ] `dual` → TUI → select workspace → tmux session appears
   626→- [ ] Detach from tmux (Ctrl+b d) → TUI reappears automatically
   627→- [ ] Select another workspace → switches to it
   628→- [ ] `q` in TUI → exits cleanly
   629→- [ ] Run `dual` from inside tmux → TUI shows → select workspace → tmux switches client
   630→- [ ] If launch fails (e.g., container not available), error message shown, TUI resumes
   631→- [ ] Ctrl+C during TUI → terminal restored properly
   632→- [ ] Panic during TUI → terminal restored properly
   633→
   634→**Implementation Note**: After completing this phase and all verification passes, this constitutes the v3.0.0 release.
   635→

    17→fn main() {

    19→    tracing_subscriber::fmt()
    20→        .with_env_filter(
    21→            tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::try_from_env("DUAL_LOG")

    30→
    31→    let exit_code = match cli.command {
    32→        None => cmd_default(&backend),
    33→        Some(Command::Add { name }) => cmd_add(name.as_deref()),
    34→        Some(Command::Create { branch, repo }) => cmd_create(repo.as_deref(), &branch),
    35→        Some(Command::Launch { workspace }) => cmd_launch(workspace.as_deref(), &backend),
    36→        Some(Command::List) => cmd_list(&backend),
    37→        Some(Command::Destroy { workspace }) => cmd_destroy(workspace.as_deref(), &backend),
    38→        Some(Command::Open { workspace }) => cmd_open(workspace),
    39→        Some(Command::Urls { workspace }) => cmd_urls(workspace),
    40→        Some(Command::Sync { workspace }) => cmd_sync(workspace),
    41→        Some(Command::Proxy) => cmd_proxy(),

    43→    };
