    46→}
    47→
    48→/// Default (no subcommand): launch TUI workspace browser.
    49→fn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {
    50→    let st = match state::load() {
    51→        Ok(s) => s,
    52→        Err(e) => {
    53→            error!("{e}");
    54→            info!("Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.");
    55→            return 1;
    56→        }
    57→    };
    58→
    59→    if st.all_workspaces().is_empty() {
    60→        info!("No workspaces. Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.");
    61→        return 0;
    62→    }
    63→
    64→    match tui::run(&st, backend) {
    65→        Ok(Some(workspace_id)) => {
    66→            // User selected a workspace — launch it
    67→            cmd_launch(Some(&workspace_id), backend)
    68→        }
    69→        Ok(None) => 0, // User quit
    70→        Err(e) => {
    71→            error!("TUI error: {e}");
    72→            1
    73→        }
    74→    }
    75→}
    76→
    77→/// Register the current repo as a dual workspace.
    78→fn cmd_add(name: Option<&str>) -> i32 {
    79→    // Detect git repo info from current directory
    80→    let (repo_root, url, branch) = match detect_git_repo() {
    81→        Ok(info) => info,
    82→        Err(e) => {
    83→            error!("{e}");
    84→            return 1;
    85→        }
    86→    };
    87→
    88→    // Derive repo name
    89→    let repo_name = match name {
    90→        Some(n) => n.to_string(),
    91→        None => derive_repo_name(&repo_root),
    92→    };
    93→
    94→    // Load or create state
    95→    let mut st = match state::load() {
    96→        Ok(s) => s,
    97→        Err(e) => {
    98→            error!("{e}");
    99→            return 1;
   100→        }
   101→    };
   102→
   103→    // Check for duplicates
   104→    if st.has_workspace(&repo_name, &branch) {
   105→        error!("workspace {}/{} already exists", repo_name, branch);
   106→        return 1;
   107→    }
   108→
   109→    // Check for .dual.toml — if missing, create a default one with helpful comments
   110→    let hints_path = repo_root.join(".dual.toml");
   111→    if !hints_path.exists() {
   112→        if let Err(e) = config::write_default_hints(&repo_root) {
   113→            warn!("failed to write .dual.toml: {e}");
   114→        } else {
   115→            info!("Created .dual.toml with defaults (image: node:20)");
   116→            info!("Edit it to customize ports, image, setup command, and env vars.");
   117→        }
   118→    }
   119→
   120→    // Initialize shared directory if [shared] is configured
   121→    let hints = config::load_hints(&repo_root).unwrap_or_default();
   122→    if let Some(ref shared_config) = hints.shared
   123→        && !shared_config.files.is_empty()
   124→    {
   125→        match shared::ensure_shared_dir(&repo_name) {
   126→            Ok(shared_dir) => {
   127→                match shared::init_from_main(&repo_root, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   128→                    Ok(moved) => {
   129→                        for f in &moved {
   130→                            info!("  shared: {f} → ~/.dual/shared/{repo_name}/");
   131→                        }
   132→                    }
   133→                    Err(e) => warn!("shared init failed: {e}"),
   134→                }
   135→            }
   136→            Err(e) => warn!("could not create shared directory: {e}"),
   137→        }
   138→    }
   139→
   140→    // Add workspace entry
   141→    let entry = state::WorkspaceEntry {
   142→        repo: repo_name.clone(),
   143→        url,
   144→        branch: branch.clone(),
   145→        path: Some(repo_root.to_string_lossy().to_string()),
   146→    };
   147→
   148→    if let Err(e) = st.add_workspace(entry) {
   149→        error!("{e}");
   150→        return 1;
   151→    }
   152→
   153→    // Save state
   154→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   155→        error!("failed to save state: {e}");
   156→        return 1;
   157→    }
   158→
   159→    let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&repo_name, &branch);
   160→    info!("Added workspace: {ws_id}");
   161→    info!("Use `dual launch {ws_id}` to start.");
   162→    0
   163→}
   164→
   165→/// Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo.
   166→fn cmd_create(repo_arg: Option<&str>, branch: &str) -> i32 {
   167→    let mut st = match state::load() {
   168→        Ok(s) => s,
   169→        Err(e) => {
   170→            error!("{e}");
   171→            return 1;
   172→        }
   173→    };
   174→
   175→    // Resolve repo name: use --repo if provided, otherwise auto-detect from cwd
   176→    let repo = match repo_arg {
   177→        Some(r) => r.to_string(),
   178→        None => match detect_repo_from_cwd(&st) {
   179→            Some(r) => {
   180→                info!("Auto-detected repo: {r}");
   181→                r
   182→            }
   183→            None => {
   184→                error!("could not detect repo from current directory");
   185→                info!("Usage: dual create <branch> --repo <name>");
   186→                info!("Or run from inside a repo that was added with `dual add`.");
   187→                return 1;
   188→            }
   189→        },
   190→    };
   191→
   192→    // Find an existing workspace for this repo
   193→    let existing = st.workspaces_for_repo(&repo);
   194→    if existing.is_empty() {
   195→        error!("repo '{repo}' not found. Run `dual add` inside the repo first.");
   196→        return 1;
   197→    }
   198→
   199→    // Check if this branch already exists
   200→    if st.has_workspace(&repo, branch) {
   201→        error!("workspace {repo}/{branch} already exists");
   202→        return 1;
   203→    }
   204→
   205→    // Get URL from existing entry
   206→    let url = existing[0].url.clone();
   207→
   208→    // Add new entry (no explicit path — will be cloned on launch)
   209→    let entry = state::WorkspaceEntry {
   210→        repo: repo.clone(),
   211→        url,
   212→        branch: branch.to_string(),
   213→        path: None,
   214→    };
   215→
   216→    if let Err(e) = st.add_workspace(entry) {
   217→        error!("{e}");
   218→        return 1;
   219→    }
   220→
   221→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   222→        error!("failed to save state: {e}");
   223→        return 1;
   224→    }
   225→
   226→    let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&repo, branch);
   227→    info!("Created workspace: {ws_id}");
   228→    info!("Use `dual launch {ws_id}` to start.");
   229→    0
   230→}
   231→
   232→/// Launch a specific workspace: clone → container → shell RC → tmux → attach.
   233→fn cmd_launch(workspace_arg: Option<&str>, backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {
   234→    let st = match state::load() {
   235→        Ok(s) => s,
   236→        Err(e) => {
   237→            error!("{e}");
   238→            return 1;
   239→        }
   240→    };
   241→
   242→    // Resolve workspace: use arg if provided, otherwise auto-detect from cwd
   243→    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {
   244→        match st.resolve_workspace(ws) {
   245→            Some(e) => e,
   246→            None => {
   247→                error!("unknown workspace '{ws}'");
   248→                info!("Configured workspaces:");
   249→                for w in st.all_workspaces() {
   250→                    let id = config::workspace_id(&w.repo, &w.branch);
   251→                    info!("  {id}");
   252→                }
   253→                return 1;
   254→            }
   255→        }
   256→    } else {
   257→        match detect_workspace(&st) {
   258→            Some(e) => {
   259→                let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&e.repo, &e.branch);
   260→                info!("Auto-detected workspace: {ws_id}");
   261→                st.resolve_workspace(&ws_id).unwrap()
   262→            }
   263→            None => {
   264→                error!("could not detect workspace from current directory");
   265→                info!("Usage: dual launch [workspace]");
   266→                return 1;
   267→            }
   268→        }
   269→    };
   270→
   271→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   272→    let container_name = config::container_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   273→    let session_name = config::session_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   274→    debug!(
   275→        repo = %entry.repo,
   276→        branch = %entry.branch,
   277→        "resolved workspace"
   278→    );
   279→
   280→    // Step 1: Resolve workspace directory
   281→    let workspace_dir = if let Some(ref path) = entry.path {
   282→        let dir = PathBuf::from(path);
   283→        if !dir.join(".git").exists() {
   284→            error!(
   285→                "workspace path {} does not contain a git repo",
   286→                dir.display()
   287→            );
   288→            return 1;
   289→        }
   290→        dir
   291→    } else {
   292→        // Try to clone from local main workspace first (fast, hardlinks)
   293→        let target_dir = config::workspace_dir(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   294→        let main_workspace_path = st
   295→            .workspaces_for_repo(&entry.repo)
   296→            .into_iter()
   297→            .find(|ws| ws.path.is_some())
   298→            .and_then(|ws| ws.path.as_ref().map(PathBuf::from));
   299→
   300→        match main_workspace_path {
   301→            Some(main_path) if main_path.join(".git").exists() => {
   302→                info!("Cloning from local main workspace...");
   303→                match clone::clone_from_local(&main_path, &target_dir, &entry.branch) {
   304→                    Ok(dir) => dir,
   305→                    Err(e) => {
   306→                        error!("local clone failed: {e}");
   307→                        return 1;
   308→                    }
   309→                }
   310→            }
   311→            _ => {
   312→                // Fallback: clone from remote URL
   313→                match clone::clone_workspace(
   314→                    &workspace_root,
   315→                    &entry.repo,
   316→                    &entry.url,
   317→                    &entry.branch,
   318→                ) {
   319→                    Ok(dir) => dir,
   320→                    Err(e) => {
   321→                        error!("clone failed: {e}");
   322→                        return 1;
   323→                    }
   324→                }
   325→            }
   326→        }
   327→    };
   328→
   329→    // Step 2: Handle shared files
   330→    let hints = config::load_hints(&workspace_dir).unwrap_or_default();
   331→    if let Some(ref shared_config) = hints.shared
   332→        && !shared_config.files.is_empty()
   333→        && let Ok(shared_dir) = shared::ensure_shared_dir(&entry.repo)
   334→    {
   335→        if entry.path.is_some() {
   336→            // Main workspace: ensure shared files are initialized
   337→            match shared::init_from_main(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   338→                Ok(moved) => {
   339→                    for f in &moved {
   340→                        info!("  shared: {f} → ~/.dual/shared/{}/", entry.repo);
   341→                    }
   342→                }
   343→                Err(e) => warn!("shared init failed: {e}"),
   344→            }
   345→        } else {
   346→            // Branch workspace: copy shared files
   347→            match shared::copy_to_branch(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   348→                Ok(copied) => {
   349→                    for f in &copied {
   350→                        info!("  shared: copied {f}");
   351→                    }
   352→                }
   353→                Err(e) => warn!("shared copy failed: {e}"),
   354→            }
   355→        }
   356→    }
   357→
   358→    // Step 3: Ensure container exists and is running
   359→    let is_new_container = matches!(
   360→        container::status(&container_name),
   361→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing
   362→    );
   363→    match container::status(&container_name) {
   364→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing => {
   365→            info!("Creating container {container_name}...");
   366→            if let Err(e) = container::create(
   367→                &container_name,
   368→                &workspace_dir,
   369→                &hints.image,
   370→                &hints.env,
   371→                &hints.anonymous_volumes,
   372→            ) {
   373→                error!("container create failed: {e}");
   374→                return 1;
   375→            }
   376→            if let Err(e) = container::start(&container_name) {
   377→                error!("container start failed: {e}");
   378→                return 1;
   379→            }
   380→        }
   381→        container::ContainerStatus::Stopped => {
   382→            info!("Starting container {container_name}...");
   383→            if let Err(e) = container::start(&container_name) {
   384→                error!("container start failed: {e}");
   385→                return 1;
   386→            }
   387→        }
   388→        container::ContainerStatus::Running => {}
   389→    }
   390→
   391→    // Step 3.5: Run setup command on new containers
   392→    if is_new_container && let Some(ref setup) = hints.setup {
   393→        info!("Running setup: {setup}");
   394→        if let Err(e) = container::exec_setup(&container_name, setup) {
   395→            error!("setup failed: {e}");
   396→            return 1;
   397→        }
   398→    }
   399→
   400→    // Step 4: Write shell RC file
   401→    let rc_path = match shell::write_rc_file(&container_name, &hints.extra_commands) {
   402→        Ok(p) => p,
   403→        Err(e) => {
   404→            error!("failed to write shell RC: {e}");
   405→            return 1;
   406→        }
   407→    };
   408→
   409→    // Step 5: Create tmux session if not alive
   410→    if !backend.is_alive(&session_name) {
   411→        let source_cmd = shell::source_file_command(&rc_path);
   412→        if let Err(e) = backend.create_session(&session_name, &workspace_dir, Some(&source_cmd)) {
   413→            error!("session creation failed: {e}");
   414→            return 1;
   415→        }
   416→    }
   417→
   418→    // Step 6: Attach
   419→    info!("Attaching to {session_name}...");
   420→    if let Err(e) = backend.attach(&session_name) {
   421→        error!("attach failed: {e}");
   422→        return 1;
   423→    }
   424→
   425→    0
   426→}
   427→
   428→/// List all configured workspaces with their live status.
   429→fn cmd_list(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {
   430→    let st = match state::load() {
   431→        Ok(s) => s,
   432→        Err(e) => {
   433→            error!("{e}");
   434→            return 1;
   435→        }
   436→    };
   437→
   438→    let workspaces = st.all_workspaces();
   439→    if workspaces.is_empty() {
   440→        info!("No workspaces configured.");
   441→        return 0;
   442→    }
   443→
   444→    print_workspace_status(&st, backend);
   445→    0
   446→}
   447→
   448→/// Destroy a workspace: tmux → container → clone.
   449→fn cmd_destroy(workspace_arg: Option<&str>, backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {
   450→    let mut st = match state::load() {
   451→        Ok(s) => s,
   452→        Err(e) => {
   453→            error!("{e}");
   454→            return 1;
   455→        }
   456→    };
   457→
   458→    // Resolve workspace: use arg if provided, otherwise auto-detect from cwd
   459→    let workspace;
   460→    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {
   461→        workspace = ws.to_string();
   462→        match st.resolve_workspace(ws) {
   463→            Some(e) => e.clone(),
   464→            None => {
   465→                error!("unknown workspace '{ws}'");
   466→                return 1;
   467→            }
   468→        }
   469→    } else {
   470→        match detect_workspace(&st) {
   471→            Some(e) => {
   472→                workspace = config::workspace_id(&e.repo, &e.branch);
   473→                info!("Auto-detected workspace: {workspace}");
   474→                e
   475→            }
   476→            None => {
   477→                error!("could not detect workspace from current directory");
   478→                info!("Usage: dual destroy [workspace]");
   479→                return 1;
   480→            }
   481→        }
   482→    };
   483→
   484→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   485→    let container_name = config::container_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   486→    let session_name = config::session_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   487→
   488→    // Destroy tmux session
   489→    if backend.is_alive(&session_name) {
   490→        info!("Destroying session {session_name}...");
   491→        if let Err(e) = backend.destroy(&session_name) {
   492→            warn!("session destroy failed: {e}");
   493→        }
   494→    }
   495→
   496→    // Stop and remove container
   497→    match container::status(&container_name) {
   498→        container::ContainerStatus::Running => {
   499→            info!("Stopping container {container_name}...");
   500→            if let Err(e) = container::stop(&container_name) {
   501→                warn!("container stop failed: {e}");
   502→            }
   503→            info!("Removing container {container_name}...");
   504→            if let Err(e) = container::destroy(&container_name) {
   505→                warn!("container remove failed: {e}");
   506→            }
   507→        }
   508→        container::ContainerStatus::Stopped => {
   509→            info!("Removing container {container_name}...");
   510→            if let Err(e) = container::destroy(&container_name) {
   511→                warn!("container remove failed: {e}");
   512→            }
   513→        }
   514→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing => {}
   515→    }
   516→
   517→    // Remove clone (only for non-explicit-path workspaces)
   518→    if entry.path.is_none() && clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch)
   519→    {
   520→        info!("Removing clone...");
   521→        if let Err(e) = clone::remove_workspace(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch) {
   522→            error!("failed to remove clone: {e}");
   523→            return 1;
   524→        }
   525→    }
   526→
   527→    // Remove from state
   528→    st.remove_workspace(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   529→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   530→        warn!("failed to save state: {e}");
   531→    }
   532→
   533→    info!("Workspace '{workspace}' destroyed.");
   534→    0
   535→}
   536→
   537→/// Open workspace services in the default browser.
   538→fn cmd_open(workspace: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   539→    let st = match state::load() {
   540→        Ok(s) => s,
   541→        Err(e) => {
   542→            error!("{e}");
   543→            return 1;
   544→        }
   545→    };
   546→
   547→    let url_groups = proxy::workspace_urls(&st);
   548→    if url_groups.is_empty() {
   549→        info!("No URLs configured. Add 'ports' to .dual.toml in your repo.");
   550→        return 0;
   551→    }
   552→
   553→    // Filter by workspace if specified
   554→    let filtered: Vec<_> = match &workspace {
   555→        Some(ws) => url_groups.into_iter().filter(|(id, _)| id == ws).collect(),
   556→        None => url_groups,
   557→    };
   558→
   559→    if filtered.is_empty() {
   560→        if let Some(ws) = &workspace {
   561→            error!("no URLs for workspace '{ws}'");
   562→        }
   563→        return 1;
   564→    }
   565→
   566→    for (_, urls) in &filtered {
   567→        for url_line in urls {
   568→            // Extract the URL part (after the status icon)
   569→            let url = url_line
   570→                .trim()
   571→                .trim_start_matches('\u{25cf}')
   572→                .trim_start_matches('\u{25cb}')
   573→                .trim();
   574→            let http_url = format!("http://{url}");
   575→            #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
   576→            let _ = std::process::Command::new("open").arg(&http_url).spawn();
   577→            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
   578→            let _ = std::process::Command::new("xdg-open")
   579→                .arg(&http_url)
   580→                .spawn();
   581→            info!("Opening {http_url}");
   582→        }
   583→    }
   584→
   585→    0
   586→}
   587→
   588→/// Show workspace URLs.
   589→fn cmd_urls(workspace: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   590→    let st = match state::load() {
   591→        Ok(s) => s,
   592→        Err(e) => {
   593→            error!("{e}");
   594→            return 1;
   595→        }
   596→    };
   597→
   598→    let url_groups = proxy::workspace_urls(&st);
   599→    if url_groups.is_empty() {
   600→        info!("No URLs configured. Add 'ports' to .dual.toml in your repo.");
   601→        return 0;
   602→    }
   603→
   604→    // Filter by workspace if specified
   605→    let filtered: Vec<_> = match &workspace {
   606→        Some(ws) => url_groups.into_iter().filter(|(id, _)| id == ws).collect(),
   607→        None => url_groups,
   608→    };
   609→
   610→    for (workspace_id, urls) in &filtered {
   611→        info!("{workspace_id}");
   612→        for url in urls {
   613→            info!("{url}");
   614→        }
   615→        info!("");
   616→    }
   617→
   618→    0
   619→}
   620→
   621→/// Start the reverse proxy.
   622→fn cmd_proxy() -> i32 {
   623→    let st = match state::load() {
   624→        Ok(s) => s,
   625→        Err(e) => {
   626→            error!("{e}");
   627→            return 1;
   628→        }
   629→    };
   630→
   631→    let rt = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().expect("failed to create tokio runtime");
   632→    match rt.block_on(proxy::start(&st)) {
   633→        Ok(()) => 0,
   634→        Err(e) => {
   635→            error!("proxy failed: {e}");
   636→            1
   637→        }
   638→    }
   639→}
   640→
   641→/// Output shell RC for a container (used by `eval "$(dual shell-rc <name>)"`).
   642→fn cmd_shell_rc(container_name: &str) -> i32 {
   643→    print!("{}", shell::generate_rc(container_name, &[]));
   644→    0
   645→}
   646→
   647→/// Sync shared config files for a workspace.
   648→fn cmd_sync(workspace_arg: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   649→    let st = match state::load() {
   650→        Ok(s) => s,
   651→        Err(e) => {
   652→            error!("{e}");
   653→            return 1;
   654→        }
   655→    };
   656→
   657→    // Resolve which workspace we're syncing
   658→    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {
   659→        match st.resolve_workspace(&ws) {
   660→            Some(e) => e.clone(),
   661→            None => {
   662→                error!("unknown workspace '{ws}'");
   663→                return 1;
   664→            }
   665→        }
   666→    } else {
   667→        match detect_workspace(&st) {
   668→            Some(e) => e,
   669→            None => {
   670→                error!("not inside a dual workspace");
   671→                info!("Usage: dual sync [workspace]");
   672→                return 1;
   673→            }
   674→        }
   675→    };
   676→
   677→    // Load hints
   678→    let workspace_dir = st.workspace_dir(&entry);
   679→    let hints = config::load_hints(&workspace_dir).unwrap_or_default();
   680→    let shared_config = match &hints.shared {
   681→        Some(s) if !s.files.is_empty() => s,
   682→        _ => {
   683→            error!("no [shared] section in .dual.toml (or files list is empty)");
   684→            return 1;
   685→        }
   686→    };
   687→
   688→    let shared_dir = match shared::ensure_shared_dir(&entry.repo) {
   689→        Ok(d) => d,
   690→        Err(e) => {
   691→            error!("{e}");
   692→            return 1;
   693→        }
   694→    };
   695→
   696→    let is_main = entry.path.is_some();
   697→
   698→    if is_main {
   699→        // Main workspace: init shared dir, then prompt to sync all branches
   700→        match shared::init_from_main(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   701→            Ok(moved) => {
   702→                for f in &moved {
   703→                    info!("  moved {f} → shared/");
   704→                }
   705→            }
   706→            Err(e) => {
   707→                error!("{e}");
   708→                return 1;
   709→            }
   710→        }
   711→
   712→        // Prompt to sync all branches
   713→        let branches: Vec<_> = st
   714→            .workspaces_for_repo(&entry.repo)
   715→            .into_iter()
   716→            .filter(|ws| ws.path.is_none())
   717→            .collect();
   718→
   719→        if branches.is_empty() {
   720→            info!("No branch workspaces to sync.");
   721→            return 0;
   722→        }
   723→
   724→        // Interactive prompt — use println! directly since this is user interaction
   725→        println!(
   726→            "\nSync shared files to ALL {} branch workspace(s)? [y/N]",
   727→            branches.len()
   728→        );
   729→        let mut input = String::new();
   730→        std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut input).unwrap_or(0);
   731→        if !input.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("y") {
   732→            info!("Cancelled.");
   733→            return 0;
   734→        }
   735→
   736→        for branch_entry in &branches {
   737→            let branch_dir = st.workspace_dir(branch_entry);
   738→            if !branch_dir.exists() {
   739→                continue; // Not yet cloned
   740→            }
   741→            let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&branch_entry.repo, &branch_entry.branch);
   742→            match shared::copy_to_branch(&branch_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   743→                Ok(copied) => {
   744→                    info!("{ws_id}: synced {} file(s)", copied.len());
   745→                }
   746→                Err(e) => error!("{ws_id}: {e}"),
   747→            }
   748→        }
   749→    } else {
   750→        // Branch workspace: copy from shared dir
   751→        match shared::copy_to_branch(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   752→            Ok(copied) => {
   753→                if copied.is_empty() {
   754→                    info!(
   755→                        "No shared files available yet. Run `dual sync` in the main workspace first."
   756→                    );
   757→                } else {
   758→                    for f in &copied {
   759→                        info!("  synced {f}");
   760→                    }
   761→                }
   762→            }
   763→            Err(e) => {
   764→                error!("{e}");
   765→                return 1;
   766→            }
   767→        }
   768→    }
   769→
   770→    0
   771→}
   772→
   773→/// Detect the repo name from the current working directory.
   774→///
   775→/// Matches the git remote URL of the cwd against known workspace URLs in state.
   776→fn detect_repo_from_cwd(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<String> {
   777→    let (_, url, _) = detect_git_repo().ok()?;
   778→
   779→    // Match against known workspace URLs
   780→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   781→        if ws.url == url {
   782→            return Some(ws.repo.clone());
   783→        }
   784→    }
   785→
   786→    // Also try matching by git root path against workspace paths
   787→    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;
   788→    let root = std::process::Command::new("git")
   789→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   790→        .output()
   791→        .ok()
   792→        .filter(|o| o.status.success())
   793→        .map(|o| PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string()))?;
   794→
   795→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   796→        let ws_dir = st.workspace_dir(ws);
   797→        if ws_dir == root || ws_dir == cwd {
   798→            return Some(ws.repo.clone());
   799→        }
   800→    }
   801→
   802→    None
   803→}
   804→
   805→/// Detect which workspace the current directory belongs to.
   806→fn detect_workspace(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<state::WorkspaceEntry> {
   807→    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;
   808→
   809→    // Try git root first (handles being in subdirectories)
   810→    let root = std::process::Command::new("git")
   811→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   812→        .output()
   813→        .ok()
   814→        .filter(|o| o.status.success())
   815→        .map(|o| PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string()))
   816→        .unwrap_or(cwd);
   817→
   818→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   819→        let ws_dir = st.workspace_dir(ws);
   820→        if ws_dir == root {
   821→            return Some(ws.clone());
   822→        }
   823→    }
   824→    None
   825→}
   826→
   827→/// Print workspace status grouped by repo.
   828→fn print_workspace_status(st: &state::WorkspaceState, backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) {
   829→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   830→
   831→    // Collect unique repo names in order of first appearance
   832→    let mut repos: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   833→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   834→        if !repos.contains(&ws.repo) {
   835→            repos.push(ws.repo.clone());
   836→        }
   837→    }
   838→
   839→    for repo in &repos {
   840→        println!("{repo}");
   841→        for ws in st.workspaces_for_repo(repo) {
   842→            let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   843→            let session_name = config::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   844→
   845→            let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {
   846→                ws.path
   847→                    .as_ref()
   848→                    .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join(".git").exists())
   849→                    .unwrap_or(false)
   850→            } else {
   851→                clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)
   852→            };
   853→            let container_st = container::status(&container_name);
   854→            let tmux_alive = backend.is_alive(&session_name);
   855→
   856→            let (icon, status_text) = match (&container_st, tmux_alive) {
   857→                (container::ContainerStatus::Running, true) => {
   858→                    ("\u{25cf}", "running  (container: up, tmux: attached)")
   859→                }
   860→                (container::ContainerStatus::Running, false) => {
   861→                    ("\u{25cf}", "running  (container: up, tmux: none)")
   862→                }
   863→                (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, true) => (
   864→                    "\u{25cb}",
   865→                    "stopped  (container: stopped, tmux: background)",
   866→                ),
   867→                (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, false) => {
   868→                    ("\u{25cb}", "stopped  (container: stopped, tmux: none)")
   869→                }
   870→                (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) if clone_exists => {
   871→                    ("\u{25cb}", "stopped  (not launched)")
   872→                }
   873→                (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) => {
   874→                    ("\u{25cc}", "lazy     (not cloned yet)")
   875→                }
   876→            };
   877→
   878→            let branch_display = config::decode_branch(&config::encode_branch(&ws.branch));
   879→            println!("  {branch_display:<24} {icon} {status_text}");
   880→        }
   881→        println!();
   882→    }
   883→}
   884→
   885→/// Detect git repo info from the current directory.
   886→fn detect_git_repo() -> Result<(PathBuf, String, String), String> {
   887→    // Get repo root
   888→    let root_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   889→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   890→        .output()
   891→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   892→    if !root_output.status.success() {
   893→        return Err("not inside a git repository".to_string());
   894→    }
   895→    let root = PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&root_output.stdout).trim());
   896→
   897→    // Get remote URL
   898→    let url_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   899→        .args(["remote", "get-url", "origin"])
   900→        .output()
   901→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   902→    let url = if url_output.status.success() {
   903→        String::from_utf8_lossy(&url_output.stdout)
   904→            .trim()
   905→            .to_string()
   906→    } else {
   907→        root.to_string_lossy().to_string() // local-only repo, use path as URL
   908→    };
   909→
   910→    // Get current branch
   911→    let branch_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   912→        .args(["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"])
   913→        .output()
   914→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   915→    let branch = String::from_utf8_lossy(&branch_output.stdout)
   916→        .trim()
   917→        .to_string();
   918→
   919→    Ok((root, url, branch))
   920→}
   921→
   922→/// Derive a short repo name from a directory path.
   923→/// "/Users/jeevan/code/lightfast" → "lightfast"
   924→fn derive_repo_name(path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
   925→    path.file_name()
   926→        .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
   927→        .unwrap_or("repo")
   928→        .to_string()
   929→}

  "old_string": "fn main() {\n    // Initialize tracing with DUAL_LOG env var (default: info)\n    tracing_subscriber::fmt()\n        .with_env_filter(\n            tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::try_from_env(\"DUAL_LOG\")\n                .unwrap_or_else(|_| tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::new(\"info\")),\n        )\n        .without_time()\n        .with_target(false)\n        .init();\n\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n    let backend = TmuxBackend::new();",
  "new_string": "fn main() {\n    // Install panic hook to restore terminal state if TUI panics\n    let original_hook = std::panic::take_hook();\n    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |panic_info| {\n        let _ = crossterm::execute!(\n            std::io::stderr(),\n            crossterm::terminal::LeaveAlternateScreen\n        );\n        let _ = crossterm::terminal::disable_raw_mode();\n        original_hook(panic_info);\n    }));\n\n    // Initialize tracing with DUAL_LOG env var (default: info)\n    tracing_subscriber::fmt()\n        .with_env_filter(\n            tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::try_from_env(\"DUAL_LOG\")\n                .unwrap_or_else(|_| tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::new(\"info\")),\n        )\n        .without_time()\n        .with_target(false)\n        .init();\n\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n    let backend = TmuxBackend::new();"
}

{

  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/src/main.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Default (no subcommand): launch TUI workspace browser.\nfn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {\n    let st = match state::load() {\n        Ok(s) => s,\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"{e}\");\n            info!(\"Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    };\n\n    if st.all_workspaces().is_empty() {\n        info!(\"No workspaces. Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.\");\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    match tui::run(&st, backend) {\n        Ok(Some(workspace_id)) => {\n            // User selected a workspace — launch it\n            cmd_launch(Some(&workspace_id), backend)\n        }\n        Ok(None) => 0, // User quit\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"TUI error: {e}\");\n            1\n        }\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Default (no subcommand): launch TUI workspace browser.\n///\n/// Implements a suspend/resume loop:\n/// 1. Show TUI → user selects workspace\n/// 2. TUI suspends (ratatui::restore) → launch workspace → tmux attach (blocks)\n/// 3. User detaches from tmux → control returns → loop back to step 1\n///\n/// If already inside tmux, switch-client is instant (non-blocking),\n/// so we exit after launch instead of looping.\nfn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {\n    let inside_tmux = backend.is_inside();\n\n    loop {\n        // Reload state each iteration (workspaces may have changed while in tmux)\n        let st = match state::load() {\n            Ok(s) => s,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                info!(\"Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.\");\n                return 1;\n            }\n        };\n\n        if st.all_workspaces().is_empty() {\n            info!(\"No workspaces. Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.\");\n            return 0;\n        }\n\n        match tui::run(&st, backend) {\n            Ok(Some(workspace_id)) => {\n                // TUI already called ratatui::restore() — terminal is in normal mode\n                let exit_code = cmd_launch(Some(&workspace_id), backend);\n\n                if inside_tmux {\n                    // switch-client is instant — don't loop back to TUI\n                    return exit_code;\n                }\n\n                if exit_code != 0 {\n                    eprintln!(\"Launch failed (exit code {exit_code}). Press Enter to continue...\");\n                    let _ = std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut String::new());\n                }\n\n                // tmux attach returned (user detached) — loop back to TUI\n                continue;\n            }\n            Ok(None) => return 0, // User quit\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"TUI error: {e}\");\n                return 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}"
}
```

 /// Default (no subcommand): launch TUI workspace browser.
+///
+/// Implements a suspend/resume loop:
+/// 1. Show TUI → user selects workspace
+/// 2. TUI suspends (ratatui::restore) → launch workspace → tmux attach (blocks)
+/// 3. User detaches from tmux → control returns → loop back to step 1
+///
+/// If already inside tmux, switch-client is instant (non-blocking),
+/// so we exit after launch instead of looping.
 fn cmd_default(backend: &dyn MultiplexerBackend) -> i32 {

-        Err(e) => {

-        }

+    let inside_tmux = backend.is_inside();
 
-    if st.all_workspaces().is_empty() {
-        info!("No workspaces. Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.");
-        return 0;
-    }
+    loop {
+        // Reload state each iteration (workspaces may have changed while in tmux)
+        let st = match state::load() {
+            Ok(s) => s,
+            Err(e) => {
+                error!("{e}");
+                info!("Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.");

-        Ok(None) => 0, // User quit
-        Err(e) => {
-            error!("TUI error: {e}");
-            1
+
+        match tui::run(&st, backend) {
+            Ok(Some(workspace_id)) => {
+                // TUI already called ratatui::restore() — terminal is in normal mode
+                let exit_code = cmd_launch(Some(&workspace_id), backend);
+
+                if inside_tmux {
+                    // switch-client is instant — don't loop back to TUI
+                    return exit_code;
+                }
+
+                if exit_code != 0 {
+                    eprintln!("Launch failed (exit code {exit_code}). Press Enter to continue...");
+                    let _ = std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut String::new());
+                }
+
+                // tmux attach returned (user detached) — loop back to TUI
+                continue;
+            }
+            Ok(None) => return 0, // User quit
+            Err(e) => {
+                error!("TUI error: {e}");
+                return 1;
+            }

 }

> AGENT

Done. **Phase 3 complete.**

**PR #139**: https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual/pull/139

**What changed** (only `src/main.rs`):
- **Panic hook** — restores terminal on crash (LeaveAlternateScreen + disable raw mode)
- **Suspend/resume loop** — TUI → select → tmux attach (blocks) → detach → TUI resumes with fresh state
- **Inside-tmux path** — exits after `switch-client` instead of looping (it's instant)

This completes all 3 phases of the v3.0.0 plan. After merging and manual verification, this is the v3.0.0 release.

> DEVELOPER

whats the command to turn on tui btw?

> AGENT

Just `dual` — no arguments. It's the default command.

If there are no workspaces registered yet, it'll prompt you to run `dual add` first. Once you have workspaces, `dual` launches the TUI browser.

`dual list` is the non-interactive fallback if you just want text output.

     1→# Changelog
     2→
     3→All notable changes to Dual are documented in this file.
     4→
     5→## [2.2.0] - 2026-02-15
     6→
     7→Bug fixes, feature completions, and context-aware CLI improvements.
     8→
     9→### Added
    10→
    11→- **Context-aware `dual create`** — Repo auto-detected from cwd when `--repo` is omitted. New syntax: `dual create <branch> [--repo NAME]`
    12→- **Context-aware `dual launch` / `dual destroy`** — Workspace arg now optional, auto-detected from cwd
    13→- **Tmux nested session detection** — Detects `$TMUX` env var and uses `switch-client` instead of `attach-session` to avoid nesting
    14→- **Grouped `dual list` output** — Workspaces displayed grouped by repo with detailed container/tmux status
    15→- **`clone_from_local()`** — New fast clone strategy using `git clone --local` from main workspace + `git checkout -b` for new branches
    16→- **Commented `.dual.toml` template** — `dual add` creates a `.dual.toml` with helpful inline documentation
    17→- **Environment variable support** — `[env]` section in `.dual.toml` passed as `-e KEY=VALUE` to `docker create`
    18→- **Setup command support** — `setup = "pnpm install"` in `.dual.toml` runs via `docker exec` after first container creation
    19→- **Configurable container commands** — `extra_commands` field in `.dual.toml` merges with default command routing list
    20→- **Configurable anonymous volumes** — `anonymous_volumes` field in `.dual.toml` replaces hardcoded `node_modules` volume
    21→
    22→### Changed
    23→
    24→- **`dual create` CLI** — Args changed from `<repo> <branch>` to `<branch> [--repo NAME]` (breaking)
    25→- **`dual launch` CLI** — Workspace arg now optional (backward compatible)
    26→- **`dual destroy` CLI** — Workspace arg now optional (backward compatible)
    27→- **`container::create()` signature** — Now accepts `env` and `anonymous_volumes` parameters
    28→- **`shell::generate_rc()` / `write_rc_file()`** — Now accept `extra_commands` parameter
    29→
    30→---
    31→
    32→## [2.0.0] - 2026-02-15
    33→
    34→Complete rewrite from TypeScript to Rust. Dual is now a compiled binary with Docker-based workspace isolation, transparent command routing, and a reverse proxy for browser access.
    35→
    36→### Added
    37→
    38→- **Rust rewrite** — Full rewrite in Rust (Edition 2024) replacing the TypeScript/npm implementation
    39→- **Docker container isolation** — Each workspace runs in its own Docker container with bind-mounted source
    40→- **Transparent command routing** — Shell RC intercepts runtime commands (`pnpm`, `node`, `curl localhost`) and routes them to containers via `docker exec`
    41→- **Reverse proxy** — HTTP reverse proxy (hyper + tokio) with `{repo}-{branch}.localhost:{port}` subdomain routing for browser access
    42→- **tmux session management** — Automatic tmux sessions per workspace with attach/detach lifecycle
    43→- **Two-file config system** — Split architecture: `.dual.toml` (per-repo hints, committed to git) + `~/.dual/workspaces.toml` (global state, managed by Dual)
    44→- **`dual add` command** — Register an existing git repo as a Dual workspace
    45→- **`dual create` command** — Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo
    46→- **`dual sync` command** — Propagate shared config files (`.vercel`, `.env.local`, etc.) across branch workspaces using symlinks (Unix) or copies (Windows)
    47→- **`dual shell-rc` command** — Generate shell RC for transparent command routing (internal use)
    48→- **Shared config propagation** — Declare shared files in `.dual.toml` `[shared]` section, synced across all branches of a repo via `~/.dual/shared/{repo}/`
    49→- **Atomic state writes** — File locking (`fs2`) + temp file + atomic rename for crash-safe state persistence
    50→- **Structured logging** — `tracing` + `tracing-subscriber` with `DUAL_LOG` environment variable filter
    51→- **Typed errors** — `thiserror`-based error types replacing string errors
    52→- **curl-based installer** — Shell and PowerShell install scripts via cargo-dist
    53→- **Cross-platform builds** — Pre-built binaries for macOS (Apple Silicon + Intel), Linux (ARM64 + x64), and Windows (x64)
    54→- **CI/CD pipeline** — GitHub Actions with check/lint, unit tests, E2E tests (Docker + tmux), and automated releases
    55→
    56→### Changed
    57→
    58→- **Architecture** — Worktree-based workflows replaced with full git clone per workspace
    59→- **Config format** — Single `dual.toml` replaced with two-file system (`.dual.toml` + `workspaces.toml`)
    60→- **Installation** — npm package replaced with compiled binary (`cargo install` or curl installer)
```

> DEVELOPER

actually before that how do is there a meta key i can use to open dual right now already?

> AGENT

Not built into Dual yet, but you can set it up right now with a tmux keybinding. Add this to your `~/.tmux.conf`:

```bash
# Prefix + D to open Dual picker
bind-key D display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% "dual"
```

`display-popup -E` runs Dual in an overlay popup — when you select a workspace, it switches client and the popup disappears. Clean.

Or if you want it without the prefix key (e.g. just `Meta+d`):

```bash
bind-key -n M-d display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% "dual"
```

After editing, reload with `tmux source-file ~/.tmux.conf`.

The "meta-key workspace switching" item in the backlog (not yet ticketed) would bake this into Dual itself — auto-installing the keybinding via `dual init` or similar. But the manual tmux config works today.

> DEVELOPER

isnt that meta + d the detach meta?

> AGENT

Good catch on being cautious, but `M-d` (Meta+d) isn't tmux detach — that's `Prefix + d` (`Ctrl+b d`). However, `M-d` *is* "delete word" in bash/zsh readline, so it would conflict there.

Better options:

```bash
# Meta+space — clean, rarely conflicts
bind-key -n M-Space display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% "dual"

# Meta+w — "workspace" mnemonic
bind-key -n M-w display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% "dual"

# F12 — dedicated function key
bind-key -n F12 display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% "dual"
```

`M-Space` is probably the safest — it's easy to hit and rarely bound to anything.

> DEVELOPER

are you sure thats the correct way? should we unbind first?

> AGENT

`bind-key` in tmux overwrites any existing binding for that key — no need to `unbind-key` first. But worth checking if anything's already on that key:

```bash
tmux list-keys | grep M-Space
```

If it's clean, just add the bind. `M-Space` has no default tmux binding, so you should be good:

```bash
bind-key -n M-Space display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% "dual"
```

> DEVELOPER

wait. so what is meta in tmux? i was talk about prefix

> AGENT

`Meta` = `Alt` (or `Option` on Mac). So `M-Space` = `Alt+Space`.

`Prefix` = `Ctrl+b` (then release, then press the key).

Two different things:
- `bind-key -n M-Space ...` → press `Alt+Space` directly, no prefix needed
- `bind-key Space ...` → press `Ctrl+b`, then `Space`

If you want it on prefix:

```bash
bind-key Space display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% "dual"
```

That's `Ctrl+b Space`. Default binding for `Prefix + Space` is `next-layout`, so you'd be overriding that. If you don't use it, no problem. Otherwise pick another key like:

```bash
bind-key w display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% "dual"
```

`Prefix + w` defaults to `choose-tree` — but Dual's TUI is basically replacing that anyway.

     1→# Dual
     2→
     3→Terminal workspace orchestrator for parallel multi-repo development with AI coding agents.
     4→
     5→Dual manages isolated development environments — one full git clone per workspace, one Docker container per clone — so you can run multiple repos on multiple branches simultaneously, all with Claude Code sessions active, all running dev servers on default ports, with zero conflicts.
     6→
     7→## Installation
     8→
     9→**curl (macOS/Linux):**
    10→
    11→```bash
    12→curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -LsSf https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual/releases/latest/download/dual-installer.sh | sh
    13→```
    14→
    15→**PowerShell (Windows):**
    16→
    17→```powershell
    18→powershell -ExecutionPolicy ByPass -c "irm https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual/releases/latest/download/dual-installer.ps1 | iex"
    19→```
    20→
    21→**From source:**
    22→
    23→```bash
    24→cargo install --path .
    25→```
    26→
    27→## Prerequisites
    28→
    29→- [Docker](https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/)
    30→- [tmux](https://github.com/tmux/tmux)
    31→
    32→## Quick Start
    33→
    34→Register your repo, create a branch workspace, and launch it:
    35→
    36→```bash
    37→cd ~/code/my-project
    38→dual add                          # Register this repo
    39→dual create my-project feat/auth  # Create a branch workspace
    40→dual launch my-project-feat__auth # Launch it
    41→```
    42→
    43→This clones the repo, starts a Docker container, generates transparent command routing, and opens a tmux session. Your editor, git, and credentials stay on the host. Runtime commands (`pnpm dev`, `node`, `curl localhost`) are transparently routed to the container.
    44→
    45→## CLI Commands
    46→
    47→| Command | Description |
    48→|---------|-------------|
    49→| `dual` | Show workspace list with status |
    50→| `dual add [--name NAME]` | Register current git repo as a workspace |
    51→| `dual create <repo> <branch>` | Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo |
    52→| `dual launch <workspace>` | Clone, start container, open tmux session |
    53→| `dual list` | List all workspaces with status |
    54→| `dual destroy <workspace>` | Tear down workspace (container, tmux, clone) |
    55→| `dual open [workspace]` | Open workspace services in browser |
    56→| `dual urls [workspace]` | Display workspace URLs |
    57→| `dual sync [workspace]` | Sync shared config files across branch workspaces |
    58→| `dual proxy` | Start reverse proxy for browser access |
    59→
    60→## Configuration
    61→
    62→Dual uses two config files:
    63→
    64→### `.dual.toml` (per-repo hints)
    65→
    66→Lives in your project root. Committed to git. Controls runtime behavior.
    67→
    68→```toml
    69→image = "node:20"
    70→ports = [3000, 3001]
    71→setup = "pnpm install"
    72→
    73→[env]
    74→NODE_ENV = "development"
    75→
    76→[shared]
    77→files = [".vercel", ".env.local"]
    78→```
    79→
    80→| Field | Description | Default |
    81→|-------|-------------|---------|
    82→| `image` | Docker image for the container | `node:20` |
    83→| `ports` | Ports that services bind to (for reverse proxy) | `[]` |
    84→| `setup` | Command to run on container start | None |
    85→| `env` | Environment variables passed to the container | `{}` |
    86→| `shared.files` | Files/directories to share across branch workspaces | `[]` |
    87→
    88→### `~/.dual/workspaces.toml` (global state)
    89→
    90→Managed by Dual. Tracks all registered workspaces.
    91→
    92→```toml
    93→workspace_root = "~/dual-workspaces"
    94→
    95→[[workspaces]]
    96→repo = "my-project"
    97→url = "git@github.com:org/my-project.git"
    98→branch = "main"
    99→
   100→[[workspaces]]
   101→repo = "my-project"
   102→url = "git@github.com:org/my-project.git"
   103→branch = "feat/auth"
   104→```
   105→
   106→## How It Works
   107→
   108→When you run `dual launch`, Dual:
   109→
   110→1. Clones the repo into `{workspace_root}/{repo}/{branch}/`
   111→2. Creates a Docker container with the clone bind-mounted
   112→3. Generates a shell RC file that transparently routes runtime commands into the container via `docker exec`
   113→4. Opens a tmux session in the workspace directory
   114→
   115→Your editor, git, and credentials stay on the host. The container handles all runtime processes. Claude Code never knows it's running inside a container.
   116→
   117→## Architecture
   118→
   119→```
   120→Host                          Container
   121→+--------------------------+  +--------------------------+
   122→| nvim, git, claude, ssh   |  | pnpm, node, python       |
   123→| file reads/writes        |  | curl localhost, tests    |
   124→| credentials, SSH keys    |  | port-binding processes   |
   125→+--------------------------+  +--------------------------+
   126→        |    bind mount    |
   127→        +------------------+
   128→
   129→Browser --> {repo}-{branch}.localhost:{port}
   130→        --> reverse proxy
   131→        --> container
   132→```
   133→
   134→| Host | Container |
   135→|------|-----------|
   136→| git, cat, ls, vim | npm, pnpm, node, python |
   137→| File reads/writes | Port-binding processes |
   138→| SSH, credentials | curl localhost, tests |
   139→
   140→## Development
   141→
   142→```bash
   143→cargo build              # Build debug binary
   144→cargo build --release    # Build release binary
   145→cargo test               # Run tests
   146→cargo clippy             # Run linter
   147→cargo fmt                # Format code
   148→```
   149→
   150→Targets: Linux, macOS (Intel + Apple Silicon), Windows.
   151→
   152→## License
   153→
   154→MIT
   155→
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/README.md",
  "content": "# Dual\n\nTerminal workspace orchestrator for parallel multi-repo development with AI coding agents.\n\nDual manages isolated development environments — one full git clone per workspace, one Docker container per clone — so you can run multiple repos on multiple branches simultaneously, all with Claude Code sessions active, all running dev servers on default ports, with zero conflicts.\n\n## Installation\n\n**curl (macOS/Linux):**\n\n```bash\ncurl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -LsSf https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual/releases/latest/download/dual-installer.sh | sh\n```\n\n**PowerShell (Windows):**\n\n```powershell\npowershell -ExecutionPolicy ByPass -c \"irm https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual/releases/latest/download/dual-installer.ps1 | iex\"\n```\n\n**From source:**\n\n```bash\ncargo install --path .\n```\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- [Docker](https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/)\n- [tmux](https://github.com/tmux/tmux)\n\n## Quick Start\n\n```bash\n# 1. Register your repo\ncd ~/code/my-project\ndual add\n\n# 2. Create a branch workspace\ndual create feat/auth\n\n# 3. Launch the TUI and select a workspace\ndual\n```\n\n`dual` opens an interactive workspace browser. Select a workspace to launch it — Dual clones the repo, starts a Docker container, sets up transparent command routing, and drops you into a tmux session. Detach from tmux (`Ctrl+b d`) and you're back in the browser.\n\n## The TUI\n\nRunning `dual` with no arguments opens the workspace browser:\n\n```\n dual  workspace browser\n┌──────────────────────────────────────┐\n│▼ my-project                          │\n│  main                     ● running  │\n│  feat/auth                ○ stopped  │\n│  feat/billing             ◌ lazy     │\n│▼ agent-os                            │\n│  main                     ● running  │\n└──────────────────────────────────────┘\n j/k navigate  enter launch  q quit\n```\n\n- **j/k** or arrow keys to navigate\n- **Enter** on a workspace to launch it (clone + container + tmux)\n- **Enter** on a repo header to expand/collapse\n- **q** or Esc to quit\n\nWhen you select a workspace, the TUI suspends, tmux takes over. Detach from tmux (`Ctrl+b d`) and the TUI resumes automatically with fresh status.\n\n### Tmux keybinding\n\nFor quick access from any tmux session, add to `~/.tmux.conf`:\n\n```bash\nbind-key w display-popup -E -w 60% -h 60% \"dual\"\n```\n\nNow `Prefix + w` opens the Dual picker in a popup overlay.\n\n## CLI Commands\n\n| Command | Description |\n|---------|-------------|\n| `dual` | Open TUI workspace browser |\n| `dual add [--name NAME]` | Register current git repo as a workspace |\n| `dual create <branch> [--repo NAME]` | Create a new branch workspace |\n| `dual launch [workspace]` | Launch a workspace (auto-detects from cwd) |\n| `dual list` | List all workspaces with status (non-interactive) |\n| `dual destroy [workspace]` | Tear down workspace (container, tmux, clone) |\n| `dual open [workspace]` | Open workspace services in browser |\n| `dual urls [workspace]` | Display workspace URLs |\n| `dual sync [workspace]` | Sync shared config files across branch workspaces |\n| `dual proxy` | Start reverse proxy for browser access |\n\n## Configuration\n\nDual uses two config files:\n\n### `.dual.toml` (per-repo hints)\n\nLives in your project root. Committed to git. Controls runtime behavior.\n\n```toml\nimage = \"node:20\"\nports = [3000, 3001]\nsetup = \"pnpm install\"\n\n[env]\nNODE_ENV = \"development\"\n\n[shared]\nfiles = [\".vercel\", \".env.local\"]\n```\n\n| Field | Description | Default |\n|-------|-------------|---------|\n| `image` | Docker image for the container | `node:20` |\n| `ports` | Ports that services bind to (for reverse proxy) | `[]` |\n| `setup` | Command to run after first container creation | None |\n| `env` | Environment variables passed to the container | `{}` |\n| `shared.files` | Files/directories to share across branch workspaces | `[]` |\n| `extra_commands` | Additional commands to route to the container | `[]` |\n| `anonymous_volumes` | Container volumes (e.g., `node_modules`) | `[\"node_modules\"]` |\n\n### `~/.dual/workspaces.toml` (global state)\n\nManaged by Dual. Tracks all registered workspaces.\n\n```toml\nworkspace_root = \"~/dual-workspaces\"\n\n[[workspaces]]\nrepo = \"my-project\"\nurl = \"git@github.com:org/my-project.git\"\nbranch = \"main\"\npath = \"/Users/you/code/my-project\"\n\n[[workspaces]]\nrepo = \"my-project\"\nurl = \"git@github.com:org/my-project.git\"\nbranch = \"feat/auth\"\n```\n\n## How It Works\n\nWhen you select a workspace (via `dual` or `dual launch`):\n\n1. **Clone** — Clones the repo into `{workspace_root}/{repo}/{branch}/` (uses `git clone --local` from main workspace for speed)\n2. **Shared files** — Copies shared config files (`.env.local`, `.vercel`, etc.) from `~/.dual/shared/{repo}/`\n3. **Container** — Creates and starts a Docker container with the clone bind-mounted\n4. **Setup** — Runs `setup` command on first launch (e.g., `pnpm install`)\n5. **Shell RC** — Generates transparent command routing that intercepts runtime commands and routes them to the container via `docker exec`\n6. **Tmux** — Creates a tmux session in the workspace directory and attaches\n\nYour editor, git, and credentials stay on the host. The container handles all runtime processes. Claude Code never knows it's running inside a container.\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\nTerminal\n├── State A: Dual TUI (ratatui)\n│   └── Select workspace → suspend TUI → launch pipeline → tmux attach\n└── State B: tmux session\n    └── Detach (Ctrl+b d) → resume TUI\n\nHost                          Container\n+--------------------------+  +--------------------------+\n| nvim, git, claude, ssh   |  | pnpm, node, python       |\n| file reads/writes        |  | curl localhost, tests    |\n| credentials, SSH keys    |  | port-binding processes   |\n+--------------------------+  +--------------------------+\n        |    bind mount    |\n        +------------------+\n\nBrowser --> {repo}-{branch}.localhost:{port}\n        --> reverse proxy\n        --> container\n```\n\n| Host | Container |\n|------|-----------|\n| git, cat, ls, vim | npm, pnpm, node, python |\n| File reads/writes | Port-binding processes |\n| SSH, credentials | curl localhost, tests |\n\n## Development\n\n```bash\ncargo build              # Build debug binary\ncargo build --release    # Build release binary\ncargo test               # Run tests (~150 tests)\ncargo clippy             # Run linter\ncargo fmt                # Format code\n```\n\nTargets: Linux, macOS (Intel + Apple Silicon), Windows.\n\n## License\n\nMIT\n"
}
```
